                     options = ["필터","경고만"], group = grp_zz_fusion)

// [ZZ_FUSION_END]
// [MARKET_OPT_START] – 시장별 최적화(실험)

// [MARKET_OPT_END]
// [EV_FILTER_START] – Posterior / EV 옵션
grp_ev = "═══ Posterior / EV 필터 ═══"
use_ev_filter = input.bool(false, "EV 필터로 LONG/SHORT source 품질 강화",                 group = grp_ev)

float ev_rr_real = 1.8

// [PATCH] 추가 EV 옵션
use_dyn_rr     = input.bool(false, "EV에 BW기반 동적 RR 사용",                group = grp_ev)

//────────────────────────────────────
// 06. 유틸 함수
//────────────────────────────────────
sum_float(src, len) =>
    float acc = 0.0
    for i = 0 to len - 1
        acc += nz(src[i])
    acc
set_cell_c(tbl, c, r, txt, tcol, bgcol) =>
    table.cell(tbl, c, r, txt, text_color = tcol, bgcolor = bgcol,
               text_size = size.normal, text_halign = text.align_center)
set_cell_l(tbl, c, r, txt, tcol, bgcol) =>
    table.cell(tbl, c, r, txt, text_color = tcol, bgcolor = bgcol,
               text_size = size.normal, text_halign = text.align_left)
// [PATCH] 확률 결합용 함수 (log-odds)
f_logit(float p) =>
    math.log(p / (1.0 - p))
f_sigmoid(float x) =>
    1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-x))
f_clamp(float v, float vMin, float vMax) =>
    math.max(math.min(v, vMax), vMin)
f_clamp01(float v) =>
    f_clamp(v, 0.0, 1.0)
f_alert_qty_long_short_eff() =>
    math.max(math.min(alert_qty_long_short, 1.0), 0.01)
f_diag_b_post_pair_min() =>
    math.max(diag_b_post_long_min, diag_b_post_short_min)
f_strategy_id_effective() =>
    string strategy_id_override_trimmed = str.trim(strategy_id_override)
    strategy_id_override_trimmed != "" ? strategy_id_override_trimmed : STRATEGY_ID
f_strategy_id_source() =>
    str.trim(strategy_id_override) != "" ? "OVERRIDE" : "DEFAULT"
f_febt_mode_runtime(string _mode) =>
    string modeU = str.upper(str.trim(_mode))
    modeU == "OFF" ? "OFF" : "SHADOW"
f_febt_same_dir_streak(bool _dir_long) =>
    int streak = 0
    for i = 0 to 9
        if na(close[i]) or na(close[i + 1])
            break
        bool same_dir = _dir_long ? (close[i] >= close[i + 1]) : (close[i] <= close[i + 1])
        if same_dir
            streak += 1
        else
            break
    streak
f_febt_phase(bool _state_valid, float _lock_score, float _edge, float _late_risk, float _failure_risk) =>
    if not _state_valid
        "VOID"
    else if _failure_risk > febt_fail_max
        "VOID"
    else if _late_risk >= febt_late_hard_max
        "LATE"
    else if _lock_score < febt_lock_arm_min
        "PREPARE"
    else if _lock_score >= febt_lock_fire_min and _edge >= febt_fire_edge_min
        "FIRE"
    else
        "ARMED"
f_febt_timing_action(string _mode_runtime, string _phase, bool _calc_ok) =>
    string modeU = str.upper(str.trim(_mode_runtime))
    string phaseU = str.upper(str.trim(_phase))
    if not _calc_ok or modeU == "OFF"
        "NO_OP"
    else if modeU == "SHADOW"
        "OBSERVE"
    else if modeU == "SOFT"
        phaseU == "FIRE" ? "PASS" : phaseU == "ARMED" ? "DEFER_HINT" : phaseU == "LATE" ? "LATE_WARN" : phaseU == "VOID" ? "BLOCK_CANDIDATE" : "NO_OP"
    else
        phaseU == "FIRE" ? "PASS" : phaseU == "ARMED" ? "DEFER_HINT" : phaseU == "VOID" or phaseU == "LATE" ? "BLOCK_CANDIDATE" : "NO_OP"
f_febt_authority(string _mode_runtime) =>
    string modeU = str.upper(str.trim(_mode_runtime))
    modeU == "HARD" ? "WAIT_PRIMARY" : modeU == "SOFT" ? "TIMING_ADVISORY" : "SHADOW_ONLY"
f_febt_pick_float(int _sig_dir, float _long_v, float _short_v) =>
    _sig_dir > 0 ? _long_v : _sig_dir < 0 ? _short_v : na
f_febt_pick_int(int _sig_dir, int _long_v, int _short_v) =>
    _sig_dir > 0 ? _long_v : _sig_dir < 0 ? _short_v : na
f_febt_pick_string(int _sig_dir, string _long_v, string _short_v) =>
    _sig_dir > 0 ? _long_v : _sig_dir < 0 ? _short_v : "UNKNOWN"

// 품질게이트 단계 상수
int STAGE_EARLY = 0
int STAGE_CORE = 1
int STAGE_DIAG_B = 2
int STAGE_REAL = 3

// 품질게이트 비트마스크 상수
int QMASK_EARLY_LONG = 1
int QMASK_EARLY_SHORT = 2
int QMASK_CORE_LONG = 4
int QMASK_CORE_SHORT = 8
int QMASK_DIAG_B_LONG = 16
int QMASK_DIAG_B_SHORT = 32
int QMASK_DIAG_C_LONG = 64
int QMASK_DIAG_C_SHORT = 128
int QMASK_ALL = 255

// 비코인 레거시 스냅샷 마스크(REAL은 16/32를 사용)
int NCMASK_EARLY_LONG = 1
int NCMASK_EARLY_SHORT = 2
int NCMASK_CORE_LONG = 4
int NCMASK_CORE_SHORT = 8
int NCMASK_DIAG_C_LONG = 16
int NCMASK_DIAG_C_SHORT = 32

f_stage_pick_float(int stage, float earlyV, float coreV, float preRealV, float realV) =>
    stage == STAGE_EARLY ? earlyV : stage == STAGE_CORE ? coreV : stage == STAGE_DIAG_B ? preRealV : realV
f_stage_pick_int(int stage, int earlyV, int coreV, int preRealV, int realV) =>
    stage == STAGE_EARLY ? earlyV : stage == STAGE_CORE ? coreV : stage == STAGE_DIAG_B ? preRealV : realV
f_stage_uses_core_ev(int stage) =>
    stage <= STAGE_CORE
f_quality_gate_one(
    int _stage, // 0:EARLY 1:CORE 2:DIAG_B 3:REAL
    bool _is_long,
    bool _strict,
    bool _noncoin_relax,
    bool _is_coin,
    bool _no_volume_asset,
    float _atr_ratio,
    float _vol_ratio,
    float _score,
    float _wave_conf,
    float _post_long,
    float _post_short,
    float _ev_core_long,
    float _ev_core_short,
    float _ev_diag_c_long,
    float _ev_diag_c_short,
    float _sp_entropy_score,
    float _sp_coherence_score,
    float _sp_transition_risk,
    float _sp_free_energy,
    bool _conflict_long,
    bool _conflict_short,
    bool _zz_conflict_long,
    bool _zz_conflict_short) =>
    float relax = (_noncoin_relax and not _is_coin) ? 0.80 : 1.00
    int rank_relax = (_noncoin_relax and not _is_coin) ? 8 : 0
    int rank_early = int(math.max(35, (_strict ? 52 : 45) - rank_relax))
    int rank_core  = int(math.max(40, (_strict ? 62 : 55) - rank_relax))
    int rank_pre   = int(math.max(45, (_strict ? 70 : 62) - rank_relax))
    int rank_real  = int(math.max(50, (_strict ? 78 : 70) - rank_relax))
    float post_spread_min = (_is_coin ? (_strict ? 0.12 : 0.08) : (_strict ? 0.08 : 0.05)) * relax
    float post_abs_early_min = (_strict ? 0.56 : 0.53) * relax
    float post_abs_core_min  = (_strict ? 0.61 : 0.57) * relax
    float post_abs_pre_min   = (_strict ? 0.65 : 0.60) * relax
    float post_abs_diag_c_min  = (_strict ? 0.68 : 0.63) * relax
    float wave_early_min = (_strict ? 0.58 : 0.53) * relax
    float wave_core_min  = (_strict ? 0.64 : 0.58) * relax
    float wave_pre_min   = (_strict ? 0.68 : 0.63) * relax
    float wave_diag_c_min  = (_strict ? 0.72 : 0.67) * relax
    float score_early_abs = (_strict ? 24.0 : 18.0) * relax
    float score_core_abs  = (_strict ? 34.0 : 28.0) * relax
    float score_pre_abs   = (_strict ? 40.0 : 34.0) * relax
    float score_diag_c_abs  = (_strict ? 46.0 : 40.0) * relax
    float conf_early_min = (_strict ? 0.55 : 0.50) * relax
    float conf_core_min  = (_strict ? 0.62 : 0.57) * relax
    float conf_pre_min   = (_strict ? 0.66 : 0.61) * relax
    float conf_diag_c_min  = (_strict ? 0.70 : 0.65) * relax
    float entropy_early_max = _strict ? 0.82 : 0.86
    float entropy_core_max  = _strict ? 0.76 : 0.82
    float entropy_pre_max   = _strict ? 0.72 : 0.78
    float entropy_diag_c_max  = _strict ? 0.68 : 0.74
    float coherence_early_min = (_strict ? 0.34 : 0.28) * relax
    float coherence_core_min  = (_strict ? 0.42 : 0.36) * relax
    float coherence_pre_min   = (_strict ? 0.48 : 0.42) * relax
    float coherence_diag_c_min  = (_strict ? 0.54 : 0.48) * relax
    float transition_early_max = _strict ? 0.80 : 0.84
    float transition_core_max  = _strict ? 0.74 : 0.80
    float transition_pre_max   = _strict ? 0.70 : 0.76
    float transition_diag_c_max  = _strict ? 0.66 : 0.72
    float ev_early_min = _strict ? 0.02 : -0.02
    float ev_core_min  = _strict ? 0.08 : 0.02
    float ev_pre_min   = _strict ? 0.12 : 0.05
    float ev_diag_c_min  = _strict ? 0.16 : 0.08
    float atr_cap = _is_coin ? (_strict ? 2.2 : 2.8) : (_strict ? 2.8 : 3.4)
    if _noncoin_relax and not _is_coin
        atr_cap := atr_cap + 0.5
    float vol_min = _strict ? 1.0 : 0.85
    if _noncoin_relax and not _is_coin
        vol_min := math.max(0.70, vol_min - 0.15)
    bool bar_noise_ok = _atr_ratio <= atr_cap
    bool vol_ok = _no_volume_asset or _vol_ratio >= vol_min
    bool post_ok = _is_long ? ((_post_long - _post_short) >= post_spread_min) : ((_post_short - _post_long) >= post_spread_min)
    bool conflict_ok = _is_long ? (not _conflict_long and not _zz_conflict_long) : (not _conflict_short and not _zz_conflict_short)
    float score_ok_thr = f_stage_pick_float(_stage, score_early_abs, score_core_abs, score_pre_abs, score_diag_c_abs)
    bool score_ok = _is_long ? (_score >= score_ok_thr) : (_score <= -score_ok_thr)
    float wave_ok_thr = f_stage_pick_float(_stage, wave_early_min, wave_core_min, wave_pre_min, wave_diag_c_min)
    bool wave_ok = _wave_conf >= wave_ok_thr
    float ev_val = f_stage_uses_core_ev(_stage) ? (_is_long ? _ev_core_long : _ev_core_short) : (_is_long ? _ev_diag_c_long : _ev_diag_c_short)
    float ev_ok_thr = f_stage_pick_float(_stage, ev_early_min, ev_core_min, ev_pre_min, ev_diag_c_min)
    bool ev_ok = ev_val >= ev_ok_thr
    float post_abs_thr = f_stage_pick_float(_stage, post_abs_early_min, post_abs_core_min, post_abs_pre_min, post_abs_diag_c_min)
    bool post_abs_ok = _is_long ? (_post_long >= post_abs_thr) : (_post_short >= post_abs_thr)
    float score_conf = f_clamp01(math.abs(_score) / 100.0)
    float side_post = _is_long ? _post_long : _post_short
    float posterior_conf = f_clamp01(math.abs(side_post - 0.5) * 2.0)
    float ev_conf = f_clamp01((ev_val - ev_ok_thr) / ev_conf_span)
    float conf_local = f_clamp01(0.4 * score_conf + 0.4 * posterior_conf + 0.2 * ev_conf)
    float conf_thr = f_stage_pick_float(_stage, conf_early_min, conf_core_min, conf_pre_min, conf_diag_c_min)
    bool conf_ok = conf_local >= conf_thr
    float entropy_thr = f_stage_pick_float(_stage, entropy_early_max, entropy_core_max, entropy_pre_max, entropy_diag_c_max)
    float coherence_thr = f_stage_pick_float(_stage, coherence_early_min, coherence_core_min, coherence_pre_min, coherence_diag_c_min)
    float transition_thr = f_stage_pick_float(_stage, transition_early_max, transition_core_max, transition_pre_max, transition_diag_c_max)
    float free_energy_early_max = _strict ? 0.78 : 0.84
    float free_energy_core_max  = _strict ? 0.70 : 0.78
    float free_energy_pre_max   = _strict ? 0.64 : 0.72
    float free_energy_diag_c_max  = _strict ? 0.58 : 0.66
    float free_energy_thr = f_stage_pick_float(_stage, free_energy_early_max, free_energy_core_max, free_energy_pre_max, free_energy_diag_c_max)
    float statphys_margin = _sp_coherence_score - _sp_transition_risk
    float statphys_margin_early_min = _strict ? -0.08 : -0.12
    float statphys_margin_core_min  = _strict ? -0.02 : -0.08
    float statphys_margin_pre_min   = _strict ? 0.02 : -0.02
    float statphys_margin_diag_c_min  = _strict ? 0.06 : 0.02
    float statphys_margin_thr = f_stage_pick_float(_stage, statphys_margin_early_min, statphys_margin_core_min, statphys_margin_pre_min, statphys_margin_diag_c_min)
    bool entropy_ok = _sp_entropy_score <= entropy_thr
    bool coherence_ok = _sp_coherence_score >= coherence_thr
    bool transition_ok = _sp_transition_risk <= transition_thr
    bool free_energy_ok = _sp_free_energy <= free_energy_thr
    bool statphys_balance_ok = statphys_margin >= statphys_margin_thr
    int dyn_rank_thr = f_stage_pick_int(_stage, rank_early, rank_core, rank_pre, rank_real)
    int dyn_len = math.max(60, quality_dynamic_len)
    int dyn_warmup = math.max(60, math.min(dyn_len, quality_dynamic_warmup))
    float score_abs_rank = ta.percentrank(math.abs(_score), dyn_len)
    float wave_conf_rank = ta.percentrank(_wave_conf, dyn_len)
    float post_spread_rank = ta.percentrank(math.abs(_post_long - _post_short), dyn_len)
    float ev_core_long_rank = ta.percentrank(_ev_core_long, dyn_len)
    float ev_core_short_rank = ta.percentrank(_ev_core_short, dyn_len)
    float ev_diag_c_long_rank = ta.percentrank(_ev_diag_c_long, dyn_len)
    float ev_diag_c_short_rank = ta.percentrank(_ev_diag_c_short, dyn_len)
    bool dyn_data_ready = bar_index >= dyn_warmup and not na(score_abs_rank) and not na(wave_conf_rank) and not na(post_spread_rank) and not na(ev_core_long_rank) and not na(ev_core_short_rank) and not na(ev_diag_c_long_rank) and not na(ev_diag_c_short_rank)
    float dyn_ev_rank = f_stage_uses_core_ev(_stage) ? (_is_long ? ev_core_long_rank : ev_core_short_rank) : (_is_long ? ev_diag_c_long_rank : ev_diag_c_short_rank)
    bool dyn_gate_on = quality_dynamic_enable and dyn_data_ready
    bool dyn_ok = not dyn_gate_on or (score_abs_rank >= dyn_rank_thr and wave_conf_rank >= dyn_rank_thr and post_spread_rank >= dyn_rank_thr and dyn_ev_rank >= dyn_rank_thr)
    bar_noise_ok and vol_ok and post_ok and post_abs_ok and score_ok and wave_ok and ev_ok and conf_ok and entropy_ok and coherence_ok and transition_ok and free_energy_ok and statphys_balance_ok and dyn_ok and conflict_ok
f_quality_gate_mask(
    bool _strict,
    bool _noncoin_relax,
    bool _is_coin,
    bool _no_volume_asset,
    float _atr_ratio,
    float _vol_ratio,
    float _score,
    float _wave_conf,
    float _post_long,
    float _post_short,
    float _ev_core_long,
    float _ev_core_short,
    float _ev_diag_c_long,
    float _ev_diag_c_short,
    float _sp_entropy_score,
    float _sp_coherence_score,
    float _sp_transition_risk,
    float _sp_free_energy,
    bool _conflict_long,
    bool _conflict_short,
    bool _zz_conflict_long,
    bool _zz_conflict_short) =>
    int mask = 0
    bool q_early_long  = f_quality_gate_one(STAGE_EARLY, true,  _strict, _noncoin_relax, _is_coin, _no_volume_asset, _atr_ratio, _vol_ratio, _score, _wave_conf, _post_long, _post_short, _ev_core_long, _ev_core_short, _ev_diag_c_long, _ev_diag_c_short, _sp_entropy_score, _sp_coherence_score, _sp_transition_risk, _sp_free_energy, _conflict_long, _conflict_short, _zz_conflict_long, _zz_conflict_short)
    bool q_early_short = f_quality_gate_one(STAGE_EARLY, false, _strict, _noncoin_relax, _is_coin, _no_volume_asset, _atr_ratio, _vol_ratio, _score, _wave_conf, _post_long, _post_short, _ev_core_long, _ev_core_short, _ev_diag_c_long, _ev_diag_c_short, _sp_entropy_score, _sp_coherence_score, _sp_transition_risk, _sp_free_energy, _conflict_long, _conflict_short, _zz_conflict_long, _zz_conflict_short)
    bool q_core_long   = f_quality_gate_one(STAGE_CORE, true,  _strict, _noncoin_relax, _is_coin, _no_volume_asset, _atr_ratio, _vol_ratio, _score, _wave_conf, _post_long, _post_short, _ev_core_long, _ev_core_short, _ev_diag_c_long, _ev_diag_c_short, _sp_entropy_score, _sp_coherence_score, _sp_transition_risk, _sp_free_energy, _conflict_long, _conflict_short, _zz_conflict_long, _zz_conflict_short)
    bool q_core_short  = f_quality_gate_one(STAGE_CORE, false, _strict, _noncoin_relax, _is_coin, _no_volume_asset, _atr_ratio, _vol_ratio, _score, _wave_conf, _post_long, _post_short, _ev_core_long, _ev_core_short, _ev_diag_c_long, _ev_diag_c_short, _sp_entropy_score, _sp_coherence_score, _sp_transition_risk, _sp_free_energy, _conflict_long, _conflict_short, _zz_conflict_long, _zz_conflict_short)
    bool q_pre_long    = f_quality_gate_one(STAGE_DIAG_B, true,  _strict, _noncoin_relax, _is_coin, _no_volume_asset, _atr_ratio, _vol_ratio, _score, _wave_conf, _post_long, _post_short, _ev_core_long, _ev_core_short, _ev_diag_c_long, _ev_diag_c_short, _sp_entropy_score, _sp_coherence_score, _sp_transition_risk, _sp_free_energy, _conflict_long, _conflict_short, _zz_conflict_long, _zz_conflict_short)
    bool q_pre_short   = f_quality_gate_one(STAGE_DIAG_B, false, _strict, _noncoin_relax, _is_coin, _no_volume_asset, _atr_ratio, _vol_ratio, _score, _wave_conf, _post_long, _post_short, _ev_core_long, _ev_core_short, _ev_diag_c_long, _ev_diag_c_short, _sp_entropy_score, _sp_coherence_score, _sp_transition_risk, _sp_free_energy, _conflict_long, _conflict_short, _zz_conflict_long, _zz_conflict_short)
    bool q_diag_c_long   = f_quality_gate_one(STAGE_REAL, true,  _strict, _noncoin_relax, _is_coin, _no_volume_asset, _atr_ratio, _vol_ratio, _score, _wave_conf, _post_long, _post_short, _ev_core_long, _ev_core_short, _ev_diag_c_long, _ev_diag_c_short, _sp_entropy_score, _sp_coherence_score, _sp_transition_risk, _sp_free_energy, _conflict_long, _conflict_short, _zz_conflict_long, _zz_conflict_short)
    bool q_diag_c_short  = f_quality_gate_one(STAGE_REAL, false, _strict, _noncoin_relax, _is_coin, _no_volume_asset, _atr_ratio, _vol_ratio, _score, _wave_conf, _post_long, _post_short, _ev_core_long, _ev_core_short, _ev_diag_c_long, _ev_diag_c_short, _sp_entropy_score, _sp_coherence_score, _sp_transition_risk, _sp_free_energy, _conflict_long, _conflict_short, _zz_conflict_long, _zz_conflict_short)
    if q_early_long
        mask += QMASK_EARLY_LONG
    if q_early_short
        mask += QMASK_EARLY_SHORT
    if q_core_long
        mask += QMASK_CORE_LONG
    if q_core_short
        mask += QMASK_CORE_SHORT
    if q_pre_long
        mask += QMASK_DIAG_B_LONG
    if q_pre_short
        mask += QMASK_DIAG_B_SHORT
    if q_diag_c_long
        mask += QMASK_DIAG_C_LONG
    if q_diag_c_short
        mask += QMASK_DIAG_C_SHORT
    mask
f_quality_mask_has(int _mask, int _bit) =>
    int(math.floor(_mask / _bit)) % 2 == 1
f_stage1_bundle_is_early_event(string _ev) =>
    string evU = str.upper(_ev)
    evU == "EARLY_LONG" or evU == "EARLY_SHORT" or evU == "LONG" or evU == "SHORT"
f_stage1_bundle_bit_for_event(string _ev, string _sd) =>
    string evU = str.upper(_ev)
    bool isLong = str.upper(_sd) == "BUY"
    (evU == "LONG" or evU == "EARLY_LONG") and isLong ? QMASK_EARLY_LONG :
     (evU == "SHORT" or evU == "EARLY_SHORT") and not isLong ? QMASK_EARLY_SHORT :
     evU == "CORE_LONG" and isLong ? QMASK_CORE_LONG :
     evU == "CORE_SHORT" and not isLong ? QMASK_CORE_SHORT :
     evU == "DIAG_B_LONG" and isLong ? QMASK_DIAG_B_LONG :
     evU == "DIAG_B_SHORT" and not isLong ? QMASK_DIAG_B_SHORT :
     evU == "DIAG_C_LONG" and isLong ? QMASK_DIAG_C_LONG :
     evU == "DIAG_C_SHORT" and not isLong ? QMASK_DIAG_C_SHORT :
     0
f_stage1_bundle_pass_for_event(string _ev, string _sd, int _mask) =>
    int _bit = f_stage1_bundle_bit_for_event(_ev, _sd)
    bool _stageAllowed = not f_stage1_bundle_is_early_event(_ev) or pine_stage1_bundle_apply_early
    _bit == 0 or not _stageAllowed ? false : f_quality_mask_has(_mask, _bit)
f_live_entry_bundle_event(string _ev, string _sd, string _entryGrade) =>
    string evU = str.upper(_ev)
    string gradeU = str.upper(_entryGrade)
    bool isLong = str.upper(_sd) == "BUY"
    if evU == "LONG" or evU == "SHORT"
        gradeU == "CORE" ? (isLong ? "CORE_LONG" : "CORE_SHORT") : (isLong ? "EARLY_LONG" : "EARLY_SHORT")
    else
        evU
// [ICH_UI_PLUS] 🔔 Bell 라벨 큐 (라벨 한도 초과 방지)
var label[] ichi_bell_labels = array.new_label()
f_ichi_bell_push(label lb) =>
    array.push(ichi_bell_labels, lb)
    if array.size(ichi_bell_labels) > ichi_bell_keep
        label.delete(array.shift(ichi_bell_labels))
// [ZZ_FUSION_START] – Wave State Vars
var float zz_bull_prob       = 0.5
var float zz_bear_prob       = 0.5
var bool  zz_main_bull       = true
var float zz_wave_conf       = 0.5
var int   zz_wave_dir        = 0
// Posterior / EV 확장 상태값
var float zz_post_prob_long  = 0.5
var float zz_post_prob_short = 0.5
var float zz_ev_core_long    = 0.0
var float zz_ev_core_short   = 0.0
var float zz_ev_diag_c_long    = 0.0
var float zz_ev_diag_c_short   = 0.0
// [ZZ_FUSION_END]

//────────────────────────────────────
// 07. 심볼/자산 분류 + TF 데이터
//────────────────────────────────────
sym_id    = syminfo.tickerid
sym_ticker = syminfo.ticker
// [PATCH-67] active runtime is Binance-only by design.
// dormant noncoin / UPBIT / KIWOOM execution paths are intentionally removed from live logic.
is_upbit  = false
is_binance= str.startswith(sym_id, "BINANCE:")
is_binance_fut = true
is_coin   = true
is_stock  = false
float zz_min_prob_long_eff = zz_min_prob_long
float zz_min_prob_short_eff = zz_min_prob_short
float rr_cap_eff = rr_cap
float bw_penalty_mid_eff = BW_PENALTY_MID
float bw_penalty_lower_eff = BW_PENALTY_LOWER
bool  bw_penalty_to_engine_eff = bw_penalty_to_engine
zz_min_prob_long_eff := math.max(zz_min_prob_long_eff, 0.65)
zz_min_prob_short_eff := math.max(zz_min_prob_short_eff, 0.67)
bw_penalty_to_engine_eff := true
bw_penalty_mid_eff := 0.55
bw_penalty_lower_eff := 0.35
rr_cap_eff := math.min(rr_cap_eff, 2.5)
bool enable_kimchi_eff = false
bool bf_enable_eff     = bf_enable
string asset_mode_txt = "Binance 선물"
heart_k_limit   = heart_k_limit_c
htf_tf          = htf_tf_coin
show_ichi_cloud = show_ichi_coin
tf_sec = timeframe.in_seconds(timeframe.period)
is_3m  = tf_sec == 60 * 3
is_5m  = tf_sec == 60 * 5
is_15m = tf_sec == 60 * 15
is_30m = tf_sec == 60 * 30
is_1h  = tf_sec == 60 * 60
is_4h  = tf_sec == 60 * 60 * 4
is_1d  = tf_sec == 60 * 60 * 24
is_1w  = tf_sec == 60 * 60 * 24 * 7
bool is_bnf_intraday = is_15m or is_1h
float volume_sma_20 = ta.sma(volume, 20)
bool is_stock_4h = false
// TF별 표시 제어
bool tf_show_diag_c_ls   = show_diag_c_ls
bool tf_show_td9       = show_td9
bool tf_show_ss_long   = show_ss_long
bool tf_show_ss_short  = show_ss_short
bool tf_show_vol_emoji = show_vol_emoji
bool tf_show_heart_vis = show_heart_vis
bool tf_show_status    = show_status_panel
bool tf_show_td9p      = enable_td9prime
// 볼륨 이모지 쿨다운 TF별
int vol_gap = gap_vol_min
if is_4h
    vol_gap := math.max(gap_vol_min, 5)
else if is_1h
    vol_gap := math.max(gap_vol_min, 10)

//────────────────────────────────────
// 8) Binance Futures 환경 필터 (BTC/XRP)
//────────────────────────────────────
string sym_upper = str.upper(sym_ticker)
bool is_btc_fut  = is_binance_fut and str.contains(sym_upper, "BTC") and str.contains(sym_upper, "USDT")
bool is_xrp_fut  = is_binance_fut and str.contains(sym_upper, "XRP") and str.contains(sym_upper, "USDT")
bool is_btc_sym  = str.contains(sym_upper, "BTC")
bool is_eth_sym  = str.contains(sym_upper, "ETH")
bool is_major_coin = is_coin and (is_btc_sym or is_eth_sym)
bool is_alt_coin   = is_coin and not is_major_coin
// legacy alias kept only to avoid compile breaks in dormant paths.
// active Binance-only logic should prefer is_major_coin / is_alt_coin / sym_ticker.
bool is_major = is_major_coin
bool is_alt = is_alt_coin
string symbol_only = sym_ticker
bool bf_exec_tf_ok = not bf_exec_tf_only or (is_3m or is_5m or is_15m or is_1h)
// 심볼 입력이 비어있거나 OFF/NONE/- 이면 비활성 처리
f_bf_has_symbol(string s) =>
    string t = str.upper(str.trim(s))
    not (t == "" or t == "OFF" or t == "NONE" or t == "-")
// 1D/4H 방향 필터 (현재 심볼 기준)
float bf_d_close = na
float bf_h4_close = na
if bf_enable_eff
    bf_d_close := request.security(syminfo.tickerid, bf_dir_1d_tf, close, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
    bf_h4_close := request.security(syminfo.tickerid, bf_dir_4h_tf, close, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
float bf_d_close_src = nz(bf_d_close, nz(bf_d_close[1], close))
float bf_h4_close_src = nz(bf_h4_close, nz(bf_h4_close[1], close))
float bf_d_ema = ta.ema(bf_d_close_src, bf_dir_ma_len)
float bf_h4_ema = ta.ema(bf_h4_close_src, bf_dir_ma_len)
bool bf_d_bull = bf_enable_eff and bf_d_close > bf_d_ema
bool bf_d_bear = bf_enable_eff and bf_d_close < bf_d_ema
bool bf_h4_bull = bf_enable_eff and bf_h4_close > bf_h4_ema
bool bf_h4_bear = bf_enable_eff and bf_h4_close < bf_h4_ema
bool bf_dir_long_ok = not bf_enable_eff or (na(bf_d_close) or na(bf_h4_close)) or (bf_d_bull and not bf_h4_bull)
bool bf_dir_short_ok = not bf_enable_eff or (na(bf_d_close) or na(bf_h4_close)) or (bf_d_bear and not bf_h4_bear)
// OI 필터 (가격↑ + OI↓ / 가격↓ + OI↓ 추격 금지)
string bf_oi_sym = is_xrp_fut ? bf_oi_xrp_symbol : bf_oi_btc_symbol
bool bf_use_oi_eff = bf_enable_eff and bf_use_oi and f_bf_has_symbol(bf_oi_sym)
float bf_oi_val = na
if bf_use_oi_eff
    bf_oi_val := request.security(bf_oi_sym, timeframe.period, close, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off, ignore_invalid_symbol = true)
float bf_oi_avg = ta.sma(bf_oi_val, 3)
float bf_oi_delta = bf_oi_avg - bf_oi_avg[1]
bool bf_oi_down = bf_oi_avg < bf_oi_avg[1] and bf_oi_avg[1] < bf_oi_avg[2]
bool bf_oi_trap_long = close > close[1] and bf_oi_down
bool bf_oi_trap_short = close < close[1] and bf_oi_down
bool bf_oi_ok_long = not bf_use_oi_eff or na(bf_oi_val) or not bf_oi_trap_long
bool bf_oi_ok_short = not bf_use_oi_eff or na(bf_oi_val) or not bf_oi_trap_short
// Funding 필터 (한쪽 과열 제한)
string bf_fund_sym = is_xrp_fut ? bf_funding_xrp_symbol : bf_funding_btc_symbol
bool bf_use_funding_eff = bf_enable_eff and bf_use_funding and f_bf_has_symbol(bf_fund_sym)
float bf_funding = na
bool bf_funding_ok_long = true
bool bf_funding_ok_short = true
if bf_use_funding_eff
    bf_funding := request.security(bf_fund_sym, timeframe.period, close, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off, ignore_invalid_symbol = true)
    bf_funding_ok_long := na(bf_funding) or bf_funding <= bf_funding_pos_thr
    bf_funding_ok_short := na(bf_funding) or bf_funding >= -bf_funding_neg_thr
// 추가 선물 최적화(과열/청산 시그널)
float bf_funding_delta = bf_funding - bf_funding[1]
float bf_oi_delta_pct = (not na(bf_oi_avg) and bf_oi_avg[1] != 0) ? (bf_oi_delta / bf_oi_avg[1]) : na
bool overheated_long = not na(bf_funding_delta) and not na(bf_oi_delta_pct) and bf_funding_delta > 0.005 and bf_oi_delta_pct > 0.05
bool liquidation_cascade = not na(bf_funding_delta) and not na(bf_oi_delta_pct) and bf_funding_delta < -0.01 and bf_oi_delta_pct < -0.10
// 가격 액션 기반 주문서 프록시 + 트랩 감지
float upper_wick = high - math.max(open, close)
float lower_wick = math.min(open, close) - low
float body_size  = math.abs(close - open)
float wick_den   = lower_wick + upper_wick + body_size + 0.0001
float buy_pressure  = lower_wick / wick_den
float sell_pressure = upper_wick / wick_den
float avg_buy_pressure = ta.sma(buy_pressure, 3)
bool  order_book_bullish = avg_buy_pressure > 0.5
bool sudden_spike = close > close[1] * 1.03 and (volume_sma_20 > 0 ? volume > volume_sma_20 * 2 : false)
bool immediate_reversal = close < open and close < close[1] * 0.98
var bool trap_alert = false
if sudden_spike
    trap_alert := true
if trap_alert and immediate_reversal
    trap_alert := false
// XRP 환경: BTC 추세/변동성/BTC.D
bool bf_use_btc_env_eff = bf_enable_eff and bf_use_btc_env_for_xrp and f_bf_has_symbol(bf_btc_symbol)
float btc_d_close = na
float btc_h4_close = na
if bf_use_btc_env_eff
    btc_d_close := request.security(bf_btc_symbol, bf_dir_1d_tf, close, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off, ignore_invalid_symbol = true)
    btc_h4_close := request.security(bf_btc_symbol, bf_dir_4h_tf, close, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off, ignore_invalid_symbol = true)
float btc_d_close_src = nz(btc_d_close, nz(btc_d_close[1], close))
float btc_h4_close_src = nz(btc_h4_close, nz(btc_h4_close[1], close))
float btc_d_ema = ta.ema(btc_d_close_src, bf_dir_ma_len)
float btc_h4_ema = ta.ema(btc_h4_close_src, bf_dir_ma_len)
bool btc_d_bull = bf_use_btc_env_eff and btc_d_close > btc_d_ema
bool btc_d_bear = bf_use_btc_env_eff and btc_d_close < btc_d_ema
bool btc_h4_bull = bf_use_btc_env_eff and btc_h4_close > btc_h4_ema
bool btc_h4_bear = bf_use_btc_env_eff and btc_h4_close < btc_h4_ema
bool btc_strong_bull = btc_d_bull and btc_h4_bull
bool btc_strong_bear = btc_d_bear and btc_h4_bear
float btc_vol_high = na
float btc_vol_low = na
float btc_vol_close = na
if bf_use_btc_env_eff
    btc_vol_high := request.security(bf_btc_symbol, bf_btc_vol_tf, high, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off, ignore_invalid_symbol = true)
    btc_vol_low := request.security(bf_btc_symbol, bf_btc_vol_tf, low, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off, ignore_invalid_symbol = true)
    btc_vol_close := request.security(bf_btc_symbol, bf_btc_vol_tf, close, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off, ignore_invalid_symbol = true)
float btc_vol_high_src = nz(btc_vol_high, high)
float btc_vol_low_src = nz(btc_vol_low, low)
float btc_vol_close_src = nz(btc_vol_close, nz(btc_vol_close[1], close))
float btc_vol_prev_close = nz(btc_vol_close_src[1], btc_vol_close_src)
float btc_vol_tr = math.max(btc_vol_high_src - btc_vol_low_src, math.max(math.abs(btc_vol_high_src - btc_vol_prev_close), math.abs(btc_vol_low_src - btc_vol_prev_close)))
float btc_atr_raw = ta.rma(btc_vol_tr, 14)
float btc_atr = bf_use_btc_env_eff ? btc_atr_raw : na
float btc_atr_ma = ta.sma(btc_atr, 20)
bool  btc_vol_spike = (not na(btc_atr) and not na(btc_atr_ma) and btc_atr_ma > 0) and (btc_atr / btc_atr_ma >= bf_btc_vol_spike)
bool  btc_vol_spike_eff = bf_use_btc_env_eff and btc_vol_spike
bool bf_use_btc_d_eff = bf_use_btc_env_eff and f_bf_has_symbol(bf_btc_d_symbol)
float btc_dom_close = na
if bf_use_btc_d_eff
    btc_dom_close := request.security(bf_btc_d_symbol, bf_btc_d_tf, close, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off, ignore_invalid_symbol = true)
float btc_dom_close_src = nz(btc_dom_close, nz(btc_dom_close[1], close))
float btc_dom_ma = ta.sma(btc_dom_close_src, bf_btc_d_ma_len)
bool  btc_dom_up    = bf_use_btc_d_eff and not na(btc_dom_close) and btc_dom_close > btc_dom_ma
bool  btc_dom_down  = bf_use_btc_d_eff and not na(btc_dom_close) and btc_dom_close < btc_dom_ma
bool  btc_dom_up_eff   = bf_use_btc_d_eff and btc_dom_up
bool  btc_dom_down_eff = bf_use_btc_d_eff and btc_dom_down
bool xrp_env_long_ok = not bf_use_btc_env_eff or not is_xrp_fut or (
     not btc_vol_spike_eff and not btc_strong_bear and not btc_dom_up_eff
)
bool xrp_env_short_ok = not bf_use_btc_env_eff or not is_xrp_fut or (
     not btc_vol_spike_eff and not btc_strong_bull and not btc_dom_down_eff
)
bool bf_long_ok  = not bf_enable_eff or (bf_exec_tf_ok and bf_dir_long_ok  and bf_oi_ok_long  and bf_funding_ok_long  and xrp_env_long_ok)
bool bf_short_ok = not bf_enable_eff or (bf_exec_tf_ok and bf_dir_short_ok and bf_oi_ok_short and bf_funding_ok_short and xrp_env_short_ok)
bool bf_ls_long_ok  = bf_ls_mode != "숏만"
bool bf_ls_short_ok = bf_ls_mode != "롱만"
bf_long_ok  := bf_long_ok and (not bf_enable_eff or bf_ls_long_ok)
bf_short_ok := bf_short_ok and (not bf_enable_eff or bf_ls_short_ok)

//────────────────────────────────────
// 7-1) 글로벌 세션
//────────────────────────────────────
bool is_krx    = str.startswith(sym_id, "KRX:") or str.startswith(sym_id, "KOSPI:") or str.startswith(sym_id, "KOSDAQ:")
bool is_nyse   = str.startswith(sym_id, "NYSE:") or str.startswith(sym_id, "AMEX:")
bool is_nasdaq = str.startswith(sym_id, "NASDAQ:")
bool is_sse    = str.startswith(sym_id, "SSE:") or str.startswith(sym_id, "SZSE:")
bool is_hkex   = str.startswith(sym_id, "HKEX:")
bool is_tse    = str.startswith(sym_id, "TSE:")
bool is_lse    = str.startswith(sym_id, "LSE:") or str.startswith(sym_id, "XETR:")
int current_hour = hour(time, "Asia/Seoul")
bool is_regular_session  = true
bool is_extended_session = false
if is_krx
    is_regular_session := current_hour >= 9 and current_hour < 16
else if is_nyse or is_nasdaq
    int est_hour = hour(time, "America/New_York")
    is_regular_session := est_hour >= 9 and est_hour < 16
else if is_sse
    int cst_hour = hour(time, "Asia/Shanghai")
    is_regular_session := cst_hour >= 9 and cst_hour < 15
else if is_hkex
    int hkt_hour = hour(time, "Asia/Hong_Kong")
    is_regular_session := hkt_hour >= 9 and hkt_hour < 16
else if is_tse
    int jst_hour = hour(time, "Asia/Tokyo")
    is_regular_session := jst_hour >= 9 and jst_hour < 15
else if is_lse
    int lon_hour = hour(time, "Europe/London")
    is_regular_session := lon_hour >= 8 and lon_hour < 17
else if is_coin
    is_regular_session := true
is_extended_session := not is_regular_session and not is_coin
// 시장별 모드 강제 (세션/필터 분리)
bool stock_session_ok = not is_stock or is_regular_session
bool stock_long_only  = is_stock
bool allow_short_entries = is_binance_fut or (syminfo.type == "index") or (syminfo.type == "futures")
// 비코인 정규장 바 카운트 (마감 스파이크 완화용)
var int regular_session_bar = 0
var int regular_session_len = na
if not is_coin
    if is_regular_session
        regular_session_bar := is_regular_session[1] ? regular_session_bar[1] + 1 : 1
    else
        regular_session_bar := 0
    if not is_regular_session and is_regular_session[1]
        regular_session_len := regular_session_bar[1]
// 시장군 유도 변수
bool is_us_stock = is_nyse or is_nasdaq
bool is_kr_stock = is_krx
bool is_jp_stock = is_tse
bool is_cn_stock = is_sse
bool is_hk_stock = is_hkex
bool is_eu_stock = is_lse

//────────────────────────────────────
// 7-2) ETF 분류 (v4.7 스타일)
//────────────────────────────────────
string _u47   = str.upper(syminfo.ticker)
string _tid47 = str.upper(syminfo.tickerid)
bool   _is_krx47 = str.startswith(_tid47, "KRX:")
bool _kr_etf_brand47 =
     _is_krx47 and (
         str.startswith(_u47, "TIGER")  or str.startswith(_u47, "KODEX") or
         str.startswith(_u47, "KBSTAR") or str.startswith(_u47, "KOSEF") or
         str.startswith(_u47, "HANARO") or str.startswith(_u47, "SOL")   or
         str.startswith(_u47, "ARIRANG"))
bool _name_looks_etf47 =
     str.contains(_u47, "ETF") or str.contains(_u47, "ETN") or str.contains(_u47, "FUND")
bool is_etf = (syminfo.type == "fund") or _kr_etf_brand47 or _name_looks_etf47
bool is_leveraged_etf =
     is_etf and (
         str.contains(_u47, "2X")   or str.contains(_u47, "3X")   or
         str.contains(_u47, "BULL") or str.contains(_u47, "LEV")  or
         str.contains(_u47, "_L")   or str.contains(_u47, "L_")   or
         str.contains(_u47, "TQQQ") or str.contains(_u47, "SOXL") or
         str.contains(_u47, "UPRO"))
bool is_inverse_etf =
     is_etf and (
         str.contains(_u47, "INVERSE") or str.contains(_u47, "SHORT") or
         str.contains(_u47, "BEAR")    or str.contains(_u47, "DOWN")  or
         str.contains(_u47, "_D")      or str.contains(_u47, "D_")    or
         str.contains(_u47, "SQQQ")    or str.contains(_u47, "SOXS")  or
         str.contains(_u47, "SPXU"))
// ETF는 주식 long-only 강제 대상에서 제외한다.
stock_long_only := is_stock and not is_etf

//────────────────────────────────────
// 08. AMC 프리셋 (AUTO)  [v5.0]
//────────────────────────────────────
// - 코인(메이저): 15m / 1H / 4H / 1D 운용, 15m는 보수적 단기 운용
// - 코인(알트)  : 4H / 1D 중심 운용, 1H는 보수적으로만, 15m는 슈퍼보수
//   → 아래 amc_preset은 이런 기본 철학을 반영한 "위험 허용도 프리셋"입니다.
f_normalize_coin_amc_override(string _mode) =>
    _mode == "일반" ? "중립" : _mode

string amc_preset = preset_mode
if preset_mode == "AUTO"
    // 09. 코인 (공통 베이스 프리셋: v4.8 유지)
    if is_coin
        if is_15m
            amc_preset := "보수"
        else if is_30m or is_1h
            amc_preset := "슈퍼보수"
        else if is_4h
            amc_preset := "중립"
        else if is_1d or is_1w
            amc_preset := "공격"
        else
            amc_preset := "보수"
    // 10. 비코인 (주식/지수/기타) – v4.8 베이스 유지
    else
        if is_us_stock
            if is_15m or is_30m
                amc_preset := "보수"
            else if is_1h or is_4h
                amc_preset := "중립"
            else if is_1d
                amc_preset := "중립+"
            else if is_1w
                amc_preset := "공격"
            else
                amc_preset := "중립"
        else if is_kr_stock
            if is_15m or is_30m
                amc_preset := "보수"
            else if is_1h or is_4h
                amc_preset := "중립"
            else if is_1d
                amc_preset := "중립+"
            else if is_1w
                amc_preset := "공격"
            else
                amc_preset := "중립"
        else if is_cn_stock or is_hk_stock
            if is_15m or is_30m
                amc_preset := "슈퍼보수"
            else if is_1h or is_4h
                amc_preset := "슈퍼보수"
            else if is_1d
                amc_preset := "보수"
            else if is_1w
                amc_preset := "중립"
            else
                amc_preset := "보수"
        else
            if is_15m or is_30m
                amc_preset := "슈퍼보수"
            else if is_1h or is_4h
                amc_preset := "보수"
            else if is_1d
                amc_preset := "중립"
            else if is_1w
                amc_preset := "공격"
            else
                amc_preset := "중립"
    // 11. [v5.0] 알트코인 전용: 동일 TF에서 메이저 대비 최소 한 단계 더 보수적으로
    //    - AUTO + 코인 + 알트 조건에서만 적용
    if is_coin and is_alt
        if amc_preset == "맥스"
            amc_preset := "공격"
        else if amc_preset == "공격"
            amc_preset := "중립+"
        else if amc_preset == "중립+"
            amc_preset := "중립"
        else if amc_preset == "중립"
            amc_preset := "보수"
        else if amc_preset == "보수"
            amc_preset := "슈퍼보수"
        // 나머지(이미 슈퍼보수 등)는 그대로 둔다
    // 12. ETF 오버라이드 (비코인 ETF 전용)
    if not is_coin and is_etf
        if is_leveraged_etf
            if amc_preset == "공격" or amc_preset == "맥스"
                amc_preset := "중립+"
            else if amc_preset == "중립+" or amc_preset == "중립"
                amc_preset := "보수"
        else if is_inverse_etf
            if amc_preset == "공격"
                amc_preset := "중립+"
            else if amc_preset == "맥스"
                amc_preset := "공격"
if is_coin and is_major_coin and major_amc_mode != "AUTO"
    amc_preset := f_normalize_coin_amc_override(major_amc_mode)
if is_coin and is_alt_coin and alt_amc_mode != "AUTO"
    amc_preset := f_normalize_coin_amc_override(alt_amc_mode)
string amc_display = amc_preset
if is_coin and is_major_coin and major_amc_mode != "AUTO"
    amc_display := amc_preset + " (메이저 수동)"
else if is_coin and is_alt_coin and alt_amc_mode != "AUTO"
    amc_display := amc_preset + " (알트 수동)"

//────────────────────────────────────
// 8-1) 프리셋별 쿨다운
//────────────────────────────────────
int gap_diag_c_min = gap_diag_c_base
int gap_core_min = gap_core_base
if amc_preset == "슈퍼보수"
    gap_diag_c_min := 15
    gap_core_min := 10
else if amc_preset == "보수"
    gap_diag_c_min := 12
    gap_core_min := 8
else if amc_preset == "중립" or amc_preset == "중립+"
    gap_diag_c_min := 10
    gap_core_min := 7
else if amc_preset == "공격"
    gap_diag_c_min := 8
    gap_core_min := 5
else if amc_preset == "맥스"
    gap_diag_c_min := 6
    gap_core_min := 4

//────────────────────────────────────
// 09. 트렌드 / HTF / Stoch / TD9
//────────────────────────────────────
trend_src  = trend_basis == "고저종/3" ? (high + low + close) / 3.0 : close
trend_fast = ta.ema(trend_src, trend_len_fast)
trend_slow = ta.ema(trend_src, trend_len_slow)
trend_bull = trend_fast > trend_slow
trend_bear = trend_fast < trend_slow
up_score   = sum_float(trend_bull ? 1.0 : 0.0, trend_strong_bars)
down_score = sum_float(trend_bear ? 1.0 : 0.0, trend_strong_bars)
string trend_state = "neutral"
if up_score >= trend_strong_bars * trend_confirm_ema_ratio
    trend_state := "bull"
else if down_score >= trend_strong_bars * trend_confirm_ema_ratio
    trend_state := "bear"
// 15m 구조 확인(60m 신호용 이중 확증 게이트)
string ltf_tf = "15"
float ltf_fast = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, ltf_tf, ta.ema(trend_src, trend_len_fast), gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
float ltf_slow = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, ltf_tf, ta.ema(trend_src, trend_len_slow), gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
bool  ltf_ready = not na(ltf_fast) and not na(ltf_slow)
bool  ltf_bull  = ltf_ready and ltf_fast > ltf_slow
bool  ltf_bear  = ltf_ready and ltf_fast < ltf_slow
bool  ltf_gate_apply = ltf_confirm_enable and is_1h
bool  ltf_gate_long  = not ltf_gate_apply or ltf_bull
bool  ltf_gate_short = not ltf_gate_apply or ltf_bear
[htf_h, htf_l, htf_c] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, htf_tf,
                                         [high, low, close], gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
htf_rsi = ta.rsi(htf_c, htf_len)
string htf_state = "neutral"
if htf_rsi >= htf_rsi_bull_min
    htf_state := "bull"
else if htf_rsi <= htf_rsi_bear_max
    htf_state := "bear"
// STOCH
k_raw = ta.stoch(high, low, close, stoch_k_len)
k_val = ta.sma(k_raw, stoch_smooth)
d_val = ta.sma(k_val, stoch_d_len)
cross_kd_up   = ta.crossover(k_val, d_val)
cross_kd_down = ta.crossunder(k_val, d_val)
// TD9
td_up   = close > close[4]
td_down = close < close[4]
int td_buy  = 0
int td_sell = 0
td_buy  := td_up   ? nz(td_buy[1])  + 1 : 0
td_sell := td_down ? nz(td_sell[1]) + 1 : 0
string td_state = "none"
if td_buy > 0 and td_sell == 0
    td_state := "buy"
else if td_sell > 0 and td_buy == 0
    td_state := "sell"
bool td_exhaustion_up   = td_buy  >= td_lookback
bool td_exhaustion_down = td_sell >= td_lookback
// TD 쿨다운
var int td_exh_long_bar  = na
var int td_exh_short_bar = na
int td_cool_bars = 2
if td_exhaustion_up
    td_exh_long_bar := bar_index
if td_exhaustion_down
    td_exh_short_bar := bar_index
bool td_cool_long  = not na(td_exh_long_bar)  and bar_index - td_exh_long_bar  <= td_cool_bars
bool td_cool_short = not na(td_exh_short_bar) and bar_index - td_exh_short_bar <= td_cool_bars

//────────────────────────────────────
// 10. Ichimoku
//────────────────────────────────────
conversion_line = (ta.highest(high, conv_len) + ta.lowest(low, conv_len)) / 2.0
base_line       = (ta.highest(high, base_len) + ta.lowest(low, base_len)) / 2.0
span_a_src      = (conversion_line + base_line) / 2.0
span_b_src      = (ta.highest(high, span_b_len) + ta.lowest(low, span_b_len)) / 2.0
span_a          = span_a_src
span_b          = span_b_src

//────────────────────────────────────
// 11. Breakwater (ATR 방파제) + 밴드 초기값
//────────────────────────────────────
basis_src = (high + low + close) / 3.0
bw_mid    = ta.ema(basis_src, bw_atr_len)
atr_val   = ta.atr(bw_atr_len)
bw0_upper = bw_mid + atr_val * bw_low_mult
bw0_lower = bw_mid - atr_val * bw_low_mult
bw1_upper = bw_mid + atr_val * bw_high_mult
bw1_lower = bw_mid - atr_val * bw_high_mult
bw2_upper = bw_mid + atr_val * bw_high_mult * bw_dbl_mult
bw2_lower = bw_mid - atr_val * bw_high_mult * bw_dbl_mult
bool in_support_band = false
bool in_resist_band  = false

//────────────────────────────────────
// 12. 볼륨 임계값 프리셋 조정 + SS 튜닝
//────────────────────────────────────
float vol_ultra_thr  = vol_ultra_mult
float vol_strong_thr = vol_strong_mult
float vol_weak_thr   = vol_weak_mult
vol_ultra_thr := vol_ultra_thr * vol_conservative_mult
vol_strong_thr := vol_strong_thr * vol_conservative_mult
if amc_preset == "슈퍼보수"
    vol_ultra_thr  := vol_ultra_thr * 1.3
    vol_strong_thr := vol_strong_thr * 1.15
    vol_weak_thr   := vol_weak_thr * 0.8
else if amc_preset == "보수"
    vol_ultra_thr  := vol_ultra_thr * 1.15
    vol_strong_thr := vol_strong_thr * 1.05
    vol_weak_thr   := vol_weak_thr * 0.85
else if amc_preset == "중립+"
    vol_ultra_thr  := vol_ultra_thr * 0.95
    vol_strong_thr := vol_strong_thr * 0.95
    vol_weak_thr   := vol_weak_thr * 0.95
else if amc_preset == "공격"
    vol_ultra_thr  := vol_ultra_thr * 0.85
    vol_strong_thr := vol_strong_thr * 0.85
else if amc_preset == "맥스"
    vol_ultra_thr  := vol_ultra_thr * 0.75
    vol_strong_thr := vol_strong_thr * 0.8
    vol_weak_thr   := vol_weak_thr * 1.1
if is_coin
    vol_ultra_thr := vol_ultra_thr * 1.05
else
    vol_ultra_thr  := vol_ultra_thr * 0.95
    vol_strong_thr := vol_strong_thr * 1.10
float ss_vol_mult = ss_vol_mult_base
if is_upbit
    if is_major
        vol_ultra_thr  := vol_ultra_thr * 0.92
        vol_strong_thr := vol_strong_thr * 0.92
        ss_vol_mult    := ss_vol_mult * 0.9
    else
        vol_ultra_thr  := vol_ultra_thr * 1.6
        vol_strong_thr := vol_strong_thr * 1.25
        ss_vol_mult    := ss_vol_mult * 1.8
if is_upbit and is_alt and (is_15m or is_30m or is_1h)
    ss_vol_mult := ss_vol_mult * 0.75

//────────────────────────────────────
// 13. 볼륨 엔진 + 세션 튜닝
//────────────────────────────────────
vol_ma    = ta.sma(volume, vol_len)
vol_ratio = vol_ma > 0 ? volume / vol_ma : 1.0
if not is_coin
    bool in_closing_range = false
    if is_regular_session and not na(regular_session_len) and regular_session_len > 0
        in_closing_range := regular_session_len - regular_session_bar < 3
    float vol_clamp = 1.3
    if in_closing_range
        vol_ratio := math.min(vol_ratio, vol_clamp)
vol_ultra  = vol_ratio >= vol_ultra_thr
vol_strong = not vol_ultra and vol_ratio >= vol_strong_thr
vol_weak   = vol_ratio <= vol_weak_thr
bool no_volume_asset = (vol_ma == 0.0) or (volume == 0.0)
if not is_coin and is_extended_session
    vol_ultra  := false
    vol_strong := false
    vol_weak   := false

//────────────────────────────────────
// 14. ADX Regime
//────────────────────────────────────
upMove   = high - high[1]
downMove = low[1] - low
plusDM   = (upMove > 0 and upMove > downMove) ? upMove  : 0.0
minusDM  = (downMove > 0 and downMove > upMove) ? downMove : 0.0
tr1 = high - low
tr2 = math.abs(high - close[1])
tr3 = math.abs(low  - close[1])
tr  = math.max(tr1, math.max(tr2, tr3))
pDM_ema = ta.rma(plusDM,  adx_len)
mDM_ema = ta.rma(minusDM, adx_len)
tr_ema  = ta.rma(tr,      adx_len)
pDI = tr_ema == 0 ? 0.0 : (pDM_ema / tr_ema) * 100.0
mDI = tr_ema == 0 ? 0.0 : (mDM_ema / tr_ema) * 100.0
dx      = (pDI + mDI == 0) ? 0.0 : (math.abs(pDI - mDI) / (pDI + mDI)) * 100.0
adx_val = ta.rma(dx, adx_len)
string regime_state = "transition"
if adx_val < adx_side_thr
    regime_state := "range"
else if adx_val > adx_trend_thr
    regime_state := "trend"
bool regime_trend = regime_state == "trend"
bool regime_soft  = not regime_trend
// [PATCH-40] Choppiness Index 빠른 레짐 감지
float ci_atr_sum = ta.sma(ta.atr(1), ci_len) * ci_len
float ci_range = ta.highest(high, ci_len) - ta.lowest(low, ci_len)
float ci_raw = ci_range > 0 ? 100.0 * math.log10(ci_atr_sum / ci_range) / math.log10(ci_len) : 50.0
string regime_fast = "transition"
if regime_fast_enable
    if ci_raw < ci_trend_thr
        regime_fast := "trend"
    else if ci_raw > (100.0 - ci_trend_thr)
        regime_fast := "range"
string regime_for_core = regime_fast_enable ? regime_fast : regime_state

//────────────────────────────────────
// 15. Volatility Zone (ATR) + Hybrid Core 밴드 + Y-레이어
//────────────────────────────────────
float atr_current = ta.atr(vz_atr_len)
float atr_avg     = ta.sma(atr_current, vz_smooth_len)
float atr_ratio   = atr_avg > 0 ? atr_current / atr_avg : 1.0
string vz_state = "mid"
if atr_ratio > vz_high_thr
    vz_state := "high"
else if atr_ratio < vz_low_thr
    vz_state := "low"
// VZ BG 색상
color vz_bg = na
if vz_state == "high"
    vz_bg := color.new(color.orange, 92)
else if vz_state == "low"
    vz_bg := color.new(color.purple, 96)
// 볼륨 존 BG
color vol_bg = na
if vol_ultra
    vol_bg := color.new(color.orange, 92)
else if vol_strong
    vol_bg := color.new(color.teal, 94)
else if vol_weak
    vol_bg := color.new(color.purple, 96)
// Hybrid Core 밴드 폭 동적 선택 (BW1↔BW2)  [PATCH: 히스테리시스]
var bool use_wide_band = false
if not use_wide_band and (atr_ratio > wide_on_thr or regime_state == "trend")
    use_wide_band := true
if use_wide_band and (atr_ratio < wide_off_thr and regime_state != "trend")
    use_wide_band := false
if use_wide_band
    in_support_band := close > bw2_lower and close < bw0_lower
    in_resist_band  := close < bw2_upper and close > bw0_upper
else
    in_support_band := close > bw1_lower and close < bw0_lower
    in_resist_band  := close < bw1_upper and close > bw0_upper
// Y축 라벨 스케일
float label_atr       = ta.atr(14)
float label_atr_avg   = ta.sma(label_atr, 50)
float label_atr_ratio = label_atr_avg > 0 ? label_atr / label_atr_avg : 1.0
float scale_factor    = label_atr_ratio > 1.5 ? 0.7 : label_atr_ratio < 0.7 ? 1.4 : 1.0
float y_diag_c_long   = low  - label_atr * 0.3 * scale_factor
float y_core_long   = low  - label_atr * 0.8 * scale_factor
float y_early_long  = low  - label_atr * 1.3 * scale_factor
float y_emo_long    = low  - label_atr * 1.8 * scale_factor
float y_vol_long    = low  - label_atr * 2.3 * scale_factor
float y_diag_c_short  = high + label_atr * 0.3 * scale_factor
float y_core_short  = high + label_atr * 0.8 * scale_factor
float y_early_short = high + label_atr * 1.3 * scale_factor
float y_emo_short   = high + label_atr * 1.8 * scale_factor
float y_vol_short   = high + label_atr * 2.3 * scale_factor
float y_td9_long    = low  - label_atr * 3.0 * scale_factor
float y_td9_short   = high + label_atr * 3.0 * scale_factor
float y_ss_long     = low  - label_atr * 3.4 * scale_factor
float y_ss_short    = high + label_atr * 3.4 * scale_factor
float y_fake        = high + label_atr * 3.8 * scale_factor

//────────────────────────────────────
// v4.7 시그널 플래그
//────────────────────────────────────
bool bull_trend = trend_state == "bull"
bool bear_trend = trend_state == "bear"
bool bull_htf   = htf_state   == "bull"
bool bear_htf   = htf_state   == "bear"
bool bull_td  = td_state == "buy"
bool bear_td  = td_state == "sell"
bool bull_stoch = k_val > d_val and k_val < 80
bool bear_stoch = k_val < d_val and k_val > 20
bool vol_bull_bias = close >= bw_mid or bull_trend
bool vol_bear_bias = close <= bw_mid or bear_trend
bool bull_vol   = (vol_ultra or vol_strong) and vol_bull_bias
bool bear_vol   = (vol_ultra or vol_strong) and vol_bear_bias
bool bull_regime= regime_state == "trend" and pDI >= mDI
bool bear_regime= regime_state == "trend" and mDI >= pDI

//────────────────────────────────────
// 13-1. 시장별 최적화용 보조 지표(전역 계산)
//────────────────────────────────────
float vol_high5 = ta.highest(volume, 5)
float vol_rsi   = ta.rsi(volume, 14)
float vol_slope = volume - volume[1]
float candle_range = high - low
float body_pct = candle_range != 0 ? math.abs(close - open) / candle_range : 0.0
bool  genuine_volume = body_pct > 0.4
bool  vol_ultra_v2 = vol_ratio >= vol_ultra_thr and vol_rsi > 60 and vol_slope > 0 and volume >= vol_high5 and genuine_volume
float vol_percentile = ta.percentrank(atr_val, opt_vol_rank_len)
float volume_ma_50 = ta.sma(volume, 50)
float volume_stdev_20 = ta.stdev(volume, 20)
float volume_stability = volume_ma_50 > 0 ? volume_stdev_20 / volume_ma_50 : na
// 코인(알트) 상관/환경 필터용
float btc_close_tf = request.security("BINANCE:BTCUSDT", timeframe.period, close, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
float btc_ret = (not na(btc_close_tf) and btc_close_tf[1] != 0) ? (btc_close_tf - btc_close_tf[1]) / btc_close_tf[1] : na
float alt_ret = close[1] != 0 ? (close - close[1]) / close[1] : na
float btc_alt_corr_raw = ta.correlation(btc_ret, alt_ret, 20)
float btc_alt_corr = (not na(btc_ret) and not na(alt_ret)) ? btc_alt_corr_raw : na
bool healthy_correlation = (not na(btc_ret) and not na(alt_ret) and not na(btc_alt_corr)) ? (btc_ret > 0 and alt_ret > 0 and btc_alt_corr > 0.5) : true
bool suspicious_pump = (not na(btc_ret) and not na(alt_ret)) ? (btc_ret < -0.02 and alt_ret > 0.03) : false
float typical_price = (high + low + close) / 3
float money_flow = typical_price * volume
float money_flow_sma20 = ta.sma(money_flow, 20)
float mf_ratio = money_flow_sma20 > 0 ? money_flow / money_flow_sma20 : 1.0
float kospi_close = na
float kospi_ret = na
float stock_ret = na
float rs_ratio_raw = na
float rs_ratio = na
bool sector_strength = true
float trade_value = close * volume
float trade_value_ma5 = ta.sma(trade_value, 5)
bool kr_stock_gate = true
bool gap_up = false
bool volume_spike = volume_sma_20 > 0 ? volume > volume_sma_20 * 3 : false
int est_hour = 0
bool market_open = false
bool market_close = false
float time_weight = 1.0
float vix = na
float vix_ma = na
bool healthy_market = true
bool panic_mode = false

//────────────────────────────────────
// 16. ADOL Proxy + Early 신호
//────────────────────────────────────
adol_hma      = ta.hma(close, adol_hma_len)
adol_mom_fast = ta.mom(adol_hma, adol_fast_len)
adol_mom_slow = ta.mom(adol_hma, adol_slow_len)
adol_mom_fast_slope = adol_mom_fast - adol_mom_fast[1]
adol_mom_slow_slope = adol_mom_slow - adol_mom_slow[1]
bool adol_up   = close > adol_hma and adol_hma > adol_hma[1] and adol_mom_fast > 0 and adol_mom_fast_slope > 0
bool adol_down = close < adol_hma and adol_hma < adol_hma[1] and adol_mom_fast < 0 and adol_mom_fast_slope < 0
// [PATCH-35] Hybrid Trend Probe: ADOL 기반 빠른 추세 감지
// [PATCH-45] 반등 필터 강화: ADOL 2봉 연속 + 모멘텀 slope 확인
bool trend_probe_bull = trend_probe_enable and close > adol_hma and adol_hma > nz(adol_hma[1]) and nz(adol_hma[1]) > nz(adol_hma[2]) and adol_mom_fast > 0 and adol_mom_fast_slope > 0
bool trend_probe_bear = trend_probe_enable and close < adol_hma and adol_hma < nz(adol_hma[1]) and nz(adol_hma[1]) < nz(adol_hma[2]) and adol_mom_fast < 0 and adol_mom_fast_slope < 0
if trend_probe_enable
    if trend_probe_bull and up_score >= trend_strong_bars * trend_probe_ema_ratio
        trend_state := "probe_bull"
    if trend_probe_bear and down_score >= trend_strong_bars * trend_probe_ema_ratio
        trend_state := "probe_bear"
bool trend_probe_or_bull = trend_state == "bull" or trend_state == "probe_bull"
bool trend_probe_or_bear = trend_state == "bear" or trend_state == "probe_bear"
bool early_long  = adol_up
bool early_short = adol_down

//────────────────────────────────────
// 17. REAL Score 계산
//────────────────────────────────────
// [PATCH-36] Gradient Score 연속 강도 함수
f_stoch_strength_long() =>
    float _base = f_clamp01((k_val - 50.0) / 50.0)
    float _spread = f_clamp01((k_val - d_val) / 20.0)
    0.6 * _base + 0.4 * _spread
f_stoch_strength_short() =>
    float _base = f_clamp01((50.0 - k_val) / 50.0)
    float _spread = f_clamp01((d_val - k_val) / 20.0)
    0.6 * _base + 0.4 * _spread
f_htf_strength_long() =>
    f_clamp01((htf_rsi - 50.0) / 30.0)
f_htf_strength_short() =>
    f_clamp01((50.0 - htf_rsi) / 30.0)
f_regime_strength() =>
    f_clamp01((adx_val - adx_side_thr) / math.max(adx_trend_thr - adx_side_thr, 1.0))
f_vol_strength_long() =>
    f_clamp01((vol_ratio - vol_weak_thr) / math.max(vol_ultra_thr - vol_weak_thr, 0.01))
f_vol_strength_short() =>
    // 숏은 약세 거래량이 강할수록 점수가 올라가야 한다.
    f_clamp01((vol_ratio - vol_weak_thr) / math.max(vol_ultra_thr - vol_weak_thr, 0.01))
f_td_strength_long() =>
    f_clamp01(td_buy / 9.0)
f_td_strength_short() =>
    f_clamp01(td_sell / 9.0)
f_trend_strength_long() =>
    float _sep = trend_fast > 0 ? (trend_fast - trend_slow) / trend_slow : 0.0
    float _consist = up_score / math.max(trend_strong_bars, 1)
    f_clamp01(0.5 * f_clamp01(_sep / 0.02) + 0.5 * _consist)
f_trend_strength_short() =>
    float _sep = trend_slow > 0 ? (trend_slow - trend_fast) / trend_slow : 0.0
    float _consist = down_score / math.max(trend_strong_bars, 1)
    f_clamp01(0.5 * f_clamp01(_sep / 0.02) + 0.5 * _consist)
raw_long  = 0.0
raw_short = 0.0
// [PATCH-36] Gradient vs Binary score
if use_gradient_score
    raw_long  := w_trend * f_trend_strength_long() + w_htf * f_htf_strength_long() + w_td * f_td_strength_long() + w_stoch * f_stoch_strength_long() + w_vol * f_vol_strength_long() + w_regime * f_regime_strength()
    raw_short := w_trend * f_trend_strength_short() + w_htf * f_htf_strength_short() + w_td * f_td_strength_short() + w_stoch * f_stoch_strength_short() + w_vol * f_vol_strength_short() + w_regime * f_regime_strength()
else
    raw_long  += bull_trend  ? w_trend  : 0.0
    raw_long  += bull_htf    ? w_htf    : 0.0
    raw_long  += bull_td     ? w_td     : 0.0
    raw_long  += bull_stoch  ? w_stoch  : 0.0
    raw_long  += bull_vol    ? w_vol    : 0.0
    raw_long  += bull_regime ? w_regime : 0.0
    raw_short += bear_trend  ? w_trend  : 0.0
    raw_short += bear_htf    ? w_htf    : 0.0
    raw_short += bear_td     ? w_td     : 0.0
    raw_short += bear_stoch  ? w_stoch  : 0.0
    raw_short += bear_vol    ? w_vol    : 0.0
    raw_short += bear_regime ? w_regime : 0.0
float total_weight   = w_trend + w_htf + w_td + w_stoch + w_vol + w_regime
float score_abs_long = total_weight > 0 ? (raw_long  / total_weight) * 100.0 : 0.0
float score_abs_short= total_weight > 0 ? (raw_short / total_weight) * 100.0 : 0.0
float score_raw      = score_abs_long - score_abs_short
float score_decision = score_raw
float vol_norm = bw_mid != 0.0 ? atr_val / math.abs(bw_mid) : 0.0
int kr_hour    = hour(time, "Asia/Seoul")
bool is_night  = kr_hour >= 18 or kr_hour < 9
if is_coin and is_night
    float night_scale = session_debuff
    if vol_norm > 0.05
        night_scale := session_debuff * 1.6
    else if vol_norm > 0.03
        night_scale := session_debuff * 1.0
    else
        night_scale := session_debuff * 0.4
    score_decision := score_raw * (1.0 - night_scale)
float score        = score_decision
float score_display= score_decision

//────────────────────────────────────
// 17-1. 시장별 최적화(점수 보정)
//────────────────────────────────────
bool opt_on = opt_enable
bool opt_major_on = opt_on and opt_coin_major and is_major_coin
bool opt_alt_on   = opt_on and opt_coin_alt and is_alt_coin
bool opt_kr_on    = opt_on and opt_kr_stock and is_kr_stock
bool opt_us_on    = opt_on and opt_us_stock and is_us_stock
bool major_regime_ok = vol_percentile > 80 ? (regime_state == "trend" or regime_state == "transition") : regime_state == "trend"
bool score_dir_long = score >= 0
bool major_td_dir_ok = score_dir_long ? (td_buy >= (vol_percentile > 80 ? 3 : 5)) : (td_sell >= (vol_percentile > 80 ? 3 : 5))
bool major_boost_ok = is_binance
     ? (opt_major_on and major_regime_ok and major_td_dir_ok and (score_dir_long ? bull_trend : bear_trend))
     : (opt_major_on and bull_trend and major_regime_ok and td_buy >= (vol_percentile > 80 ? 3 : 5))
bool htf_align_boost = (trend_state == htf_state) and (trend_state == "bull" or trend_state == "bear")
float score_boost = 1.0
if major_boost_ok
    score_boost := score_boost * 1.3
if is_binance
    if opt_alt_on and mf_ratio > 2.0 and (score_dir_long ? close > open : close < open)
        score_boost := score_boost * 1.2
else
    if opt_alt_on and mf_ratio > 2.0 and close > open
        score_boost := score_boost * 1.2
if is_binance
    if opt_kr_on and volume_spike and (score_dir_long ? gap_up : (close[1] != 0 ? (open < close[1] * 0.98) : false)) and (score_dir_long ? bull_trend : bear_trend)
        score_boost := score_boost * 1.5
else
    if opt_kr_on and gap_up and volume_spike and bull_trend
        score_boost := score_boost * 1.5
if opt_us_on
    score_boost := score_boost * time_weight
if is_binance
    if htf_align_boost and ((score_dir_long and trend_state == "bull") or (not score_dir_long and trend_state == "bear"))
        score_boost := score_boost * 1.05
else
    if htf_align_boost
        score_boost := score_boost * 1.05
score := score * score_boost
score_display := score_display * score_boost
bool bf_minor_trend_ok = not (bf_enable_eff and bf_minor_trend_only) or regime_state == "trend"
bool early_soft_long = close > adol_hma and adol_hma > adol_hma[1] and adol_mom_fast > 0 and score >= 20 and trend_state != "bear" and htf_state != "bear"
bool early_soft_short = close < adol_hma and adol_hma < adol_hma[1] and adol_mom_fast < 0 and score <= -20 and trend_state != "bull" and htf_state != "bull"
// 5.5.5.5 품질 유지: 소프트 EARLY 확장은 바이낸스 선물에서만 사용.
early_long  := is_binance_fut ? (adol_up or early_soft_long) : adol_up
early_short := is_binance_fut ? (adol_down or early_soft_short) : adol_down
bool bf_early_long_ok  = not bf_enable_eff or score >= bf_early_score_min
bool bf_early_short_ok = not bf_enable_eff or score <= -bf_early_score_min
if bf_enable_eff
    early_long  := early_long  and bf_minor_trend_ok and bf_early_long_ok
    early_short := early_short and bf_minor_trend_ok and bf_early_short_ok
// BW 기반 스코어 패널티 적용 함수 (양/음수 대칭)
f_apply_bw_penalty(float _score, float _close, float _mid, float _lower, float _upper) =>
    float result = _score
    if _score > 0
        if _close < _mid
            result := result * bw_penalty_mid_eff
        if _close < _lower
            result := result * bw_penalty_lower_eff
    else if _score < 0
        if _close > _mid
            result := result * bw_penalty_mid_eff
        if _close > _upper
            result := result * bw_penalty_lower_eff
    result
// 패널/엔진 스코어 모두 동일 로직으로 패널티 적용
score_display := f_apply_bw_penalty(score_display, close, bw_mid, bw1_lower, bw1_upper)
// [PATCH-12] 옵션: BW 패널티를 엔진 score에도 동일 적용
if bw_penalty_to_engine_eff
    score := f_apply_bw_penalty(score, close, bw_mid, bw1_lower, bw1_upper)
float score_panel = score_display
float score_long  = score_abs_long
float score_short = score_abs_short
score_prev = nz(score[1])
slope      = score - score_prev
// 점수 텍스트
// [PATCH-39] 모멘텀 정렬 시 충돌 해제
bool momentum_aligned_long = slope >= 0 and adol_mom_fast > 0
bool momentum_aligned_short = slope <= 0 and adol_mom_fast < 0
bool conflict_long  = score_panel > 0  and trend_state != htf_state and regime_state != "trend" and not (conflict_allow_momentum_override and momentum_aligned_long)
bool conflict_short = score_panel < 0  and trend_state != htf_state and regime_state != "trend" and not (conflict_allow_momentum_override and momentum_aligned_short)
// probe 추세 활성 시 충돌 해제
if trend_probe_enable and (trend_state == "probe_bull" or trend_state == "probe_bear")
    conflict_long  := false
    conflict_short := false
bool conflict       = conflict_long or conflict_short
string score_txt = ""
if conflict_long
    score_txt := "롱 편향(충돌)"
else if conflict_short
    score_txt := "숏 편향(충돌)"
else if score_panel >= 55
    score_txt := "강력 매수 존"
else if score_panel >= 15
    score_txt := "매수 우위"
else if score_panel > -15 and score_panel < 15
    score_txt := "중립"
else if score_panel <= -55
    score_txt := "강력 매도 존"
else if score_panel <= -15
    score_txt := "매도 우위"
else
    score_txt := "중립"
// [ZZ_FUSION_START] – ZigZag Wave Engine (경량)
// 1) 유틸 함수 (지역)
f_zz_pct_change(float fromP, float toP) =>
    fromP == 0.0 ? 0.0 : (toP - fromP) / fromP * 100.0
f_zz_clamp01(float v) =>
    f_clamp01(v)
// 2) 상태/배열
var int[]   zz_idx_arr   = array.new_int()
var float[] zz_price_arr = array.new_float()
var int[]   zz_dir_arr   = array.new_int()
var float[] zz_rsi_arr   = array.new_float()
// 임시 특징 값
var bool  zz_fractal3_detected = false
var float zz_fractal3_strength = 0.0
var bool  zz_rsi_div           = false
var float zz_rsi_div_strength  = 0.0
// Ω C파 근사
var float  zz_omega_c_prob      = 0.0
var bool   zz_omega_c_done      = false
var string zz_omega_next_dir    = "NONE"
var float  zz_omega_conf_local  = na
// 3) 파라미터
int   zz_left    = 5
int   zz_right   = 5
float zz_min_dev = 2.0          // 피봇 간 최소 변동폭(%)
int   zz_max_pts = 200
float zz_rsi_eps = 2.0          // RSI 다이버전스 민감도
// 4) Pivot 추출 (단순 ZigZag)
float zz_rsi_src = ta.rsi(close, 14)
float ph = ta.pivothigh(high, zz_left, zz_right)
float pl = ta.pivotlow(low,  zz_left, zz_right)
int   piv_dir   = 0
float piv_price = na
int   piv_index = na
if not na(ph)
    piv_dir   := 1
    piv_price := ph
    piv_index := bar_index - zz_right
else if not na(pl)
    piv_dir   := -1
    piv_price := pl
    piv_index := bar_index - zz_right
if piv_dir != 0 and piv_index >= 0
    int n = array.size(zz_price_arr)
    bool accept = true
    if n > 0
        float lastP = array.get(zz_price_arr, n - 1)
        int   lastD = array.get(zz_dir_arr,   n - 1)
        float dev   = math.abs(f_zz_pct_change(lastP, piv_price))
        // 같은 방향이면 기존 pivot 업데이트만
        if piv_dir == lastD
            if dev >= zz_min_dev
                array.set(zz_price_arr, n - 1, piv_price)
                array.set(zz_idx_arr,   n - 1, piv_index)
                array.set(zz_rsi_arr,   n - 1, zz_rsi_src)
            accept := false
        else
            accept := dev >= zz_min_dev
    if accept
        array.push(zz_idx_arr,   piv_index)
        array.push(zz_price_arr, piv_price)
        array.push(zz_dir_arr,   piv_dir)
        array.push(zz_rsi_arr,   zz_rsi_src)
        // 오래된 피봇 삭제
        int sz = array.size(zz_idx_arr)
        if sz > zz_max_pts
            int extra = sz - zz_max_pts
            for i = 0 to extra - 1
                array.shift(zz_idx_arr)
                array.shift(zz_price_arr)
                array.shift(zz_dir_arr)
                array.shift(zz_rsi_arr)
// 5) Fractal3 (연속 스윙 수) + 임펄스 강도
int pivCount = array.size(zz_idx_arr)
zz_fractal3_detected := false
zz_fractal3_strength := 0.0
bool zz_current_bull = true
if pivCount >= 3
    int lastDir = array.get(zz_dir_arr, pivCount - 1)
    zz_current_bull := lastDir == 1
    int sameDir = 1
    for k = 0 to pivCount - 2
        int j = pivCount - 2 - k
        if j < 0
            break
        if array.get(zz_dir_arr, j) == lastDir
            sameDir += 1
        else
            break
    zz_fractal3_detected := sameDir >= 3
    zz_fractal3_strength := f_zz_clamp01((sameDir - 2.0) / 3.0)
// 6) RSI 다이버전스 (마지막 두 피봇 기준 간단 버전)
bool bullDiv = false
bool bearDiv = false
if pivCount >= 4
    // 마지막 저점/고점과 그 이전 저점/고점 추출
    float lastLow     = na
    float prevLow     = na
    float lastLowRsi  = na
    float prevLowRsi  = na
    float lastHigh    = na
    float prevHigh    = na
    float lastHighRsi = na
    float prevHighRsi = na
    // 뒤에서부터 스캔
    int lowsFound  = 0
    int highsFound = 0
    for k = 0 to pivCount - 1
        int j = pivCount - 1 - k
        int d = array.get(zz_dir_arr, j)
        float p = array.get(zz_price_arr, j)
        float r = array.get(zz_rsi_arr,   j)
        if d == -1 and lowsFound < 2
            if lowsFound == 0
                lastLow    := p
                lastLowRsi := r
            else
                prevLow    := p
                prevLowRsi := r
            lowsFound += 1
        if d == 1 and highsFound < 2
            if highsFound == 0
                lastHigh    := p
                lastHighRsi := r
            else
                prevHigh    := p
                prevHighRsi := r
            highsFound += 1
    bullDiv :=
         not na(lastLow) and not na(prevLow) and
         not na(lastLowRsi) and not na(prevLowRsi) and
         lastLow < prevLow and lastLowRsi > prevLowRsi + zz_rsi_eps
    bearDiv :=
         not na(lastHigh) and not na(prevHigh) and
         not na(lastHighRsi) and not na(prevHighRsi) and
         lastHigh > prevHigh and lastHighRsi < prevHighRsi - zz_rsi_eps
zz_rsi_div_strength := (bullDiv or bearDiv) ? 1.0 : 0.0
zz_rsi_div          := bullDiv or bearDiv
// 7) Ω C파 근사 확률
float struct_base =
     0.6 * zz_fractal3_strength +
     0.4 * zz_rsi_div_strength
float regime_mult = 0.6
if regime_state == "trend"
    regime_mult := 1.0
else if regime_state == "transition"
    regime_mult := 0.8
zz_omega_c_prob := f_zz_clamp01(struct_base * regime_mult)
zz_omega_c_done := zz_omega_c_prob >= 0.55
// [PATCH v5.5.1] 방향: ZZ pivot dir 우선(불일치 완화) + score fallback
float score_norm = math.max(math.min(score / 100.0, 1.0), -1.0)
// 최근 ZigZag pivot 방향 힌트(+1 high, -1 low)
int zz_dir_hint = 0
if array.size(zz_dir_arr) > 0
    zz_dir_hint := array.get(zz_dir_arr, array.size(zz_dir_arr) - 1)
// pivot 힌트가 없으면 score 기반 fallback
if zz_dir_hint == 0
    zz_dir_hint := score_norm >= 0 ? 1 : -1
if zz_omega_c_done
    zz_omega_next_dir := zz_dir_hint >= 0 ? "UP" : "DOWN"
else
    zz_omega_next_dir := "NONE"
// 7-1) 신뢰도 업데이트 (지수평활, 삼항 제거 버전)
float momScore = f_zz_clamp01(0.5 + 0.5 * score_norm)
// confRaw 계산
float confRaw = 0.0
if zz_omega_next_dir != "NONE"
    confRaw := zz_omega_c_prob * 0.6 + struct_base * 0.4
else
    confRaw := zz_omega_c_prob * 0.5
float confNew = f_zz_clamp01((confRaw + momScore * 0.4) / 1.4)
// 직전 값 기반 지수 평활
if na(zz_omega_conf_local[1])
    zz_omega_conf_local := confNew
else
    zz_omega_conf_local := zz_omega_conf_local[1] * 0.7 + confNew * 0.3
// 8) 엔진(P_engine) + 구조(Q_struct) 결합 → bull/bear 확률
float bullBase = f_zz_clamp01((score_norm + 1.0) / 2.0)
float omega_up = zz_omega_next_dir == "UP"   ? nz(zz_omega_conf_local, 0.0) : 0.0
float omega_dn = zz_omega_next_dir == "DOWN" ? nz(zz_omega_conf_local, 0.0) : 0.0
float eng_up_raw = bullBase * 0.6 + omega_up * 0.4
float eng_dn_raw = (1.0 - bullBase) * 0.6 + omega_dn * 0.4
float eng_sum = eng_up_raw + eng_dn_raw
float p_engine_up   = 0.5
float p_engine_down = 0.5
if eng_sum > 0.0
    p_engine_up   := eng_up_raw / eng_sum
    p_engine_down := eng_dn_raw / eng_sum
// 구조 품질(Q_struct)
float w_f3    = 0.35
float w_omega = 0.45
float w_rsi   = 0.20
float q_struct_num =
      w_f3    * nz(zz_fractal3_strength, 0.0) +
      w_omega * nz(zz_omega_c_prob,      0.0) +
      w_rsi   * nz(zz_rsi_div_strength,  0.0)
float q_struct_den = w_f3 + w_omega + w_rsi
float Q_struct = 0.0
if q_struct_den > 0.0
    Q_struct := q_struct_num / q_struct_den
Q_struct := f_zz_clamp01(Q_struct)
float q_struct_up   = 0.5
float q_struct_down = 0.5
if Q_struct > 0.0 and zz_omega_next_dir != "NONE"
    if zz_omega_next_dir == "UP"
        q_struct_up   := 0.5 + 0.5 * Q_struct
        q_struct_down := 0.5 - 0.5 * Q_struct
    else
        q_struct_down := 0.5 + 0.5 * Q_struct
        q_struct_up   := 0.5 - 0.5 * Q_struct
float w_struct_min = 0.25
float w_struct_max = 0.75
float w_struct = w_struct_min + (w_struct_max - w_struct_min) * Q_struct
float w_engine = 1.0 - w_struct
float bullFinal_raw = w_struct * q_struct_up   + w_engine * p_engine_up
float bearFinal_raw = w_struct * q_struct_down + w_engine * p_engine_down
bullFinal_raw := f_zz_clamp01(bullFinal_raw)
bearFinal_raw := f_zz_clamp01(bearFinal_raw)
float sumFinal = bullFinal_raw + bearFinal_raw
float bullProb = 0.5
float bearProb = 0.5
if sumFinal > 0.0
    bullProb := bullFinal_raw / sumFinal
    bearProb := bearFinal_raw / sumFinal
// 8-1) 파동 위치 기반 가격 구간 (0~1, 최근 100바 기준)
float zz_loc = 0.5
float zz_loc_low  = ta.lowest(low, 100)
float zz_loc_high = ta.highest(high, 100)
if zz_loc_high != zz_loc_low
    zz_loc := f_zz_clamp01((close - zz_loc_low) / (zz_loc_high - zz_loc_low))
// 8-2) 바닥/고점 확률 (단순 근사)
float bottom_prob = 0.4 * bearProb + 0.3 * zz_fractal3_strength + 0.3 * (1.0 - zz_loc)
float top_prob    = 0.4 * bullProb + 0.3 * zz_rsi_div_strength  + 0.3 * zz_loc
// 9) 전역 Wave / Posterior / EV 출력 업데이트
zz_bull_prob := bullProb
zz_bear_prob := bearProb
// (1) Prior 계산 [PATCH-37] 독립 Prior 모드 지원
float prior_long = 0.5
if post_prior_mode == "SCORE_LEGACY"
    prior_long := 0.5 + 0.5 * score_norm
else if post_prior_mode == "HTF_STRUCT"
    float _htf_rsi_norm = f_clamp01((htf_rsi - 30.0) / 40.0)
    float _ichi_pos = close > math.max(span_a, span_b) ? 1.0 : close < math.min(span_a, span_b) ? 0.0 : 0.5
    prior_long := 0.4 * _htf_rsi_norm + 0.3 * _ichi_pos + 0.3 * 0.5
else if post_prior_mode == "REGIME"
    prior_long := regime_state == "trend" ? (trend_state == "bull" or trend_state == "probe_bull" ? 0.65 : trend_state == "bear" or trend_state == "probe_bear" ? 0.35 : 0.50) : 0.50 + 0.10 * score_norm
prior_long := math.max(math.min(prior_long, 1.0), 0.0)
float prior_short = 1.0 - prior_long
// (2) Wave 기반 Likelihood
float wave_long  = zz_bull_prob
float wave_short = zz_bear_prob
// (3) Wave 신뢰도 기반 결합계수 λ (0.05~0.95)  [PATCH: 0.5 근처 wave 영향 축소]
float wave_conf_local = math.max(wave_long, wave_short)
float conf = math.abs(wave_long - 0.5) * 2.0          // 0..1 (0.5면 0, 0/1이면 1)
float lambda_post = post_lambda_min + post_lambda_range * conf    // [PATCH-37]
lambda_post := math.max(math.min(lambda_post, 0.95), post_lambda_min)
// (4) Posterior (log-odds blend)  [PATCH]
float eps = 1e-6
float priorL = math.max(math.min(prior_long, 1.0 - eps), eps)
float waveL  = math.max(math.min(wave_long,  1.0 - eps), eps)
float logit_post = (1.0 - lambda_post) * f_logit(priorL) + lambda_post * f_logit(waveL)
float post_long  = f_sigmoid(logit_post)
float post_short = 1.0 - post_long
zz_post_prob_long  := post_long
zz_post_prob_short := post_short
// (5) EV 계산 (보상 = RR, 손실 = 1R 고정)  [PATCH: BW 기반 동적 RR 옵션]
float band_up = is_coin ? bw1_upper : (use_wide_band ? bw2_upper : bw1_upper)
float band_dn = is_coin ? bw1_lower : (use_wide_band ? bw2_lower : bw1_lower)
float riskL   = math.max(math.abs(close - band_dn), syminfo.mintick)
float rewardL = math.max(band_up - close, syminfo.mintick)
float rrL_dyn = math.min(rewardL / riskL, rr_cap_eff)
float riskS   = math.max(math.abs(band_up - close), syminfo.mintick)
float rewardS = math.max(close - band_dn, syminfo.mintick)
float rrS_dyn = math.min(rewardS / riskS, rr_cap_eff)
float core_rr_L = use_dyn_rr ? rrL_dyn : ev_rr_core
float core_rr_S = use_dyn_rr ? rrS_dyn : ev_rr_core
float diag_c_rr_L = use_dyn_rr ? rrL_dyn : ev_rr_real
float diag_c_rr_S = use_dyn_rr ? rrS_dyn : ev_rr_real
zz_ev_core_long  := post_long  * core_rr_L - (1.0 - post_long)
zz_ev_core_short := post_short * core_rr_S - (1.0 - post_short)
zz_ev_diag_c_long  := post_long  * diag_c_rr_L - (1.0 - post_long)
zz_ev_diag_c_short := post_short * diag_c_rr_S - (1.0 - post_short)
// (5-1) EV 임계값: 목표 승률 기반 역산 옵션(방향별)  [PATCH]
float ev_thr_core_L = ev_use_wr_target ? (ev_target_wr_core * (core_rr_L + 1.0) - 1.0) : ev_thr_core
float ev_thr_core_S = ev_use_wr_target ? (ev_target_wr_core * (core_rr_S + 1.0) - 1.0) : ev_thr_core
float ev_thr_diag_c_L = ev_use_wr_target ? (ev_target_wr_real * (diag_c_rr_L + 1.0) - 1.0) : ev_thr_real
float ev_thr_diag_c_S = ev_use_wr_target ? (ev_target_wr_real * (diag_c_rr_S + 1.0) - 1.0) : ev_thr_real
// (6) Wave 메인 방향: Posterior + Ω C파 우선
bool wave_bull_bias = zz_post_prob_long >= 0.5
// Ω C파 신뢰도가 충분하면 Ω 방향 우선
if not na(zz_omega_conf_local) and zz_omega_conf_local >= 0.55 and zz_omega_next_dir != "NONE"
    wave_bull_bias := zz_omega_next_dir == "UP"
else
    // Posterior가 0.45~0.55 구간(중립)일 때만 순수 Wave 확률로 보정
    if zz_post_prob_long > 0.45 and zz_post_prob_long < 0.55
        if zz_bull_prob > zz_bear_prob
            wave_bull_bias := true
        else if zz_bull_prob < zz_bear_prob
            wave_bull_bias := false
zz_main_bull := wave_bull_bias
zz_wave_conf := wave_conf_local
zz_wave_dir  := zz_main_bull ? 1 : -1
// [ZZ_FUSION_END]

//────────────────────────────────────
// 18. CORE / REAL 조건  [v5.0]
//────────────────────────────────────
// ※ 중요: 이 스크립트에서 "SHORT" 계열 신호(REAL SHORT / CORE SHORT / SS Short / TD Sell 등)는
//    기본적으로 "기존 롱 포지션의 청산·비중 축소·헤지 타이밍"을 의미합니다.
//    단, Binance 선물(롱/숏) 모드에서는 숏 진입 신호로 사용됩니다.
// 19. TD 최소 카운트
int td_buy_min  = td_lookback
int td_sell_min = td_lookback
if is_coin
    if is_15m or is_30m
        td_buy_min  := 4
        td_sell_min := 4
    else if is_1h
        td_buy_min  := 4
        td_sell_min := 4
    else if is_4h
        td_buy_min  := 5
        td_sell_min := 5
else
    if is_us_stock
        if is_15m
            td_buy_min  := math.min(td_lookback, 4)
            td_sell_min := math.min(td_lookback, 4)
        else if is_1h
            td_buy_min  := math.min(td_lookback, 5)
            td_sell_min := math.min(td_lookback, 5)
        else if is_4h
            td_buy_min  := math.min(td_lookback, 6)
            td_sell_min := math.min(td_lookback, 6)
        else if is_1d
            td_buy_min  := math.min(td_lookback, 7)
            td_sell_min := math.min(td_lookback, 7)
    else if is_kr_stock
        if is_15m
            td_buy_min  := math.min(td_lookback, 4)
            td_sell_min := math.min(td_lookback, 4)
        else if is_1h
            td_buy_min  := math.min(td_lookback, 6)
            td_sell_min := math.min(td_lookback, 6)
        else if is_4h
            td_buy_min  := math.min(td_lookback, 7)
            td_sell_min := math.min(td_lookback, 7)
        else if is_1d
            td_buy_min  := math.min(td_lookback, 6)
            td_sell_min := math.min(td_lookback, 6)
// 공통 플래그
bool td_ok_long  = td_buy  >= td_buy_min
bool td_ok_short = td_sell >= td_sell_min
bool vol_ok_long  = bull_vol
bool vol_ok_short = bear_vol
// 시장별 최적화: 강한 거래량 구간에서 LONG 신호 보정
if opt_enable and (opt_coin_major or opt_coin_alt) and (is_major_coin or is_alt_coin) and vol_ultra_v2
    vol_ok_long := true
if is_1h or is_15m
    vol_ok_long  := vol_strong or volume > vol_ma
    vol_ok_short := vol_strong or volume > vol_ma
if no_volume_asset
    vol_ok_long  := true
    vol_ok_short := true
bool regime_ok_trend = regime_state == "trend"
bool regime_ok_core  = regime_state == "trend" or regime_state == "transition"
bool regime_ok_range = regime_state == "range"
bool base_core_long  = score > 0 and bull_trend and (htf_state != "bear")
bool base_core_short = score < 0 and bear_trend and (htf_state != "bull")
if regime_ok_range
    if htf_state == "bull" and score >= 25
        base_core_long := true
    if htf_state == "bear" and score <= -25
        base_core_short := true
bool stoch_loose_long  = k_val >= d_val or cross_kd_up
bool stoch_loose_short = k_val <= d_val or cross_kd_down
bool core_long_cond  = false
bool core_short_cond = false
bool diag_c_long_cond  = false
bool diag_c_short_cond = false

// [PATCH-54] 방향전환 쿨다운 추적 변수 (BNF intraday 전용)
var int _p54_last_long_bar = 0
var int _p54_last_short_bar = 0

//────────────────────────────────────
// 18-1) 코인 전용
//────────────────────────────────────
if is_coin
    bool price_zone_long  = close >= bw1_lower and close <= bw1_upper
    bool price_zone_short = close <= bw1_upper and close >= bw1_lower
    bool stoch_core_long  = stoch_loose_long
    bool stoch_core_short = stoch_loose_short
    bool early_boost_long  = early_long  and score > 0
    bool early_boost_short = early_short and score < 0
    int bf_core_td = is_binance_fut ? bf_core_td_min : 4
    int bf_diag_c_td = is_binance_fut ? bf_diag_c_td_min : 5
    int bf_core_score = is_binance_fut ? bf_core_score_min : 20
    int bf_diag_c_score = is_binance_fut ? bf_diag_c_score_min : 35
    int bf_pred_diag_c_score = bf_pred_diag_c_score_min  // [PATCH-47] 예측형 전용 스코어 임계값
    core_long_cond :=
         bull_trend and
         regime_ok_core and
         price_zone_long and
         (base_core_long or early_boost_long or td_buy >= bf_core_td or score >= bf_core_score) and
         stoch_core_long
    core_short_cond :=
         bear_trend and
         regime_ok_core and
         price_zone_short and
         (base_core_short or early_boost_short or td_sell >= bf_core_td or score <= -bf_core_score) and
         stoch_core_short
    // Binance 선물 intraday(15m/1h): 지수/선물 완화 규칙 적용
    if is_bnf_intraday
        int td_threshold = bf_core_td
        // [PATCH-38] CORE 2-of-3 게이트 + [PATCH-35] probe trend
        int _core_sub_long = (early_long ? 1 : 0) + (td_buy >= td_threshold ? 1 : 0) + (base_core_long ? 1 : 0)
        int _core_sub_short = (early_short ? 1 : 0) + (td_sell >= td_threshold ? 1 : 0) + (base_core_short ? 1 : 0)
        bool _core_override_long = score >= 50
        bool _core_override_short = score <= -50
        int _core_min_long = core_sub_min_enable ? ((_core_override_long) ? 1 : core_sub_min_count) : 1
        int _core_min_short = core_sub_min_enable ? ((_core_override_short) ? 1 : core_sub_min_count) : 1
        // [PATCH-48] CORE 추세 완화: bear 확정이 아니면 롱 허용 (EMA bull_trend 불요)
        //   기존: bull_trend or probe_bull → 6-12봉 지연
        //   변경: trend_state != "bear" → neutral/probe_bull/bull 모두 허용 (2-4봉 지연)
        bool _core_trend_long = trend_state != "bear" and trend_state != "probe_bear"
        bool _core_trend_short = trend_state != "bull" and trend_state != "probe_bull"
        string _regime_for_core = regime_fast_enable ? regime_fast : regime_state
        // [PATCH-49] 캔들 품질 필터: 면제 임계값 대폭 하향 (45 → 20)
        //   문제: 저점에서 음봉이므로 CORE 롱 차단 → 가장 좋은 타이밍 놓침
        //   해법: score >= 20이면 캔들 필터 면제 (셋업 바 진입 허용)
        float _bar_range = high - low
        float _bar_body_ratio_long = _bar_range > syminfo.mintick ? (close - low) / _bar_range : 0.5
        float _bar_body_ratio_short = _bar_range > syminfo.mintick ? (high - close) / _bar_range : 0.5
        bool _candle_quality_long = close > open and _bar_body_ratio_long >= 0.55
        bool _candle_quality_short = close < open and _bar_body_ratio_short >= 0.55
        bool _candle_ok_long = _candle_quality_long or score >= 20
        bool _candle_ok_short = _candle_quality_short or score <= -20
        // [PATCH-66] transition score 강화는 과도했으므로 되돌리고, stat-physics 확인으로만 추가 보수화한다.
        bool _regime_ok_core = _regime_for_core == "trend" or (_regime_for_core == "transition" and math.abs(score) >= 28)
        // [PATCH-49] 스토캐스틱 과매도/과매수 오버라이드
        //   문제: 저점에서 K<D → stoch_core_long=false → CL 차단
