        //   해법: 과매도(K<=30)면 롱 허용, 과매수(K>=70)면 숏 허용
        bool _stoch_ok_long = stoch_core_long or k_val <= 30
        bool _stoch_ok_short = stoch_core_short or k_val >= 70
        // [PATCH-49] 범위 위치 필터: 고점 롱 / 저점 숏 차단 (핵심!)
        //   문제: 상승 끝(고점)에서 CL, 하락 끝(저점)에서 CS → 안티시그널
        //   해법: 20봉 범위 내 위치로 극단 진입 차단
        float _rh20 = ta.highest(high, 20)
        float _rl20 = ta.lowest(low, 20)
        float _rng20 = _rh20 - _rl20
        float _pos20 = _rng20 > syminfo.mintick ? (close - _rl20) / _rng20 : 0.5
        bool _pos_ok_long = _pos20 <= 0.75 or score >= 40   // 상위 25% 롱 차단
        bool _pos_ok_short = _pos20 >= 0.25 or score <= -40  // 하위 25% 숏 차단
        // [PATCH-49] 과매수/과매도 보호: 극단 스토캐스틱 구간 역방향 차단
        bool _not_overbought = k_val < 78 or score >= 45
        bool _not_oversold = k_val > 22 or score <= -45
        core_long_cond :=
             _core_trend_long and
             _regime_ok_core and
             _core_sub_long >= _core_min_long and
             _stoch_ok_long and
             _candle_ok_long and
             _pos_ok_long and
             _not_overbought
        core_short_cond :=
             _core_trend_short and
             _regime_ok_core and
             _core_sub_short >= _core_min_short and
             _stoch_ok_short and
             _candle_ok_short and
             _pos_ok_short and
             _not_oversold
    // [v5.0] 알트코인 유동성 필터
    // - 업비트 알트(is_alt)일 때는 거래량이 평소 대비 충분히 붙은 구간에서만 REAL LONG 허용
    bool alt_liquidity_ok = true
    if opt_enable and opt_coin_alt and is_alt_coin and not no_volume_asset
        bool alt_liquidity_gate =
             volume > volume_ma_50 * 1.5 and
             (na(volume_stability) ? true : volume_stability < 0.8) and
             volume[1] > volume_ma_50 and
             volume[2] > volume_ma_50
        alt_liquidity_ok := alt_liquidity_gate
    else if is_alt and not no_volume_asset
        alt_liquidity_ok := volume > vol_ma * 1.2
    diag_c_long_cond :=
         core_long_cond and
         regime_ok_trend and
         vol_ok_long and
         (td_buy >= bf_diag_c_td or score >= bf_diag_c_score) and
         alt_liquidity_ok and
         (not (opt_enable and opt_coin_alt and is_alt_coin) or (healthy_correlation and not suspicious_pump))
    diag_c_short_cond :=
         core_short_cond and
         regime_ok_trend and
         vol_ok_short and
         (td_sell >= bf_diag_c_td or score <= -bf_diag_c_score)
    // [PATCH-48] BNF intraday REAL: CORE 종속 유지 + 임계값 완화
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //   CORE 추세 완화(PATCH-48)로 CORE가 빨리 뜨면 REAL도 빨라짐
    //   REAL = CORE + 볼륨 + TD/score (score 임계값만 완화: 38 → 20)
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    if is_bnf_intraday
        diag_c_long_cond  := core_long_cond and
             vol_ok_long and
             (td_buy >= bf_diag_c_td or td_ok_long) and
             score >= bf_pred_diag_c_score
        diag_c_short_cond := core_short_cond and
             vol_ok_short and
             (td_sell >= bf_diag_c_td or td_ok_short) and
             score <= -bf_pred_diag_c_score
    // Binance 선물: 환경 필터(방향/OI/Funding/BTC 환경) 적용
    if is_binance_fut and bf_enable
        core_long_cond  := core_long_cond  and bf_long_ok
        core_short_cond := core_short_cond and bf_short_ok
        diag_c_long_cond  := diag_c_long_cond  and bf_long_ok
        diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and bf_short_ok
    // Binance 선물 추가 최적화(과열/트랩/청산 구간)
    if is_binance_fut and opt_enable and opt_binance_fut
        core_long_cond := core_long_cond and not overheated_long and not trap_alert
        diag_c_long_cond := diag_c_long_cond and not overheated_long and not trap_alert
        if liquidation_cascade
            diag_c_long_cond := diag_c_long_cond or (core_long_cond and bull_trend)
    // Binance 선물 롱/숏 품질 동시 게이트: trend + ADX + score + posterior + wave
    // [PATCH-46+48] BNF intraday는 CORE 추세 완화 적용, 확인형 조건 완화
    if is_binance_fut and bf_gate_quality_enable
        bool _is_bnf_intraday = is_bnf_intraday
        // 롱 추세 확인: BNF intraday는 transition 허용 + probe_bull 허용
        bool bf_long_trend_ok = _is_bnf_intraday ?
             ((regime_state == "trend" or regime_state == "transition") and (bull_trend or trend_state == "probe_bull") and htf_state != "bear") :
             (regime_state == "trend" and adx_val >= bf_gate_adx_min and bull_trend and htf_state != "bear")
        bool bf_long_post_wave_ok = zz_post_prob_long >= bf_gate_post_min and zz_wave_conf >= bf_gate_wave_min
        bool bf_long_core_gate_ok = bf_long_trend_ok and bf_long_post_wave_ok and score >= bf_gate_core_score_min
        bool bf_long_diag_c_gate_ok = bf_long_trend_ok and bf_long_post_wave_ok and score >= bf_gate_diag_c_score_min
        core_long_cond := core_long_cond and bf_long_core_gate_ok
        diag_c_long_cond := diag_c_long_cond and bf_long_diag_c_gate_ok
        // 롱 타이밍 보정: BNF intraday는 예측형이므로 직전고점갱신/1봉확인 비활성
        bool bf_long_prev_quality_ok = not na(score[1]) and score[1] >= bf_gate_core_score_min and zz_post_prob_long[1] >= bf_gate_post_min and zz_wave_conf[1] >= bf_gate_wave_min
        bool bf_long_prevbar_ok = _is_bnf_intraday or (not bf_gate_prevbar_confirm or bf_long_prev_quality_ok)
        bool bf_long_break_high_ok = _is_bnf_intraday or (not bf_gate_follow_break_extreme or na(high[1]) or close >= high[1])
        float bf_long_rise_pct = close[1] != 0 ? ((close - close[1]) / close[1]) * 100.0 : 0.0
        float bf_long_range = high - low
        float bf_long_upper_wick = high - math.max(open, close)
        float bf_long_upper_wick_ratio = bf_long_range > syminfo.mintick ? (bf_long_upper_wick / bf_long_range) : 0.0
        bool bf_long_exhaustion_risk = bf_long_rise_pct >= bf_gate_dump_pct and bf_long_upper_wick_ratio >= bf_gate_wick_min and k_val >= (100 - bf_gate_stoch_os) and (close < open or cross_kd_down)
        core_long_cond := core_long_cond and bf_long_prevbar_ok and bf_long_break_high_ok
        diag_c_long_cond := diag_c_long_cond and bf_long_prevbar_ok and bf_long_break_high_ok
        if bf_gate_exhaustion_guard and bf_long_exhaustion_risk
            early_long := false
            core_long_cond := false
            diag_c_long_cond := false
        // 숏 추세 확인: BNF intraday는 transition 허용 + probe_bear 허용
        bool bf_short_trend_ok = _is_bnf_intraday ?
             ((regime_state == "trend" or regime_state == "transition") and (bear_trend or trend_state == "probe_bear") and htf_state != "bull") :
             (regime_state == "trend" and adx_val >= bf_gate_adx_min and bear_trend and htf_state != "bull")
        bool bf_short_post_wave_ok = zz_post_prob_short >= bf_gate_post_min and zz_wave_conf >= bf_gate_wave_min
        bool bf_short_core_gate_ok = bf_short_trend_ok and bf_short_post_wave_ok and score <= -bf_gate_core_score_min
        bool bf_short_diag_c_gate_ok = bf_short_trend_ok and bf_short_post_wave_ok and score <= -bf_gate_diag_c_score_min
        core_short_cond := core_short_cond and bf_short_core_gate_ok
        diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and bf_short_diag_c_gate_ok
        // 숏 타이밍 보정: BNF intraday는 예측형이므로 직전저점갱신/1봉확인 비활성
        bool bf_short_prev_quality_ok = not na(score[1]) and score[1] <= -bf_gate_core_score_min and zz_post_prob_short[1] >= bf_gate_post_min and zz_wave_conf[1] >= bf_gate_wave_min
        bool bf_short_prevbar_ok = _is_bnf_intraday or (not bf_gate_prevbar_confirm or bf_short_prev_quality_ok)
        bool bf_short_break_low_ok = _is_bnf_intraday or (not bf_gate_follow_break_extreme or na(low[1]) or close <= low[1])
        float bf_short_drop_pct = close[1] != 0 ? ((close[1] - close) / close[1]) * 100.0 : 0.0
        float bf_short_range = high - low
        float bf_short_lower_wick = math.min(open, close) - low
        float bf_short_lower_wick_ratio = bf_short_range > syminfo.mintick ? (bf_short_lower_wick / bf_short_range) : 0.0
        bool bf_short_exhaustion_risk = bf_short_drop_pct >= bf_gate_dump_pct and bf_short_lower_wick_ratio >= bf_gate_wick_min and k_val <= bf_gate_stoch_os and (close > open or cross_kd_up)
        core_short_cond := core_short_cond and bf_short_prevbar_ok and bf_short_break_low_ok
        diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and bf_short_prevbar_ok and bf_short_break_low_ok
        if bf_gate_exhaustion_guard and bf_short_exhaustion_risk
            early_short := false
            core_short_cond := false
            diag_c_short_cond := false
    // 횡보장에서는 CORE 롱/숏을 동일하게 차단
    if is_binance_fut and bf_core_gate_range_block and regime_state == "range"
        core_long_cond := false
        core_short_cond := false
    // [PATCH-50] v2.6.5 동기화: 필요할 때만 강제 덮어쓰기(입력값 우선 보장)
    if legacy_v265_sync
        float v265_diag_c_min = math.max(math.min(0.57 + (atr_ratio > vz_high_thr ? 0.01 : atr_ratio < vz_low_thr ? -0.01 : 0.0), 0.60), 0.56)
        core_long_cond := (htf_state == "bull" or trend_state == "bull") and score >= 5 and close >= base_line and zz_post_prob_long >= (v265_diag_c_min - 0.03)
        core_short_cond := (htf_state == "bear" or trend_state == "bear") and score <= -5 and close <= base_line and zz_post_prob_short >= (v265_diag_c_min - 0.03)
        diag_c_long_cond := core_long_cond and score >= 15 and (vol_strong or vol_ultra) and cross_kd_up and zz_post_prob_long >= v265_diag_c_min
        diag_c_short_cond := core_short_cond and score <= -15 and (vol_strong or vol_ultra) and cross_kd_down and zz_post_prob_short >= v265_diag_c_min
    // [PATCH-53] BNF intraday: 공격적 품질 강화 — 확신 없으면 안 한다
    //   원칙: 횡보장에선 쉰다 / HTF 역행은 거의 안 한다 / 잡음 완전 제거
    if is_bnf_intraday
        // ① CORE: score 20+ AND posterior 0.56+ (노이즈 완전 제거)
        core_long_cond := core_long_cond and score >= 8 and zz_post_prob_long >= 0.51
        core_short_cond := core_short_cond and score <= -8 and zz_post_prob_short >= 0.51
        // ② HTF 역방향: score 35+ 필요 (역추세는 매우 강할 때만)
        if htf_state == "bear"
            core_long_cond := core_long_cond and score >= 24
            diag_c_long_cond := diag_c_long_cond and score >= 24
        if htf_state == "bull"
            core_short_cond := core_short_cond and score <= -24
            diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and score <= -24
        // ③ 횡보(range) 레짐: 신호 완전 차단
        if regime_state == "range"
            core_long_cond := false
            core_short_cond := false
            diag_c_long_cond := false
            diag_c_short_cond := false
        // ④ REAL: score 25+ (실행 신호 = 높은 확신 필수)
        diag_c_long_cond := diag_c_long_cond and score >= 25
        diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and score <= -25
        // [PATCH-54c] ⑤ 숏: 50봉 낙폭 기반 — "이미 많이 떨어졌으면 숏 금지"
        float _peak50 = ta.highest(high, 50)
        float _drawdown50 = _peak50 > 0 ? (_peak50 - close) / _peak50 * 100.0 : 0.0
        if _drawdown50 > 5.0 and score > -35
            core_short_cond := false
        // [PATCH-54c] ⑥ 롱: 20봉 range 위치 기반 — "이미 많이 올랐으면 롱 주의"
        float _rh20 = ta.highest(high, 20)
        float _rl20 = ta.lowest(low, 20)
        float _rng20 = _rh20 - _rl20
        float _pos20 = _rng20 > syminfo.mintick ? (close - _rl20) / _rng20 : 0.5
        if _pos20 > 0.80 and score < 35
            core_long_cond := false
        if _pos20 > 0.70 and score < 35
            diag_c_long_cond := false
        // [PATCH-54] ⑦ 방향전환 쿨다운: 3봉 이내 역방향 score 35+ (pRL↔CS 혼재 방지)
        int _bars_since_l = bar_index - _p54_last_long_bar
        int _bars_since_s = bar_index - _p54_last_short_bar
        if _bars_since_s > 0 and _bars_since_s <= 3
            core_long_cond := core_long_cond and score >= 24
            diag_c_long_cond := diag_c_long_cond and score >= 24
        if _bars_since_l > 0 and _bars_since_l <= 3
            core_short_cond := core_short_cond and score <= -24
            diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and score <= -24

// [PATCH-67] Binance-only runtime: legacy noncoin path removed on purpose.

//────────────────────────────────────
// 18-3) 공격 모드 + TD 쿨다운
//────────────────────────────────────
bool aggressive_long =
     is_coin and
     (amc_preset == "공격" or amc_preset == "맥스") and
     (cross_kd_up or bull_td or td_buy >= 6) and
     vol_ultra and
     score >= 35 and
     regime_ok_trend
bool aggressive_short =
     is_coin and
     (amc_preset == "공격" or amc_preset == "맥스") and
     (cross_kd_down or bear_td or td_sell >= 6) and
     vol_ultra and
     score <= -45 and
     zz_wave_conf >= 0.70 and
     zz_post_prob_short >= 0.62 and
     regime_ok_trend
// [PATCH-46] legacy_v265_sync 옵션 상태에서 공격 모드 보강 신호 병합
if is_coin
    diag_c_long_cond  := diag_c_long_cond  or aggressive_long
    diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond or aggressive_short
// [PATCH] REAL 보수 필터 적용 (REAL만) : conflict + momentum(slope)
if strict_conflict_block
    diag_c_long_cond  := diag_c_long_cond  and not conflict_long
    diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and not conflict_short
if use_mom_filter_real
    bool mom_ok_long  = slope >=  slope_min
    bool mom_ok_short = slope <= -slope_min
    diag_c_long_cond  := diag_c_long_cond  and mom_ok_long
    diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and mom_ok_short
// [ZZ_FUSION_START] – Wave Filter / Conflict Logic
bool diag_c_long_cond_raw  = diag_c_long_cond
bool diag_c_short_cond_raw = diag_c_short_cond
bool core_long_cond_raw  = core_long_cond
bool core_short_cond_raw = core_short_cond
bool zz_wave_bull_ok = zz_main_bull and zz_bull_prob >= zz_min_prob_long_eff
bool zz_wave_bear_ok = (not zz_main_bull) and zz_bear_prob >= zz_min_prob_short_eff
bool zz_conflict_long = false
bool zz_conflict_short = false
if zz_use_wave_filter and is_coin
    if zz_wave_mode == "필터"
        diag_c_long_cond  := diag_c_long_cond  and zz_wave_bull_ok
        core_long_cond  := core_long_cond  and zz_wave_bull_ok
        diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and zz_wave_bear_ok
        core_short_cond := core_short_cond and zz_wave_bear_ok
    // 경고만 모드: 조건은 유지, 충돌 플래그만 표시
    if zz_wave_mode == "경고만"
        zz_conflict_long  := (diag_c_long_cond_raw or core_long_cond_raw)   and not zz_wave_bull_ok and zz_wave_conf >= 0.6
        zz_conflict_short := (diag_c_short_cond_raw or core_short_cond_raw) and not zz_wave_bear_ok and zz_wave_conf >= 0.6
// [EV_FILTER_START] – Posterior EV 필터 (REAL/CORE)
// - Wave 필터 적용 이후, EV가 기준 미만이면 신호 차단
if use_ev_filter
    if core_long_cond and zz_ev_core_long < ev_thr_core_L
        core_long_cond := false
    if core_short_cond and zz_ev_core_short < ev_thr_core_S
        core_short_cond := false
    if diag_c_long_cond and zz_ev_diag_c_long < ev_thr_diag_c_L
        diag_c_long_cond := false
    if diag_c_short_cond and zz_ev_diag_c_short < ev_thr_diag_c_S
        diag_c_short_cond := false
// [EV_FILTER_END]
// 15m 구조 확인 게이트(60m 신호에만 적용)
if ltf_gate_apply
    early_long := early_long and ltf_gate_long
    early_short := early_short and ltf_gate_short
    core_long_cond := core_long_cond and ltf_gate_long
    core_short_cond := core_short_cond and ltf_gate_short
    diag_c_long_cond := diag_c_long_cond and ltf_gate_long
    diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and ltf_gate_short
// 횡보(range) 진입 차단은 바이낸스 선물에만 적용(비바이낸스 5.5.5.5 동작 유지)
if range_block_entries and regime_state == "range" and is_binance_fut
    core_long_cond := false
    core_short_cond := false
    diag_c_long_cond := false
    diag_c_short_cond := false
// 주식(키움) 전용: 장중 외 신호 차단 + 롱만 허용
if is_stock and not stock_session_ok
    early_long := false
    early_short := false
    core_long_cond := false
    core_short_cond := false
    diag_c_long_cond := false
    diag_c_short_cond := false
if stock_long_only
    early_short := false
    core_short_cond := false
    diag_c_short_cond := false
// [STAT_PHYSICS] 시장 상태 점수: entropy / coherence / critical transition risk
float sp_dir_bias = math.abs(ta.sma(close > open ? 1.0 : close < open ? -1.0 : 0.0, 12))
float sp_directional_entropy = f_clamp01(1.0 - sp_dir_bias)
float sp_entropy_score = f_clamp01(0.60 * f_clamp01(ci_raw / 100.0) + 0.40 * sp_directional_entropy)
float sp_price_spin = close > close[1] ? 1.0 : close < close[1] ? -1.0 : 0.0
float sp_score_spin = score_panel > 0 ? 1.0 : score_panel < 0 ? -1.0 : 0.0
float sp_post_spin = zz_post_prob_long > zz_post_prob_short ? 1.0 : zz_post_prob_long < zz_post_prob_short ? -1.0 : 0.0
float sp_wave_spin = zz_main_bull ? 1.0 : -1.0
float sp_trend_dir = trend_state == "bull" or trend_state == "probe_bull" ? 1.0 : trend_state == "bear" or trend_state == "probe_bear" ? -1.0 : 0.0
float sp_htf_dir = htf_state == "bull" or htf_state == "probe_bull" ? 1.0 : htf_state == "bear" or htf_state == "probe_bear" ? -1.0 : 0.0
float sp_field_sum = sp_price_spin + sp_score_spin + sp_post_spin + sp_wave_spin + sp_trend_dir + sp_htf_dir
float sp_field_alignment = f_clamp01(math.abs(sp_field_sum) / 6.0)
float sp_price_domain_same = sp_price_spin != 0 and sp_price_spin == nz(sp_price_spin[1]) ? 1.0 : 0.0
float sp_score_domain_same = sp_score_spin != 0 and sp_score_spin == nz(sp_score_spin[1]) ? 1.0 : 0.0
float sp_post_domain_same = sp_post_spin != 0 and sp_post_spin == nz(sp_post_spin[1]) ? 1.0 : 0.0
float sp_domain_persistence = f_clamp01(
     0.50 * ta.sma(sp_price_domain_same, 8) +
     0.30 * ta.sma(sp_score_domain_same, 8) +
     0.20 * ta.sma(sp_post_domain_same, 8))
float sp_price_flip = sp_price_spin != 0 and sp_price_spin != nz(sp_price_spin[1]) ? 1.0 : 0.0
float sp_score_flip = sp_score_spin != 0 and sp_score_spin != nz(sp_score_spin[1]) ? 1.0 : 0.0
float sp_post_flip = sp_post_spin != 0 and sp_post_spin != nz(sp_post_spin[1]) ? 1.0 : 0.0
float sp_domain_wall_density = f_clamp01(
     0.45 * ta.sma(sp_price_flip, 8) +
     0.35 * ta.sma(sp_score_flip, 8) +
     0.20 * ta.sma(sp_post_flip, 8))
float sp_dir_agreement = (sp_trend_dir == 0 or sp_htf_dir == 0) ? 0.50 : (sp_trend_dir == sp_htf_dir ? 1.0 : 0.0)
float sp_di_balance = (pDI + mDI) > 0 ? f_clamp01(math.abs(pDI - mDI) / (pDI + mDI)) : 0.0
float sp_post_spread = f_clamp01(math.abs(zz_post_prob_long - zz_post_prob_short))
float sp_wave_order = f_clamp01(zz_wave_conf)
float sp_score_order = f_clamp01(math.abs(score_panel) / 100.0)
float sp_regime_order = regime_state == "trend" ? 1.0 : regime_state == "transition" ? 0.60 : 0.25
float sp_score_sigma = ta.stdev(score_panel, 12)
float sp_score_sigma_norm = f_clamp01(sp_score_sigma / 35.0)
float sp_susceptibility = f_clamp01(
     0.45 * sp_domain_wall_density +
     0.30 * sp_score_sigma_norm +
     0.25 * (1.0 - sp_field_alignment))
float sp_cluster_order = f_clamp01(0.60 * sp_field_alignment + 0.40 * sp_domain_persistence)
float sp_coherence_score = f_clamp01(
     0.18 * sp_dir_agreement +
     0.14 * sp_di_balance +
     0.16 * sp_post_spread +
     0.16 * sp_wave_order +
     0.18 * sp_cluster_order +
     0.10 * sp_score_order +
     0.08 * sp_regime_order)
float sp_upper_wick_ratio = candle_range > syminfo.mintick ? (upper_wick / candle_range) : 0.0
float sp_lower_wick_ratio = candle_range > syminfo.mintick ? (lower_wick / candle_range) : 0.0
float sp_wick_instability = math.max(sp_upper_wick_ratio, sp_lower_wick_ratio)
float sp_body_instability = f_clamp01(1.0 - body_pct)
float sp_atr_stretch = f_clamp01((atr_ratio - 1.0) / 1.5)
float sp_posterior_instability = f_clamp01(1.0 - sp_post_spread)
float sp_free_energy = f_clamp01(
     0.40 * sp_entropy_score +
     0.35 * sp_susceptibility +
     0.25 * (1.0 - sp_cluster_order))
float sp_transition_risk = f_clamp01(
     0.16 * sp_domain_wall_density +
     0.16 * sp_susceptibility +
     0.18 * sp_wick_instability +
     0.12 * sp_body_instability +
     0.12 * sp_atr_stretch +
     0.10 * sp_posterior_instability +
     0.08 * sp_entropy_score +
     0.08 * (1.0 - sp_coherence_score))
string sp_state = "MIXED"
if sp_transition_risk >= 0.78 or sp_free_energy >= 0.74
    sp_state := "CRITICAL"
else if (sp_entropy_score >= 0.68 and sp_coherence_score <= 0.42) or (sp_domain_wall_density >= 0.55 and sp_field_alignment <= 0.45)
    sp_state := "DISORDERED"
else if sp_coherence_score >= 0.68 and sp_entropy_score <= 0.42 and sp_transition_risk <= 0.42 and sp_free_energy <= 0.40
    sp_state := "ORDERED"
// [PATCH-71] FEBT Phase 1 SHADOW: OHLCV 기반 timing sensor 계산(라이브 판정 미사용)
string febt_mode_runtime = f_febt_mode_runtime(febt_mode)
string febt_authority_runtime = f_febt_authority(febt_mode_runtime)
float febt_range_safe = math.max(candle_range, syminfo.mintick)
float febt_body_ratio = f_clamp01(body_pct)
float febt_range_pos = f_clamp01((close - low) / febt_range_safe)
float febt_upper_wick_ratio = candle_range > syminfo.mintick ? (upper_wick / candle_range) : 0.0
float febt_lower_wick_ratio = candle_range > syminfo.mintick ? (lower_wick / candle_range) : 0.0
float febt_dir_close_pos_long = febt_range_pos
float febt_dir_close_pos_short = f_clamp01(1.0 - febt_range_pos)
float febt_dir_reject_long = f_clamp01(febt_lower_wick_ratio)
float febt_dir_reject_short = f_clamp01(febt_upper_wick_ratio)
float febt_opp_wick_long = f_clamp01(febt_upper_wick_ratio)
float febt_opp_wick_short = f_clamp01(febt_lower_wick_ratio)
float febt_vol_ma_20_raw = ta.sma(volume, 20)
float febt_vol_ma_20 = math.max(febt_vol_ma_20_raw, 1.0)
float febt_vol_ratio_20 = volume / febt_vol_ma_20
float febt_vol_spike = f_clamp01(febt_vol_ratio_20 / 2.5)
int febt_same_dir_streak_long = f_febt_same_dir_streak(true)
int febt_same_dir_streak_short = f_febt_same_dir_streak(false)
float febt_recent_move_1_pct = not na(close[1]) ? ((close - close[1]) / math.max(close[1], syminfo.mintick)) * 100.0 : na
float febt_recent_move_2_pct = not na(close[2]) ? ((close - close[2]) / math.max(close[2], syminfo.mintick)) * 100.0 : na
float febt_break_ref_long = na(high[1]) or na(high[2]) ? na : math.max(high[1], high[2])
float febt_break_ref_short = na(low[1]) or na(low[2]) ? na : math.min(low[1], low[2])
float febt_break_retention_long = na(febt_break_ref_long) ? na : f_clamp01(0.5 + ((close - febt_break_ref_long) / febt_range_safe))
float febt_break_retention_short = na(febt_break_ref_short) ? na : f_clamp01(0.5 + ((febt_break_ref_short - close) / febt_range_safe))
float febt_close_control_long = f_clamp01(0.65 * febt_dir_close_pos_long + 0.35 * febt_body_ratio)
float febt_close_control_short = f_clamp01(0.65 * febt_dir_close_pos_short + 0.35 * febt_body_ratio)
float febt_prev_range = na(high[1]) or na(low[1]) ? na : (high[1] - low[1])
float febt_prev_range_safe = na(febt_prev_range) ? syminfo.mintick : math.max(febt_prev_range, syminfo.mintick)
float febt_body_ratio_prev = not na(close[1]) and not na(open[1]) ? math.abs(close[1] - open[1]) / febt_prev_range_safe : na
float febt_dir_body_prev_long = not na(close[1]) and not na(open[1]) and close[1] >= open[1] ? f_clamp01(febt_body_ratio_prev) : 0.0
float febt_dir_body_prev_short = not na(close[1]) and not na(open[1]) and close[1] <= open[1] ? f_clamp01(febt_body_ratio_prev) : 0.0
float febt_dir_body_now_long = close >= open ? f_clamp01(febt_body_ratio) : 0.0
float febt_dir_body_now_short = close <= open ? f_clamp01(febt_body_ratio) : 0.0
float febt_extension_fail_long = na(high[1]) ? na : f_clamp01((high[1] - close) / febt_range_safe)
float febt_extension_fail_short = na(low[1]) ? na : f_clamp01((close - low[1]) / febt_range_safe)
float febt_impulse_decay_long = f_clamp01(0.60 * math.max(febt_dir_body_prev_long - febt_dir_body_now_long, 0.0) + 0.40 * nz(febt_extension_fail_long, 0.0))
float febt_impulse_decay_short = f_clamp01(0.60 * math.max(febt_dir_body_prev_short - febt_dir_body_now_short, 0.0) + 0.40 * nz(febt_extension_fail_short, 0.0))
float febt_counter_rejection_long = f_clamp01(0.60 * febt_dir_reject_long + 0.40 * febt_dir_close_pos_long)
float febt_counter_rejection_short = f_clamp01(0.60 * febt_dir_reject_short + 0.40 * febt_dir_close_pos_short)
float febt_micro_absorption_long = f_clamp01(0.45 * febt_vol_spike + 0.35 * febt_dir_reject_long + 0.20 * febt_dir_close_pos_long)
float febt_micro_absorption_short = f_clamp01(0.45 * febt_vol_spike + 0.35 * febt_dir_reject_short + 0.20 * febt_dir_close_pos_short)
float febt_same_dir_streak_norm_long = f_clamp01(febt_same_dir_streak_long / 4.0)
float febt_same_dir_streak_norm_short = f_clamp01(febt_same_dir_streak_short / 4.0)
float febt_recent_move_1_norm = f_clamp01(math.abs(febt_recent_move_1_pct) / 2.5)
float febt_recent_move_2_norm = f_clamp01(math.abs(febt_recent_move_2_pct) / 4.0)
float febt_break_retention_norm_long = f_clamp01(febt_break_retention_long)
float febt_break_retention_norm_short = f_clamp01(febt_break_retention_short)
float febt_close_control_norm_long = f_clamp01(febt_close_control_long)
float febt_close_control_norm_short = f_clamp01(febt_close_control_short)
float febt_impulse_decay_norm_long = f_clamp01(febt_impulse_decay_long)
float febt_impulse_decay_norm_short = f_clamp01(febt_impulse_decay_short)
float febt_counter_rejection_norm_long = f_clamp01(febt_counter_rejection_long)
float febt_counter_rejection_norm_short = f_clamp01(febt_counter_rejection_short)
float febt_micro_absorption_norm_long = f_clamp01(febt_micro_absorption_long)
float febt_micro_absorption_norm_short = f_clamp01(febt_micro_absorption_short)
float febt_lock_score_long = f_clamp01(
     0.28 * febt_dir_close_pos_long +
     0.22 * febt_break_retention_norm_long +
     0.20 * febt_body_ratio +
     0.18 * febt_dir_reject_long +
     0.12 * febt_micro_absorption_norm_long)
float febt_lock_score_short = f_clamp01(
     0.28 * febt_dir_close_pos_short +
     0.22 * febt_break_retention_norm_short +
     0.20 * febt_body_ratio +
     0.18 * febt_dir_reject_short +
     0.12 * febt_micro_absorption_norm_short)
float febt_delay_cost_long = f_clamp01(
     (0.30 * febt_close_control_norm_long +
      0.24 * febt_break_retention_norm_long +
      0.18 * febt_recent_move_1_norm +
      0.16 * febt_counter_rejection_norm_long +
      0.12 * febt_micro_absorption_norm_long) - (0.15 * febt_impulse_decay_norm_long))
float febt_delay_cost_short = f_clamp01(
     (0.30 * febt_close_control_norm_short +
      0.24 * febt_break_retention_norm_short +
      0.18 * febt_recent_move_1_norm +
      0.16 * febt_counter_rejection_norm_short +
      0.12 * febt_micro_absorption_norm_short) - (0.15 * febt_impulse_decay_norm_short))
float febt_late_risk_long_raw =
     0.34 * febt_same_dir_streak_norm_long +
     0.26 * febt_recent_move_1_norm +
     0.18 * febt_recent_move_2_norm +
     0.12 * febt_close_control_norm_long +
     0.10 * febt_impulse_decay_norm_long
float febt_late_risk_short_raw =
     0.34 * febt_same_dir_streak_norm_short +
     0.26 * febt_recent_move_1_norm +
     0.18 * febt_recent_move_2_norm +
     0.12 * febt_close_control_norm_short +
     0.10 * febt_impulse_decay_norm_short
float febt_late_risk_long = f_clamp01(febt_late_risk_long_raw - (febt_micro_absorption_norm_long >= 0.70 ? 0.08 : 0.0))
float febt_late_risk_short = f_clamp01(febt_late_risk_short_raw - (febt_micro_absorption_norm_short >= 0.70 ? 0.08 : 0.0))
float febt_failure_risk_long = f_clamp01(
     0.30 * febt_opp_wick_long +
     0.24 * febt_impulse_decay_norm_long +
     0.18 * (1.0 - febt_break_retention_norm_long) +
     0.16 * (1.0 - febt_close_control_norm_long) +
     0.12 * (1.0 - febt_counter_rejection_norm_long))
float febt_failure_risk_short = f_clamp01(
     0.30 * febt_opp_wick_short +
     0.24 * febt_impulse_decay_norm_short +
     0.18 * (1.0 - febt_break_retention_norm_short) +
     0.16 * (1.0 - febt_close_control_norm_short) +
     0.12 * (1.0 - febt_counter_rejection_norm_short))
float febt_edge_long = f_clamp01(0.5 + febt_delay_cost_long - febt_late_risk_long) - 0.5
float febt_edge_short = f_clamp01(0.5 + febt_delay_cost_short - febt_late_risk_short) - 0.5
bool febt_state_valid = regime_state != "range_hard_block" and not na(sp_coherence_score) and not na(sp_transition_risk) and sp_coherence_score >= 0.30 and sp_transition_risk <= 0.84
bool febt_inputs_ready = not na(close[2]) and not na(high[2]) and not na(low[2]) and not na(open[1]) and not na(close[1]) and not na(febt_vol_ma_20_raw) and not na(sp_coherence_score) and not na(sp_transition_risk)
bool febt_scores_ready = febt_inputs_ready and not na(febt_lock_score_long) and not na(febt_lock_score_short) and not na(febt_delay_cost_long) and not na(febt_delay_cost_short) and not na(febt_late_risk_long) and not na(febt_late_risk_short) and not na(febt_failure_risk_long) and not na(febt_failure_risk_short) and not na(febt_edge_long) and not na(febt_edge_short)
bool febt_calc_ok = febt_mode_runtime != "OFF" and febt_scores_ready
string febt_calc_reason = febt_mode_runtime == "OFF" ? "OFF" : not febt_inputs_ready ? "MISSING_INPUT" : not febt_scores_ready ? "SHADOW_FAILSAFE" : "OK"
string febt_phase_long = febt_calc_ok ? f_febt_phase(febt_state_valid, febt_lock_score_long, febt_edge_long, febt_late_risk_long, febt_failure_risk_long) : "UNKNOWN"
string febt_phase_short = febt_calc_ok ? f_febt_phase(febt_state_valid, febt_lock_score_short, febt_edge_short, febt_late_risk_short, febt_failure_risk_short) : "UNKNOWN"
// [QUALITY_GATE_FIX_2026-02-20]
// 품질 게이트(정확도 우선): 노이즈 구간을 줄이고 방향 우세가 명확할 때만 통과
// 주의: tuple destructuring([a,b,...])/함수 내부 percentrank는 컴파일 이슈가 있어 사용하지 않는다.
// 비바이낸스(주식/지수/FX/ETF/업비트)는 5.5.5.5 동작을 우선 유지한다.
bool quality_filter_runtime = quality_filter_enable and is_binance_fut
bool quality_gate_strict_runtime = quality_mode == "STRICT"
int quality_gate_mask = QMASK_ALL
if quality_filter_runtime
    quality_gate_mask := f_quality_gate_mask(
         quality_gate_strict_runtime,
         quality_noncoin_relax,
         is_coin, no_volume_asset, atr_ratio, vol_ratio, score, zz_wave_conf,
         zz_post_prob_long, zz_post_prob_short, zz_ev_core_long, zz_ev_core_short, zz_ev_diag_c_long, zz_ev_diag_c_short,
         sp_entropy_score, sp_coherence_score, sp_transition_risk, sp_free_energy,
         conflict_long, conflict_short, zz_conflict_long, zz_conflict_short
    )
if quality_filter_runtime
    early_long := early_long and f_quality_mask_has(quality_gate_mask, QMASK_EARLY_LONG)
    early_short := early_short and f_quality_mask_has(quality_gate_mask, QMASK_EARLY_SHORT)
    core_long_cond := core_long_cond and f_quality_mask_has(quality_gate_mask, QMASK_CORE_LONG)
    core_short_cond := core_short_cond and f_quality_mask_has(quality_gate_mask, QMASK_CORE_SHORT)
    diag_c_long_cond := diag_c_long_cond and f_quality_mask_has(quality_gate_mask, QMASK_DIAG_C_LONG)
    diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and f_quality_mask_has(quality_gate_mask, QMASK_DIAG_C_SHORT)
bool pine_stage1_bundle_runtime = pine_stage1_bundle_enable and quality_filter_runtime
int pine_stage1_bundle_mask = quality_gate_mask
// [PATCH-60] 운영 정책: 현재 라이브 출력은 LONG/SHORT only, source band는 EARLY/CORE, qty profile은 FIXED
bool binance_diag_c_block_eff = is_binance_fut and binance_diag_c_block
if binance_diag_c_block_eff
    diag_c_long_cond := false
    diag_c_short_cond := false
// 현재 라이브 source band: EARLY < CORE
bool early_stage_long = early_long
bool early_stage_short = early_short
bool diag_b_long = false
bool diag_b_short = false
// [PATCH-46] legacy_v265_sync 하드코딩 (항상 true), else 브랜치 삭제
diag_b_long := core_long_cond and
     score >= diag_b_score_min and
     zz_post_prob_long >= f_diag_b_post_pair_min() and
     zz_wave_conf >= diag_b_wave_conf_min and
     (zz_post_prob_long - zz_post_prob_short) >= diag_b_post_spread_min and
     (not diag_b_vol_gate or vol_ratio >= diag_b_vol_min) and
     (not diag_b_k_dir_gate or k_val > nz(k_val[1]))
diag_b_short := core_short_cond and
     score <= -diag_b_score_min and
     zz_post_prob_short >= f_diag_b_post_pair_min() and
     zz_wave_conf >= diag_b_wave_conf_min and
     (zz_post_prob_short - zz_post_prob_long) >= diag_b_post_spread_min and
     (not diag_b_vol_gate or vol_ratio >= diag_b_vol_min) and
     (not diag_b_k_dir_gate or k_val < nz(k_val[1]))
// [PATCH-46] 버그 수정: bf_diag_b_short_enable은 숏만 차단 (기존: 롱/숏 모두 차단)
if is_binance_fut and not bf_diag_b_short_enable
    diag_b_short := false
if is_bnf_intraday
    diag_b_long := diag_b_long and (htf_state != "bear" or score >= 50)
    diag_b_short := diag_b_short and (htf_state != "bull" or score <= -50)
if diag_b_conflict_block
    diag_b_long := diag_b_long and not conflict_long and not zz_conflict_long
    diag_b_short := diag_b_short and not conflict_short and not zz_conflict_short
if use_mom_filter_real
    float diag_b_slope_min = slope_min * 0.5
    diag_b_long := diag_b_long and slope >= diag_b_slope_min
    diag_b_short := diag_b_short and slope <= -diag_b_slope_min
if quality_filter_runtime
    diag_b_long := diag_b_long and f_quality_mask_has(quality_gate_mask, QMASK_DIAG_B_LONG)
    diag_b_short := diag_b_short and f_quality_mask_has(quality_gate_mask, QMASK_DIAG_B_SHORT)
if binance_diag_c_block_eff
    diag_b_long := false
    diag_b_short := false
// 단계 중복 제거(동일 bar에서 상위 단계 1개만 유지)
diag_b_long := diag_b_long and not diag_c_long_cond
diag_b_short := diag_b_short and not diag_c_short_cond
core_long_cond := core_long_cond and not diag_b_long and not diag_c_long_cond
core_short_cond := core_short_cond and not diag_b_short and not diag_c_short_cond
early_stage_long := early_stage_long and not core_long_cond and not diag_b_long and not diag_c_long_cond
early_stage_short := early_stage_short and not core_short_cond and not diag_b_short and not diag_c_short_cond
// [PATCH-58] 기대 승률 하한 가드
bool profit_guard_apply = profit_guard_enable and (not profit_guard_binance_only or is_binance_fut)
if profit_guard_apply
    float wr_floor = math.max(0.50, math.min(profit_guard_wr_min, 0.90))
    float post_spread_min_eff = math.max(0.0, profit_guard_post_spread_min)
    float ev_floor_core_long = wr_floor * (core_rr_L + 1.0) - 1.0
    float ev_floor_core_short = wr_floor * (core_rr_S + 1.0) - 1.0
    float ev_floor_diag_c_long = wr_floor * (diag_c_rr_L + 1.0) - 1.0
    float ev_floor_diag_c_short = wr_floor * (diag_c_rr_S + 1.0) - 1.0
    bool pg_core_long = zz_post_prob_long >= wr_floor and (zz_post_prob_long - zz_post_prob_short) >= post_spread_min_eff and zz_ev_core_long >= ev_floor_core_long
    bool pg_core_short = zz_post_prob_short >= wr_floor and (zz_post_prob_short - zz_post_prob_long) >= post_spread_min_eff and zz_ev_core_short >= ev_floor_core_short
    bool pg_diag_c_long = zz_post_prob_long >= wr_floor and (zz_post_prob_long - zz_post_prob_short) >= post_spread_min_eff and zz_ev_diag_c_long >= ev_floor_diag_c_long
    bool pg_diag_c_short = zz_post_prob_short >= wr_floor and (zz_post_prob_short - zz_post_prob_long) >= post_spread_min_eff and zz_ev_diag_c_short >= ev_floor_diag_c_short
    early_stage_long := early_stage_long and pg_core_long
    early_stage_short := early_stage_short and pg_core_short
    core_long_cond := core_long_cond and pg_core_long
    core_short_cond := core_short_cond and pg_core_short
    diag_b_long := diag_b_long and pg_diag_c_long
    diag_b_short := diag_b_short and pg_diag_c_short
    diag_c_long_cond := diag_c_long_cond and pg_diag_c_long
    diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and pg_diag_c_short
// [PATCH-59] 승률 방어 스택: 진입 신호에 다중 하한 적용
bool wr_def_apply = winrate_defense_enable and (not winrate_defense_binance_only or is_binance_fut)
if wr_def_apply
    float strict_bias = quality_master_mode == "ULTRA" ? 0.02 : quality_master_mode == "BALANCED" ? -0.01 : 0.0
    float growth_relax = monthly_target_profile == "M10_GROWTH" ? -0.01 : 0.0
    float total_bias = strict_bias + growth_relax
    float wr_post_core_min = (winrate_defense_mode == "STRICT" ? 0.58 : 0.54) + total_bias
    float wr_post_diag_c_min = (winrate_defense_mode == "STRICT" ? 0.60 : 0.56) + total_bias
    float wr_post_spread_min = (winrate_defense_mode == "STRICT" ? 0.03 : 0.01) + math.max(strict_bias, 0.0)
    float wr_wave_core_min = (winrate_defense_mode == "STRICT" ? 0.55 : 0.50) + math.max(strict_bias, 0.0)
    float wr_wave_diag_c_min = (winrate_defense_mode == "STRICT" ? 0.58 : 0.53) + math.max(strict_bias, 0.0)
    float wr_score_core_min = (winrate_defense_mode == "STRICT" ? 22.0 : 16.0) + (quality_master_mode == "ULTRA" ? 4.0 : quality_master_mode == "BALANCED" ? -2.0 : 0.0)
    float wr_score_diag_c_min = (winrate_defense_mode == "STRICT" ? 28.0 : 20.0) + (quality_master_mode == "ULTRA" ? 5.0 : quality_master_mode == "BALANCED" ? -2.0 : 0.0)
    wr_post_core_min := f_clamp(wr_post_core_min, 0.52, 0.75)
    wr_post_diag_c_min := f_clamp(wr_post_diag_c_min, 0.54, 0.80)
    wr_post_spread_min := f_clamp(wr_post_spread_min, 0.01, 0.08)
    wr_wave_core_min := f_clamp(wr_wave_core_min, 0.50, 0.80)
    wr_wave_diag_c_min := f_clamp(wr_wave_diag_c_min, 0.52, 0.85)
    bool wr_core_long_ok =
         zz_post_prob_long >= wr_post_core_min and
         (zz_post_prob_long - zz_post_prob_short) >= wr_post_spread_min and
         zz_wave_conf >= wr_wave_core_min and
         score >= wr_score_core_min and
         trend_state != "bear"
    bool wr_core_short_ok =
         zz_post_prob_short >= wr_post_core_min and
         (zz_post_prob_short - zz_post_prob_long) >= wr_post_spread_min and
         zz_wave_conf >= wr_wave_core_min and
         score <= -wr_score_core_min and
         trend_state != "bull"
    bool wr_diag_c_long_ok =
         zz_post_prob_long >= wr_post_diag_c_min and
         (zz_post_prob_long - zz_post_prob_short) >= wr_post_spread_min and
         zz_wave_conf >= wr_wave_diag_c_min and
         score >= wr_score_diag_c_min and
         trend_state != "bear"
    bool wr_diag_c_short_ok =
         zz_post_prob_short >= wr_post_diag_c_min and
         (zz_post_prob_short - zz_post_prob_long) >= wr_post_spread_min and
         zz_wave_conf >= wr_wave_diag_c_min and
         score <= -wr_score_diag_c_min and
         trend_state != "bull"
    early_stage_long := early_stage_long and wr_core_long_ok
    early_stage_short := early_stage_short and wr_core_short_ok
    core_long_cond := core_long_cond and wr_core_long_ok
    core_short_cond := core_short_cond and wr_core_short_ok
    diag_b_long := diag_b_long and wr_diag_c_long_ok
    diag_b_short := diag_b_short and wr_diag_c_short_ok
    diag_c_long_cond := diag_c_long_cond and wr_diag_c_long_ok
    diag_c_short_cond := diag_c_short_cond and wr_diag_c_short_ok
// [PATCH-67] noncoin legacy mask path removed with Binance-only runtime cleanup.
// [ZZ_FUSION_END]
///────────────────────────────────────
// 20. 액션 텍스트 (TIER와 분리된 축)  [v5.0-RealAlign]
// - 최종 사용 액션: "관망", "매수 준비", "분할 매수", "강력 매수",
//                   "홀딩", "분할 매도", "다 팔아"

//────────────────────────────────────
string action_txt = "관망"
// 자산군별 액션 기준 점수 (코인 / 비코인 분리)
// - 코인   : core ≥ 60, real ≥ 80 / sell은 대칭
// - 비코인 : core ≥ 55, real ≥ 75 / sell은 대칭
int core_buy_level  = is_coin ? 60 : 55
int diag_c_buy_level  = is_coin ? 80 : 75
int core_sell_level = is_coin ? -60 : -55
int diag_c_sell_level = is_coin ? -80 : -75
// 21. REAL LONG 우선: 시장이 REAL이면 최소 "분할 매수" 고정
if diag_c_long_cond
    // REAL LONG + 점수 충분 → 강력 매수
    if score >= diag_c_buy_level
        action_txt := "강력 매수"
    // REAL LONG인데 점수가 부족해도 → 최소 분할 매수 (신규 진입 확정)
    else
        action_txt := "분할 매수"
// 22. REAL SHORT: 롱 청산·비중 축소 액션
else if diag_c_short_cond
    if score <= diag_c_sell_level
        action_txt := "다 팔아"
    else if score <= core_sell_level
        action_txt := "분할 매도"
    else
        // REAL SHORT지만 점수가 약하면 과도한 청산 대신 홀딩
        action_txt := "홀딩"
else if diag_b_long
    if score >= core_buy_level
        action_txt := "분할 매수"
    else if score > 0
        action_txt := "매수 준비"
    else
        action_txt := "관망"
else if diag_b_short
    if score <= core_sell_level
        action_txt := "분할 매도"
    else if score < 0
        action_txt := "매도 준비"
    else
        action_txt := "관망"
else if core_long_cond
    if score >= core_buy_level
        // CORE LONG + 점수 OK → 분할 매수
        action_txt := "분할 매수"
    else if score > 0
        // CORE LONG이지만 점수 미달 → 매수 준비
        action_txt := "매수 준비"
    else
        action_txt := "관망"
else if core_short_cond
    if score <= core_sell_level
        action_txt := "분할 매도"
    else if score < 0
        action_txt := "매도 준비"
    else
        action_txt := "관망"
// 25. CORE / REAL 모두 꺼져 있을 때: 순수 환경 스코어 기반
else
    // 신호가 없을 때는 score 방향만으로 액션을 올리지 않는다.
    action_txt := "관망"
// 25. 레짐 보정: range에서만 강도 1단계 완화
//    - transition 구간에서는 LONG/SHORT 모두 강도 유지
if regime_soft
    // SHORT 액션은 range/transition 공통으로 한 단계 완화
    if action_txt == "다 팔아"
        action_txt := "분할 매도"
    // LONG 액션은 range에서만 완화 (transition에서는 그대로 유지)
    if regime_state == "range" and action_txt == "강력 매수"
        action_txt := "분할 매수"
// 26. Zero-Noise 오버라이트 (극단 점수에서 액션 끌어올리기)
//    - 신호 충돌(conflict)이 없고, 점수가 매우 높을 때만 사용
if not conflict
    if score >= 70 and (action_txt == "관망" or action_txt == "홀딩")
        action_txt := "강력 매수"
    if score <= -70 and (action_txt == "관망" or action_txt == "홀딩")
        action_txt := "다 팔아"
color score_col = color.new(color.gray, 30)
if conflict
    score_col := color.new(color.orange, 40)
else if score_panel >= diag_c_buy_level
    score_col := color.lime
else if score_panel >= core_buy_level
    score_col := color.new(color.lime, 20)
else if score_panel <= diag_c_sell_level
    score_col := color.red
else if score_panel <= core_sell_level
    score_col := color.new(color.red, 20)

//────────────────────────────────────
// 27. SS 전략 모드
//────────────────────────────────────
string ss_strategy_auto = "리버설"
if regime_state == "trend"
    if score >= 0
        ss_strategy_auto := "리버설"
    else
        ss_strategy_auto := "브레이크아웃"
else
    ss_strategy_auto := "리버설"
string ss_mode = ss_strategy == "AUTO" ? ss_strategy_auto : ss_strategy

//────────────────────────────────────
// 28. Super Swing Strict (SS)
//────────────────────────────────────
float ss_rsi        = ta.rsi(close, ss_rsi_len)
bool  ss_vol_ok     = volume > vol_ma * ss_vol_mult
bool  ss_env_rev_ok = regime_state != "range"
bool  ss_env_bo_ok  = regime_state == "trend"
bool ss_rev_long_raw =
     enable_ss and ss_vol_ok and ss_env_rev_ok and
     ss_rsi < 35 and cross_kd_up
bool ss_rev_short_raw =
     enable_ss and ss_vol_ok and ss_env_rev_ok and
     ss_rsi > 65 and cross_kd_down
bool ss_bo_long_raw =
     enable_ss and ss_vol_ok and ss_env_bo_ok and
     ss_rsi > 55 and cross_kd_up
bool ss_bo_short_raw =
     enable_ss and ss_vol_ok and ss_env_bo_ok and
     ss_rsi < 45 and cross_kd_down
bool ss_long_raw =
     ss_mode == "리버설"       ? ss_rev_long_raw  :
     ss_mode == "브레이크아웃" ? ss_bo_long_raw   :
                                 ss_rev_long_raw
bool ss_short_raw =
     ss_mode == "리버설"       ? ss_rev_short_raw :
     ss_mode == "브레이크아웃" ? ss_bo_short_raw  :
                                 ss_rev_short_raw
var int last_ss_long_bar  = na
var int last_ss_short_bar = na
bool ss_long_gap_ok  = na(last_ss_long_bar)  or bar_index - last_ss_long_bar  > gap_ss_min
bool ss_short_gap_ok = na(last_ss_short_bar) or bar_index - last_ss_short_bar > gap_ss_min
bool bf_ss_long_ok  = not bf_enable_eff or score >= bf_ss_score_min
bool bf_ss_short_ok = not bf_enable_eff or score <= -bf_ss_score_min
bool ss_long_fire  = ss_long_raw  and ss_long_gap_ok and bf_ss_long_ok  and bf_minor_trend_ok
bool ss_short_fire = ss_short_raw and ss_short_gap_ok and bf_ss_short_ok and bf_minor_trend_ok
if is_stock and not stock_session_ok
    ss_long_fire := false
    ss_short_fire := false
if stock_long_only
    ss_short_fire := false
if ss_long_fire
    last_ss_long_bar := bar_index
if ss_short_fire
    last_ss_short_bar := bar_index
bool ssr_long  = false
bool ssr_short = false

//────────────────────────────────────
// 29. 김치 프리미엄 / Fake Pump
//────────────────────────────────────
float kimchi_prem     = 0.0
float kimchi_alt_prem = na
bool is_upbit_krw_coin = is_upbit and (syminfo.type == "crypto") and str.endswith(symbol_only, "KRW")
string base_raw   = str.replace(symbol_only, "KRW", "")
bool   base_ok    = str.length(base_raw) >= 2 and base_raw != "USDT" and base_raw != "KRW"
bool   base_major = is_upbit_krw_coin and base_ok and is_major
string binance_base = base_major ? base_raw : "BTC"
string binance_sym  = "BINANCE:" + binance_base + "USDT"
float binance_coin = request.security(binance_sym, timeframe.period, close, gaps = barmerge.gaps_off, lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
float binance_krw  = binance_coin * kimchi_usdt_krw
if is_upbit_krw_coin and enable_kimchi_eff and base_major and binance_krw > 0
    float upbit_krw = close
    kimchi_alt_prem := (upbit_krw - binance_krw) / binance_krw * 100.0
else
    kimchi_alt_prem := na
if is_upbit_krw_coin and enable_kimchi_eff and not na(kimchi_alt_prem)
    kimchi_prem := kimchi_alt_prem
else
    kimchi_prem := 0.0
float kimchi_high_thr_final = kimchi_high_thr
float kimchi_low_thr_final  = kimchi_low_thr
if is_upbit_krw_coin and is_alt
    kimchi_high_thr_final := kimchi_high_thr_final * 1.2
bool kimchi_extreme = false
if is_upbit_krw_coin and enable_kimchi_eff
    if kimchi_prem >= kimchi_high_thr_final or kimchi_prem <= kimchi_low_thr_final
        kimchi_extreme := true
float body_len       = math.abs(close - open)
float upper_wick_len = high - math.max(open, close)
bool cond1           = upper_wick_len >= body_len * 3.0
bool cond2           = upper_wick_len >= ta.atr(14) * 2.0
bool long_upper_wick_extreme = cond1 and cond2 and close < open
bool fake_pump_cond =
     is_upbit_krw_coin and enable_kimchi_eff and kimchi_prem >= kimchi_high_thr_final and
     vol_ultra and long_upper_wick_extreme and close < open
bool kimchi_extreme_pulse = kimchi_extreme and not kimchi_extreme[1]
bool fake_pump_pulse      = fake_pump_cond and not fake_pump_cond[1]
bool kimchi_risk_off_high = is_upbit_krw_coin and enable_kimchi_eff and kimchi_prem >= kimchi_high_thr_final and kimchi_extreme
bool kimchi_risk_off_low  = is_upbit_krw_coin and enable_kimchi_eff and kimchi_prem <= kimchi_low_thr_final  and kimchi_extreme
if kimchi_risk_off_high
    diag_c_long_cond  := false
    core_long_cond  := core_long_cond and score > core_buy_level + 10.0
if kimchi_risk_off_low
    diag_c_short_cond := false
    core_short_cond := core_short_cond and score < core_sell_level - 10.0

//────────────────────────────────────
// 30. 감정 신호 / 볼륨 아이콘 간격
//────────────────────────────────────
big_up_move   = close > close[1] * 1.003
big_down_move = close < close[1] * 0.997
var int last_heart_bar = na
var int last_vol_bar   = na
heart_ready = na(last_heart_bar) or bar_index - last_heart_bar > gap_heart_min
vol_ready   = na(last_vol_bar)   or bar_index - last_vol_bar   > vol_gap
trend_flip_up   = trend_state == "bull" and trend_state[1] != "bull"
trend_flip_down = trend_state == "bear" and trend_state[1] != "bear"
score_flip_up   = score > 0 and score_prev <= 0
score_flip_down = score < 0 and score_prev >= 0
heart_trend_up =
     (regime_state == "transition" and trend_state != "bear") or
     trend_flip_up or
     score_flip_up
heart_trend_down =
     (regime_state == "transition" and trend_state != "bull") or
     trend_flip_down or
     score_flip_down
bool heart_stoch_up =
     cross_kd_up or
     (k_val > d_val and k_val > nz(k_val[1]) and k_val < heart_k_limit + 12)
bool heart_stoch_down =
     cross_kd_down or
     (k_val < d_val and k_val < nz(k_val[1]) and k_val > heart_k_limit - 12)
heart_long_cond =
     tf_show_heart_vis and show_heart_long and heart_ready and heart_trend_up and
     (vol_strong or vol_ultra) and (big_up_move or score >= 8) and
     heart_stoch_up
heart_short_cond =
     tf_show_heart_vis and show_heart_short and heart_ready and heart_trend_down and
     (vol_strong or vol_ultra) and (big_down_move or score <= -8) and
     heart_stoch_down
if heart_long_cond or heart_short_cond
    last_heart_bar := bar_index
vol_weak_significant = vol_weak and volume < vol_ma * 0.4
float emo_th = bf_enable_eff ? math.max(10.0, float(bf_emo_score_min)) : 10.0
bool emo_session_ok = true
if vz_state == "low"
    emo_session_ok := false
if (is_15m or is_1d) and amc_preset == "슈퍼보수"
    emo_session_ok := false
if is_stock and not stock_session_ok
    emo_session_ok := false
bool emo_post_long_ok = zz_post_prob_long >= 0.52
bool emo_post_short_ok = zz_post_prob_short >= 0.52
bool emo_wave_ok = zz_wave_conf >= 0.50
bool emo_trend_up = trend_state != "bear" or score_flip_up
bool emo_trend_down = trend_state != "bull" or score_flip_down
bool emo_long  = emo_session_ok and score >= math.min(emo_th, 8.0) and emo_trend_up and bf_minor_trend_ok and math.abs(score) >= 8.0 and emo_post_long_ok and emo_wave_ok and not early_long and not core_long_cond and not diag_b_long and not diag_c_long_cond
bool emo_short = emo_session_ok and score <= -math.min(emo_th, 8.0) and emo_trend_down and bf_minor_trend_ok and math.abs(score) >= 8.0 and emo_post_short_ok and emo_wave_ok and not early_short and not core_short_cond and not diag_b_short and not diag_c_short_cond
if stock_long_only
    emo_short := false

//────────────────────────────────────
// 31. BG 레이어
//────────────────────────────────────
color trend_col = color.new(color.gray, 97)
if trend_state == "bull"
    trend_col := color.new(color.lime, 94)
else if trend_state == "bear"
    trend_col := color.new(color.red, 94)
color htf_col = color.new(color.gray, 98)
if htf_state == "bull"
    htf_col := color.new(color.lime, 96)
else if htf_state == "bear"
    htf_col := color.new(color.red, 96)
color bw_zone_bg = na
if close > bw2_upper or close < bw2_lower
    bw_zone_bg := color.new(color.red, 88)
else if close > bw1_upper or close < bw1_lower
    bw_zone_bg := color.new(color.orange, 92)
color diag_c_bg = na
if diag_c_long_cond
    diag_c_bg := color.new(color.lime, 92)
else if diag_c_short_cond
    diag_c_bg := color.new(color.red, 92)
color bg_color = na
if is_1d
    if bg_show_bigtrend
        bg_color := trend_col
else if is_4h
    if is_coin
        if bg_show_bigtrend
            bg_color := trend_col
        if bg_show_bw_zone and not na(bw_zone_bg)
            bg_color := bw_zone_bg
        if bg_show_vz and not na(vz_bg)
            bg_color := vz_bg
        if bg_show_vol_zone and not na(vol_bg)
            bg_color := vol_bg
        if bg_show_diag_c_ls and not na(diag_c_bg)
            bg_color := diag_c_bg
    else
        if bg_show_bigtrend
            bg_color := trend_col
        if bg_show_diag_c_ls and not na(diag_c_bg)
            bg_color := diag_c_bg
else if is_1h
    if bg_show_bw_zone and not na(bw_zone_bg)
        bg_color := bw_zone_bg
    if bg_show_diag_c_ls and not na(diag_c_bg)
        bg_color := diag_c_bg
else if is_15m
    bg_color := na
else
    if bg_show_bigtrend
        bg_color := trend_col
    if bg_show_htf and not na(htf_col)
        bg_color := htf_col
    if bg_show_bw_zone and not na(bw_zone_bg)
        bg_color := bw_zone_bg
    if bg_show_vz and not na(vz_bg)
        bg_color := vz_bg
    if bg_show_vol_zone and not na(vol_bg)
        bg_color := vol_bg
    if bg_show_diag_c_ls and not na(diag_c_bg)
        bg_color := diag_c_bg
bgcolor(bg_color)

//────────────────────────────────────
// 32. Breakwater / Ichimoku Plot
//────────────────────────────────────
plot(show_breakwater ? bw0_upper : na, "BW0 Upper", color = color.new(color.gray,   60), style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(show_breakwater ? bw0_lower : na, "BW0 Lower", color = color.new(color.gray,   60), style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(show_breakwater ? bw1_upper : na, "BW1 Upper", color = color.new(color.yellow, 50), style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(show_breakwater ? bw1_lower : na, "BW1 Lower", color = color.new(color.yellow, 50), style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(show_breakwater ? bw2_upper : na, "BW2 Upper", color = color.new(color.red,    60), style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(show_breakwater ? bw2_lower : na, "BW2 Lower", color = color.new(color.red,    60), style = plot.style_linebr)
// Ichimoku 구름 (UI+ Refined)
span_a_plot = show_ichi_cloud ? span_a[ichi_displace] : na
span_b_plot = show_ichi_cloud ? span_b[ichi_displace] : na
// UI+ 팔레트 (OFF면 기존 컬러 유지)
color ichi_bull_col = ichi_ui_enable ? ichi_ui_col_bull : color.teal
color ichi_bear_col = ichi_ui_enable ? ichi_ui_col_bear : color.purple
color ichi_ten_col  = ichi_ui_enable ? ichi_ui_col_tenkan : color.new(color.blue, 0)
color ichi_kij_col  = ichi_ui_enable ? ichi_ui_col_kijun  : color.new(color.red, 0)
// (Bell/필터에서 재사용)
float kumo_top = (not na(span_a_plot) and not na(span_b_plot)) ? math.max(span_a_plot, span_b_plot) : na
float kumo_bot = (not na(span_a_plot) and not na(span_b_plot)) ? math.min(span_a_plot, span_b_plot) : na
float kumo_thk = (not na(span_a_plot) and not na(span_b_plot)) ? math.abs(span_a_plot - span_b_plot) : na
// 구름 투명도: 두께 기반 자동(옵션)
int ichi_cloud_tr = ichi_ui_enable ? ichi_ui_cloud_tr : 90
float ichi_ui_atr = ta.atr(ichi_ui_thick_atr_len)
if ichi_ui_enable and ichi_ui_cloud_tr_auto and not na(kumo_thk)
    float ichi_cloud_thk_norm = ichi_ui_atr > 0 ? math.min(1.0, kumo_thk / (ichi_ui_atr * ichi_ui_thick_scale)) : 0.0
    // thick_norm=0 → 약(투명), thick_norm=1 → 강(진함)
    ichi_cloud_tr := int(math.round(ichi_ui_cloud_tr - ichi_cloud_thk_norm * (ichi_ui_cloud_tr - ichi_ui_cloud_tr_strong)))
    ichi_cloud_tr := math.max(0, math.min(100, ichi_cloud_tr))
// 경계선/스팬선
int   ichi_edge_tr  = ichi_ui_enable ? ichi_ui_edge_tr : 80
color ichi_edge_src = ichi_ui_enable ? ichi_ui_col_edge : color.gray
bool  ichi_show_edge = ichi_ui_edge_on
bool  ichi_show_span = ichi_ui_show_span_lines
int   ichi_ln_tr = (ichi_show_edge or ichi_show_span) ? ichi_edge_tr : 100
color ichi_ln_col = color.new(ichi_edge_src, ichi_ln_tr)
pa = plot(span_a_plot, "Span A", color = ichi_ln_col, linewidth = ichi_ui_w_span)
pb = plot(span_b_plot, "Span B", color = ichi_ln_col, linewidth = ichi_ui_w_span)
// 구름 채움
color fill_color = na
if show_ichi_cloud and ichi_ui_show_cloud_fill and not na(span_a_plot) and not na(span_b_plot)
    fill_color := span_a_plot > span_b_plot ? color.new(ichi_bull_col, ichi_cloud_tr) : color.new(ichi_bear_col, ichi_cloud_tr)
fill(pa, pb, color = fill_color)
// 전환/기준선 라인 (옵션)
plot(show_ichi_cloud and ichi_ui_show_lines ? conversion_line : na,
     "Tenkan", color = ichi_ten_col, linewidth = ichi_ui_w_main)
plot(show_ichi_cloud and ichi_ui_show_lines ? base_line : na,
     "Kijun",  color = ichi_kij_col, linewidth = ichi_ui_w_main)
// 후행스팬 (옵션)
plot(show_ichi_cloud and ichi_ui_show_chikou ? close : na,
     "Chikou", offset = -ichi_displace,
     color = color.new(ichi_ten_col, 55), linewidth = 1)
// Kumo Twist (●)
bool ichi_twist_raw = ta.cross(span_a_plot, span_b_plot)
bool ichi_twist = ichi_ui_enable and ichi_ui_show_twist and ichi_twist_raw
plot(ichi_twist ? span_a_plot : na, "Kumo Twist", style = plot.style_circles, linewidth = 2,
     color = color.new(ichi_edge_src, 10))

//────────────────────────────────────
// 🔔 Ichi Bell (전환선↔기준선) + 하이라이트 BG (동기화)
//────────────────────────────────────
bool ichi_cross_up_raw = ta.crossover(conversion_line, base_line)
bool ichi_cross_dn_raw = ta.crossunder(conversion_line, base_line)
bool ichi_cross_up = show_ichi_cloud and ichi_bell_enable and ichi_cross_up_raw
bool ichi_cross_dn = show_ichi_cloud and ichi_bell_enable and ichi_cross_dn_raw
bool ichi_kumo_long_ok  = not ichi_bell_kumo_filter or (not na(kumo_top) and close > kumo_top)
bool ichi_kumo_short_ok = not ichi_bell_kumo_filter or (not na(kumo_bot) and close < kumo_bot)
var int ichi_bell_last_bar = na
bool ichi_bell_gap_ok = ichi_bell_gap <= 0 ? true : (na(ichi_bell_last_bar) ? true : (bar_index - ichi_bell_last_bar >= ichi_bell_gap))

//────────────────────────────────────
// 🔔 통합 신뢰도: (일목 강도) × (돈벌자 엔진 score/CORE/REAL)
// - 엔진 신뢰도(0~1): score가 CORE/REAL 레벨을 얼마나 넘는지 + REAL/CORE cond tier 가중
// - 최종 강도: strength_raw × ((1-w)+w×engine_conf)

//────────────────────────────────────
bool ichi_bell_long_pre  = ichi_cross_up and ichi_kumo_long_ok  and ichi_bell_gap_ok
bool ichi_bell_short_pre = ichi_cross_dn and ichi_kumo_short_ok and ichi_bell_gap_ok
// 강도(0~1): 구름 두께 + 구름 이탈 거리(ATR 정규화)
float _atr = atr_val
float thick_norm = (not na(kumo_thk) and _atr > 0) ? math.min(1.0, kumo_thk / (_atr * 2.0)) : 0.0
float dist_norm_long  = (not na(kumo_top) and _atr > 0) ? math.min(1.0, math.max(0.0, (close - kumo_top) / (_atr * 2.0))) : 0.0
float dist_norm_short = (not na(kumo_bot) and _atr > 0) ? math.min(1.0, math.max(0.0, (kumo_bot - close) / (_atr * 2.0))) : 0.0
float strength_raw_long  = ichi_bell_long_pre  ? (0.35 * thick_norm + 0.65 * dist_norm_long)  : na
float strength_raw_short = ichi_bell_short_pre ? (0.35 * thick_norm + 0.65 * dist_norm_short) : na
// 돈벌자 엔진 신뢰도(0~1): score가 CORE/REAL 레벨을 얼마나 넘는지
float eng_score_long = 0.0
if score >= diag_c_buy_level
    eng_score_long := 1.0
else if score >= core_buy_level
    eng_score_long := 0.70 + 0.30 * (score - core_buy_level) / math.max(1.0, (diag_c_buy_level - core_buy_level))
else if score > 0
    eng_score_long := 0.70 * (score / core_buy_level)
float eng_score_short = 0.0
float _core_sell_abs = math.abs(float(core_sell_level))
if score <= diag_c_sell_level
    eng_score_short := 1.0
else if score <= core_sell_level
    eng_score_short := 0.70 + 0.30 * (core_sell_level - score) / math.max(1.0, (core_sell_level - diag_c_sell_level))
else if score < 0 and _core_sell_abs > 0
    eng_score_short := 0.70 * (math.abs(score) / _core_sell_abs)
float eng_tier_long  = diag_c_long_cond  ? 1.0 : core_long_cond  ? 0.85 : 0.55
float eng_tier_short = diag_c_short_cond ? 1.0 : core_short_cond ? 0.85 : 0.55
float eng_conf_long  = math.max(0.0, math.min(1.0, eng_score_long  * eng_tier_long))
float eng_conf_short = math.max(0.0, math.min(1.0, eng_score_short * eng_tier_short))
// 결합: w=0 → 일목 강도만 / w=1 → 일목 강도×엔진 신뢰도
float mix_long  = ichi_bell_merge_engine ? ((1.0 - ichi_bell_engine_w) + ichi_bell_engine_w * eng_conf_long)  : 1.0
float mix_short = ichi_bell_merge_engine ? ((1.0 - ichi_bell_engine_w) + ichi_bell_engine_w * eng_conf_short) : 1.0
float strength_long  = not na(strength_raw_long)  ? (strength_raw_long  * mix_long)  : na
float strength_short = not na(strength_raw_short) ? (strength_raw_short * mix_short) : na
bool ichi_bell_long  = ichi_bell_long_pre  and not na(strength_long)  and strength_long  >= ichi_bell_min_conf
bool ichi_bell_short = ichi_bell_short_pre and not na(strength_short) and strength_short >= ichi_bell_min_conf
float strength_now = ichi_bell_long ? strength_long :
                     ichi_bell_short ? strength_short : na
int tr_now = not na(strength_now) ? int(math.round(80 - 60 * strength_now)) : 100
tr_now := math.max(0, math.min(100, tr_now))
// 마지막 신호 스냅샷(하이라이트 BG 동기화용)
var float ichi_bell_last_strength = 0.0
var int   ichi_bell_last_tr       = 90
var int   ichi_bell_last_dir      = 0
if ichi_bell_long or ichi_bell_short
    ichi_bell_last_bar := bar_index
    ichi_bell_last_strength := strength_now
    ichi_bell_last_tr := tr_now
    ichi_bell_last_dir := ichi_bell_long ? 1 : -1
// 🔔 라벨(배경 없음) + 강도에 따라 크기/투명도 자동
if ichi_bell_long or ichi_bell_short
    color bell_col = ichi_bell_long ? ichi_bull_col : ichi_bear_col
    color bell_txt = color.new(bell_col, tr_now)
    float bell_off = _atr * (0.15 + 0.25 * strength_now)
    float bell_y   = ichi_bell_long ? (low - bell_off) : (high + bell_off)
    if strength_now >= 0.75
        label lb = label.new(bar_index, bell_y, "🔔",
             xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price,
             style = label.style_none, textcolor = bell_txt,
             size = size.large)
        f_ichi_bell_push(lb)
    else if strength_now >= 0.50
        label lb = label.new(bar_index, bell_y, "🔔",
             xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price,
             style = label.style_none, textcolor = bell_txt,
             size = size.normal)
        f_ichi_bell_push(lb)
    else if strength_now >= 0.25
        label lb = label.new(bar_index, bell_y, "🔔",
             xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price,
             style = label.style_none, textcolor = bell_txt,
             size = size.small)
        f_ichi_bell_push(lb)
    else
        label lb = label.new(bar_index, bell_y, "🔔",
             xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price,
             style = label.style_none, textcolor = bell_txt,
             size = size.tiny)
        f_ichi_bell_push(lb)
// 🔔 BG 하이라이트 (신호 bar부터 N bar, 🔔와 동기화)
bool ichi_bell_hl_active = bg_show_ichi_bell_hl and ichi_bell_enable and not na(ichi_bell_last_bar) and (bar_index - ichi_bell_last_bar < ichi_bell_hl_len)
color ichi_bell_hl_col = na
if ichi_bell_hl_active
    color _c = ichi_bell_last_dir == 1 ? ichi_bull_col : ichi_bear_col
    ichi_bell_hl_col := color.new(_c, ichi_bell_last_tr)
// BG: 🔔 하이라이트는 '최종 BG'에서만 덮어씀 (기본 BG 유지)
color ichi_bg_final = not na(ichi_bell_hl_col) ? ichi_bell_hl_col : bg_color
bgcolor(ichi_bg_final)
// 🔔 alert pulse
bool ichi_bell_long_pulse  = ichi_bell_long  and not ichi_bell_long[1]
bool ichi_bell_short_pulse = ichi_bell_short and not ichi_bell_short[1]

//────────────────────────────────────
// 26-1) REAL/CORE 최근 bar 인덱스
//────────────────────────────────────
var int last_diag_c_long_bar  = na
var int last_diag_c_short_bar = na
var int last_core_long_bar  = na
var int last_core_short_bar = na
var int last_diag_b_long_bar  = na
var int last_diag_b_short_bar = na

//────────────────────────────────────
// 33. REAL/CORE Gap 제어
//────────────────────────────────────
var int tier_dir   = 0
var int tier_phase = 0
var int tier_idle  = 0
float gap_scale = 1.0
if atr_ratio > 1.5
    gap_scale := 1.3
else if atr_ratio < 0.8
    gap_scale := 0.8
if is_binance_fut and cost_shield_enable
    gap_scale *= cost_shield_gap_mult
float gap_tf_scale = is_15m ? 4.0 : is_30m ? 2.0 : 1.0
int gap_diag_c_dyn = int(math.round(gap_diag_c_min * gap_scale * gap_tf_scale))
int gap_core_dyn = int(math.round(gap_core_min * gap_scale * gap_tf_scale))
int gap_diag_b_dyn = int(math.round((gap_core_dyn + gap_diag_c_dyn) * 0.5))
bool diag_c_long_ready  = na(last_diag_c_long_bar)  or bar_index - last_diag_c_long_bar  > gap_diag_c_dyn
bool diag_c_short_ready = na(last_diag_c_short_bar) or bar_index - last_diag_c_short_bar > gap_diag_c_dyn
bool core_long_ready  = na(last_core_long_bar)  or bar_index - last_core_long_bar  > gap_core_dyn
bool core_short_ready = na(last_core_short_bar) or bar_index - last_core_short_bar > gap_core_dyn
bool diag_b_long_ready  = na(last_diag_b_long_bar)  or bar_index - last_diag_b_long_bar  > gap_diag_b_dyn
bool diag_b_short_ready = na(last_diag_b_short_bar) or bar_index - last_diag_b_short_bar > gap_diag_b_dyn
bool diag_c_long_fire  = diag_c_long_cond  and diag_c_long_ready
bool diag_c_short_fire = diag_c_short_cond and diag_c_short_ready
bool core_long_fire  = core_long_cond  and core_long_ready
bool core_short_fire = core_short_cond and core_short_ready
bool diag_b_long_fire  = diag_b_long  and diag_b_long_ready
bool diag_b_short_fire = diag_b_short and diag_b_short_ready
//────────────────────────────────────
// RS 이후 관망 쿨다운 설정
//────────────────────────────────────
var int rs_cool_bar = na
// 몇 바 동안 관망으로 묶을지 (기본 3바, 필요하면 2~5로 조절)
int rs_cool_len = input.int(1, "RS 이후 관망 바 수", minval = 1, maxval = 20, group = grp_dev)
int rs_cool_len_bf = input.int(5, "RS 이후 관망 바 수 (바이낸스 선물)", minval = 5, maxval = 30, group = grp_dev)
bool rs_block_until_score_pos = input.bool(false, "RS 이후 score>0 전환까지 LONG 차단(바이낸스 선물)", group = grp_dev)
bool rs_require_trend_bull = input.bool(false, "RS 이후 Trend/HTF 회복 필요(바이낸스 선물)", group = grp_dev)
bool rs_use_posterior_gate = input.bool(false, "RS 이후 Posterior 회복 허용(바이낸스 선물)", group = grp_dev)
float rs_post_thr = input.float(0.55, "RS Posterior 회복 임계값", minval = 0.50, maxval = 0.80, step = 0.01, group = grp_dev)
if barstate.isconfirmed and diag_c_short_fire
    rs_cool_bar := bar_index
int rs_cool_len_eff = is_binance_fut ? rs_cool_len_bf : rs_cool_len
float rs_cool_scale = atr_ratio > 1.2 ? 1.3 : atr_ratio < 0.8 ? 0.8 : 1.0
int rs_cool_len_dyn = int(math.round(rs_cool_len_eff * rs_cool_scale))
rs_cool_len_dyn := math.max(1, rs_cool_len_dyn)
bool is_rs_cool = not na(rs_cool_bar) and (bar_index - rs_cool_bar <= rs_cool_len_dyn)
bool rs_trend_ok = not rs_require_trend_bull or (trend_state == "bull" or htf_state == "bull")
bool rs_release_score = score > 0 and rs_trend_ok
bool rs_release_posterior = rs_use_posterior_gate and zz_post_prob_long >= rs_post_thr
bool rs_release = rs_release_score or rs_release_posterior
bool rs_score_block = rs_block_until_score_pos and is_binance_fut and not na(rs_cool_bar) and not rs_release
bool rs_long_block = is_binance_fut and (is_rs_cool or rs_score_block)
if rs_long_block
    action_txt := "관망"
    diag_c_long_cond := false
    core_long_cond := false
    diag_b_long := false
    early_stage_long := false
    early_long := false
    emo_long := false
    ss_long_fire := false
    diag_c_long_fire := false
    core_long_fire := false
    diag_b_long_fire := false
if is_bnf_intraday
    if core_long_fire or diag_b_long_fire or diag_c_long_fire
        _p54_last_long_bar := bar_index
    if core_short_fire or diag_b_short_fire or diag_c_short_fire
        _p54_last_short_bar := bar_index
if barstate.isconfirmed and diag_c_long_fire
    last_diag_c_long_bar := bar_index
if barstate.isconfirmed and diag_c_short_fire
    last_diag_c_short_bar := bar_index
if barstate.isconfirmed and core_long_fire
    last_core_long_bar := bar_index
if barstate.isconfirmed and core_short_fire
    last_core_short_bar := bar_index
if barstate.isconfirmed and diag_b_long_fire
    last_diag_b_long_bar := bar_index
if barstate.isconfirmed and diag_b_short_fire
    last_diag_b_short_bar := bar_index

//────────────────────────────────────
// SL/TP 라인(표시용) - REAL 진입 기준
//────────────────────────────────────
var float sltp_entry = na
var float sltp_sl    = na
var float sltp_tp    = na
var int   sltp_dir   = 0  // 1=long, -1=short
if sltp_enable
    if diag_c_long_fire
        sltp_entry := close
        sltp_sl := close - atr_val * sltp_atr_mult_sl
        sltp_tp := close + atr_val * sltp_atr_mult_tp
        sltp_dir := 1
    else if diag_c_short_fire
        sltp_entry := close
        sltp_sl := close + atr_val * sltp_atr_mult_sl
        sltp_tp := close - atr_val * sltp_atr_mult_tp
        sltp_dir := -1
    // SL/TP 도달 시 리셋
    if sltp_dir == 1 and (close <= sltp_sl or close >= sltp_tp)
        sltp_dir := 0
    if sltp_dir == -1 and (close >= sltp_sl or close <= sltp_tp)
        sltp_dir := 0
else
    sltp_dir := 0
// 현재 바 기준 방향/단계 산출
int current_dir = 0
if diag_c_long_cond or core_long_cond or early_long or emo_long
    current_dir := 1
else if diag_c_short_cond or core_short_cond or early_short or emo_short
    current_dir := -1
int current_phase = 0
if current_dir == 1
    if diag_c_long_cond
        current_phase := 3
    else if core_long_cond
        current_phase := 2
    else if early_long
        current_phase := 1
    else if emo_long
        current_phase := 0
else if current_dir == -1
    if diag_c_short_cond
        current_phase := 3
    else if core_short_cond
        current_phase := 2
    else if early_short
        current_phase := 1
    else if emo_short
        current_phase := 0
// Tier 제한 (바닥/고점 위치 반영)
// 1) 고점(high zone)에서는 Core/Real 금지
if top_prob > 0.7 and current_dir == 1 and current_phase > 1
    current_phase := 1
// 2) 가격이 너무 위(zz_loc > 0.8)면 롱 티어 자체를 막음
if zz_loc > 0.8 and current_dir == 1 and current_phase > 0
    current_phase := 0
// 3) 바닥(bottom zone)에서는 롱 쪽 최소 Core 이상으로 유도
if bottom_prob > 0.7 and current_dir == 1 and current_phase < 2
    current_phase := 2
// [TIER_POSTERIOR_START] – Posterior / Wave 기반 티어 완화
// Posterior가 0.45~0.55 구간이거나 Wave 충돌 시, Core/Real → Early로 다운그레이드
bool zz_posterior_soft = zz_post_prob_long > 0.45 and zz_post_prob_long < 0.55
bool tier_soft_zone    = zz_posterior_soft or zz_conflict_long or zz_conflict_short
if tier_soft_zone and current_phase >= 2
    current_phase := 1
// Posterior 방향이 강할 때만 반대 방향 Core 신호로 티어 방향 전환 허용
bool posterior_strong_long  = zz_post_prob_long >= 0.6
bool posterior_strong_short = zz_post_prob_long <= 0.4
bool opposite_core_signal =
     (tier_dir == 1  and current_dir == -1 and current_phase >= 2 and posterior_strong_short) or
     (tier_dir == -1 and current_dir == 1  and current_phase >= 2 and posterior_strong_long)
// [TIER_POSTERIOR_END]
// 티어 상태머신 업데이트 (명시적 함수화)
f_tier_transition(int _tier_dir, int _tier_phase, int _tier_idle, int _current_dir, int _current_phase, bool _opposite_core_signal, int _tier_reset_bars) =>
    int new_dir = _tier_dir
    int new_phase = _tier_phase
    int new_idle = _tier_idle
    if _opposite_core_signal
        new_dir := _current_dir
        new_phase := _current_phase
        new_idle := 0
    else if _current_dir == _tier_dir and _current_dir != 0
        new_phase := math.max(_tier_phase, _current_phase)
        new_idle := 0
    else if _tier_dir == 0 and _current_dir != 0
        new_dir := _current_dir
        new_phase := _current_phase
        new_idle := 0
    else if _current_dir == 0
        new_idle += 1
    if _current_dir == 0 and new_dir != 0 and new_idle >= _tier_reset_bars
        new_dir := 0
        new_phase := 0
        new_idle := 0
    [new_dir, new_phase, new_idle]
int tier_reset_bars = (is_1d or is_1w) ? 2 : 3
[new_tier_dir, new_tier_phase, new_tier_idle] = f_tier_transition(
    tier_dir, tier_phase, tier_idle,
    current_dir, current_phase,
    opposite_core_signal,
    tier_reset_bars
)
tier_dir := new_tier_dir
tier_phase := new_tier_phase
tier_idle := new_tier_idle
bool show_emo_long_sm    = tier_dir == 1  and tier_phase == 0 and emo_long
bool show_early_long_sm  = tier_dir == 1  and tier_phase == 1 and early_long
bool show_core_long_sm   = tier_dir == 1  and tier_phase == 2 and core_long_fire
bool show_diag_c_long_sm   = tier_dir == 1  and tier_phase == 3 and diag_c_long_fire
bool show_emo_short_sm   = tier_dir == -1 and tier_phase == 0 and emo_short
bool show_early_short_sm = tier_dir == -1 and tier_phase == 1 and early_short
bool show_core_short_sm  = tier_dir == -1 and tier_phase == 2 and core_short_fire
bool show_diag_c_short_sm  = tier_dir == -1 and tier_phase == 3 and diag_c_short_fire

//────────────────────────────────────
// 34. TD9 PRIME / REAL / CORE Plot
//────────────────────────────────────
td9_buy_ready  = low  <= ta.lowest(low, 2)[1]
td9_sell_ready = high >= ta.highest(high, 2)[1]
bool td9p_stoch_req = td9p_use_stoch or (bf_enable_eff and bf_td9_force_stoch)
bool bf_td9_long_ok  = not bf_enable_eff or score >= bf_td9_score_min
bool bf_td9_short_ok = not bf_enable_eff or score <= -bf_td9_score_min
bool td9p_buy_fire  = tf_show_td9p and tf_show_td9 and (td_buy  == td_lookback) and td9_buy_ready  and (not td9p_stoch_req or k_val <= td9p_k_buy_max)  and bf_td9_long_ok  and bf_minor_trend_ok
bool td9p_sell_fire = tf_show_td9p and tf_show_td9 and (td_sell == td_lookback) and td9_sell_ready and (not td9p_stoch_req or k_val >= td9p_k_sell_min) and bf_td9_short_ok and bf_minor_trend_ok
if is_stock and not stock_session_ok
    td9p_buy_fire := false
    td9p_sell_fire := false
if stock_long_only
    td9p_sell_fire := false

var label[] g_label_queue = array.new_label()
int LABEL_MAX = 400
f_push_label(label lb) =>
    array.push(g_label_queue, lb)
    while array.size(g_label_queue) > LABEL_MAX
        label old = array.shift(g_label_queue)
        label.delete(old)

else if risk_label == "횡보 · 박스"
    if action_txt == "강력 매수"
        risk_txt := "박스 상단 추격 주의"
    else if action_txt == "분할 매수" or action_txt == "매수 준비"
        risk_txt := "박스 매매 / 짧게 대응"
    else
        risk_txt := "횡보장 / 관망 우세"
else if risk_label == "전환 주의"
    risk_txt := "추세 전환 가능성 / 포지션 줄이기"
else
    if action_txt == "강력 매수" or action_txt == "분할 매수"
        risk_txt := "무난한 환경 / 평소 비중"
    else if action_txt == "관망"
        risk_txt := "무난한 환경 / 기회 탐색"
    else if action_txt == "홀딩"
        risk_txt := "무난한 환경 / 홀딩"
    else
        risk_txt := "무난한 환경 / 비중 조절"
// SS 전략 표시 텍스트
string ss_strategy_display = ss_mode == "리버설" ? "리버설" : "브레이크아웃"
f_render_status_panel() =>
    if tf_show_status and barstate.islast
        table.clear(status_tbl, 0, 0, status_cols - 1, status_rows - 1)
        int r = 0
        table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "PRO STATUS", text_color = txt_white, bgcolor = bg_header, text_halign = text.align_center, text_size = size.small)
        table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, "", text_color = txt_white, bgcolor = bg_header, text_halign = text.align_center, text_size = size.small)

        r += 1

        r += 1

        table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, score_line, text_color = score_disp_col, bgcolor = bg_row_eff, text_size = size.small)

        table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, wave_txt, text_color = txt_white, bgcolor = bg_row_eff, text_size = size.small)

        else

            table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, asset_type_txt, text_color = txt_white, bgcolor = bg_row_eff, text_size = size.small)

        else

            table.cell(status_tbl, 0, r, "장상태", text_color = txt_lab, bgcolor = bg_row_eff, text_size = size.small)
            table.cell(status_tbl, 1, r, market_txt_local, text_color = txt_white, bgcolor = bg_row_eff, text_size = size.small)
f_render_status_panel()

// [PATCH-43] PnL5 포트폴리오 22개 input 삭제 (별도 지표로 분리)
//────────────────────────────────────
// 42. Alert 펄스 (SERVER SIGNAL)
