bool bf_gate_long  = not bf_enable_eff or bf_long_ok
bool bf_gate_short = not bf_enable_eff or bf_short_ok
// 1) 기본 pulse (v2.6.5 정렬: 상태 ON의 첫 bar를 신호로 사용)
diag_c_long_pulse  = not binance_diag_c_block_eff and diag_c_long_cond  and not diag_c_long_cond[1]
diag_c_short_pulse = not binance_diag_c_block_eff and diag_c_short_cond and not diag_c_short_cond[1]
core_long_pulse  = core_long_cond  and not core_long_cond[1]
core_short_pulse = core_short_cond and not core_short_cond[1]
diag_b_long_pulse  = not binance_diag_c_block_eff and diag_b_long  and not diag_b_long[1]
diag_b_short_pulse = not binance_diag_c_block_eff and diag_b_short and not diag_b_short[1]
var int last_early_stage_long_pulse_bar  = na
var int last_early_stage_short_pulse_bar = na
int gap_early_dyn = int(math.round(gap_early_min * gap_tf_scale))
bool early_stage_long_gap_ok  = na(last_early_stage_long_pulse_bar)  or bar_index - last_early_stage_long_pulse_bar  > gap_early_dyn
bool early_stage_short_gap_ok = na(last_early_stage_short_pulse_bar) or bar_index - last_early_stage_short_pulse_bar > gap_early_dyn
bool early_stage_long_pulse  = early_stage_long  and not early_stage_long[1]  and bf_gate_long  and early_stage_long_gap_ok
bool early_stage_short_pulse = early_stage_short and not early_stage_short[1] and bf_gate_short and early_stage_short_gap_ok
if early_stage_long_pulse
    last_early_stage_long_pulse_bar := bar_index
if early_stage_short_pulse
    last_early_stage_short_pulse_bar := bar_index
ss_long_pulse    = ss_long_fire    and not ss_long_fire[1]  and bf_gate_long
ss_short_pulse   = ss_short_fire   and not ss_short_fire[1] and bf_gate_short
td9p_buy_pulse   = td9p_buy_fire   and not td9p_buy_fire[1]  and bf_gate_long
td9p_sell_pulse  = td9p_sell_fire  and not td9p_sell_fire[1] and bf_gate_short
// 감정(🙂/😭): 이미 쿨다운/중복필터가 적용된 "emo_long/emo_short"를 그대로 pulse로 사용
emo_long_pulse  = emo_long  and not emo_long[1]  and bf_gate_long
emo_short_pulse = emo_short and not emo_short[1] and bf_gate_short
// 2) 액션 기반 전체 청산 감지
int action_code = action_txt == "다 팔아" ? 1 : 0
bool enter_all_exit = (action_code == 1) and (nz(action_code[1]) == 0)
f_early_allowed(int sigDir) =>
    if not early_core_diag_c_enable
        false
    else
        bool dir_long = sigDir > 0
        bool align_ok = (trend_state == htf_state) and (trend_state == "bull" or trend_state == "bear")
        bool regime_ok = regime_state == "trend" or regime_state == "transition"
        bool score_ok = dir_long ? (score >= early_core_diag_c_score_min) : (score <= -early_core_diag_c_score_min)
        bool conflict_ok = dir_long ? not conflict_long : not conflict_short
        float post_now = dir_long ? zz_post_prob_long : zz_post_prob_short
        float post_opp = dir_long ? zz_post_prob_short : zz_post_prob_long
        bool post_ok = post_now >= early_exec_post_min and (post_now - post_opp) >= early_exec_post_spread_min
        bool wave_ok = zz_wave_conf >= early_exec_wave_min
        align_ok and regime_ok and score_ok and conflict_ok and post_ok and wave_ok
// 2-1) 현재 라이브 경로는 BAR_CLOSE 확정 신호만 사용
float early_ratio = 0.0
var int early_core_diag_c_bar = na
var bool early_core_long_sent = false
var bool early_core_short_sent = false
var bool early_diag_c_long_sent = false
var bool early_diag_c_short_sent = false
if early_core_diag_c_bar != bar_index
    early_core_diag_c_bar := bar_index
    early_core_long_sent := false
    early_core_short_sent := false
    early_diag_c_long_sent := false
    early_diag_c_short_sent := false
bool can_early = pre_alert_enable and early_ratio > 0 and barstate.isrealtime and not barstate.isconfirmed
bool early_core_long_pulse  = can_early and core_long_cond  and core_long_ready  and f_early_allowed(1)  and not early_core_long_sent
bool early_core_short_pulse = can_early and core_short_cond and core_short_ready and f_early_allowed(-1) and not early_core_short_sent
bool early_diag_c_long_pulse  = can_early and diag_c_long_cond  and diag_c_long_ready  and f_early_allowed(1)  and not early_diag_c_long_sent
bool early_diag_c_short_pulse = can_early and diag_c_short_cond and diag_c_short_ready and f_early_allowed(-1) and not early_diag_c_short_sent
// REALTIME_PRE 전송 바를 표시에 고정해 화면/웹훅 일치를 유지
var int rt_webhook_latch_bar = na
var bool rt_diag_c_long_sent_bar = false
var bool rt_diag_c_short_sent_bar = false
var bool rt_core_long_sent_bar = false
var bool rt_core_short_sent_bar = false
if rt_webhook_latch_bar != bar_index
    rt_webhook_latch_bar := bar_index
    rt_diag_c_long_sent_bar := false
    rt_diag_c_short_sent_bar := false
    rt_core_long_sent_bar := false
    rt_core_short_sent_bar := false
// 3) 외부 진입 신호는 LONG/SHORT 단일화: 발생원은 EARLY 또는 CORE, 수량은 FIXED 비중 사용
// v6.0.3.0 baseline: chart markers and webhook LONG/SHORT must stay anchored to this block unless the SSOT changes.
bool sp_long_hard_block =
     sp_state == "CRITICAL" or
     (sp_transition_risk >= sp_hard_block_transition_min_eff and sp_entropy_score >= 0.70) or
     (sp_coherence_score <= 0.28 and sp_entropy_score >= 0.66) or
     (sp_free_energy >= 0.68 and sp_domain_wall_density >= 0.50)
bool sp_short_hard_block = sp_long_hard_block
bool sp_core_promote_ok =
     sp_state != "CRITICAL" and
     sp_state != "DISORDERED" and
     sp_coherence_score >= sp_core_promote_coherence_min_eff and
     sp_transition_risk <= 0.54 and
     sp_entropy_score <= 0.60 and
     sp_domain_wall_density <= 0.42 and
     sp_free_energy <= 0.52
bool sp_live_allow_ok =
     sp_state != "CRITICAL" and
     sp_coherence_score >= 0.34 and
     sp_transition_risk <= sp_live_allow_transition_max_eff and
     sp_entropy_score <= 0.78 and
     sp_domain_wall_density <= 0.58 and
     sp_susceptibility <= 0.72 and
     not sp_long_hard_block
bool long_alert_from_core = core_long_pulse and sp_core_promote_ok and not sp_long_hard_block
bool short_alert_from_core = core_short_pulse and sp_core_promote_ok and not sp_short_hard_block
bool long_alert_from_early = (early_stage_long_pulse or (core_long_pulse and not long_alert_from_core and sp_live_allow_ok)) and not long_alert_from_core and not sp_long_hard_block
bool short_alert_from_early = (early_stage_short_pulse or (core_short_pulse and not short_alert_from_core and sp_live_allow_ok)) and not short_alert_from_core and not sp_short_hard_block
bool long_alert_pulse  = long_alert_from_core or long_alert_from_early
bool short_alert_pulse = short_alert_from_core or short_alert_from_early
string long_alert_entry_grade = long_alert_from_core ? "CORE" : long_alert_from_early ? "EARLY" : ""
string short_alert_entry_grade = short_alert_from_core ? "CORE" : short_alert_from_early ? "EARLY" : ""
bool long_display_pulse = long_alert_pulse
bool short_display_pulse = short_alert_pulse
// SS는 메인 LONG/SHORT 신호가 있으면 차단
bool ss_long_alert_pulse  = ss_long_pulse  and not early_stage_long_pulse
bool ss_short_alert_pulse = ss_short_pulse and not early_stage_short_pulse
// TD9P는 메인 LONG/SHORT 신호/SS가 있으면 차단
bool td9p_buy_alert_pulse  = td9p_buy_pulse  and not early_stage_long_pulse  and not ss_long_pulse
bool td9p_sell_alert_pulse = td9p_sell_pulse and not early_stage_short_pulse and not ss_short_pulse
// EMO는 메인 LONG/SHORT 신호가 있으면 차단
bool emo_long_alert_pulse  = false
bool emo_short_alert_pulse = false

// 신호 표시 = 실제 알림 신호(confirmed stream)와 동일하게 렌더링
plotshape(tf_show_diag_c_ls and show_diag_c_long and long_display_pulse,
          title = "Webhook Long", location = location.belowbar,
          style = shape.triangleup, color = color.lime,
          text = "L", textcolor = color.white, size = size.tiny)
plotshape(tf_show_diag_c_ls and show_diag_c_short and short_display_pulse,
          title = "Webhook Short", location = location.abovebar,
          style = shape.triangledown, color = color.red,
          text = "S", textcolor = color.white, size = size.tiny)

//────────────────────────────────────
// 42-3. ALERT() 단일 스트림 (코인당 알림 1개용)
// TradingView에서 "Any alert() function call"로 알림 1개만 만들면
// 모든 신호가 이 alert()로 나갑니다.

//────────────────────────────────────
var int tv_pos_dir = 0
var int last_entry_bar = na
var int last_entry_dir = 0
// 42-3A) 포지션/방향 상태 유틸
f_pos_state(int dir) =>
    dir == 0 ? "FLAT" : (dir > 0 ? "LONG" : "SHORT")
f_dir_from_side(string sd) =>
    sd == "BUY" ? 1 : (sd == "SELL" ? -1 : 0)
// 42-3B) 액션 정책(ENTRY/ADD/DROP) 유틸
f_reverse_allowed(int posIn, int sigDir) =>
    // Binance 선물만 "조건부 반전" 적용
    if not is_binance_fut
        true
    else
        bool want_reverse = posIn != 0 and sigDir != 0 and sigDir != posIn
        if not want_reverse
            true
        else
            bool dir_long = sigDir > 0
            bool ltf_ok = dir_long ? ltf_gate_long : ltf_gate_short
            bool htf_ok = dir_long ? (htf_state == "bull") : (htf_state == "bear")
            bool trend_ok = dir_long ? (trend_state == "bull") : (trend_state == "bear")
            bool regime_ok = regime_state == "trend" or regime_state == "transition"
            bool score_ok = dir_long ? (score >= 35) : (score <= -35)
            ltf_ok and htf_ok and trend_ok and regime_ok and score_ok
f_add_allowed(int sigDir) =>
    if not add_gate_enable
        true
    else
        bool dir_long = sigDir > 0
        bool score_ok = dir_long ? (score >= add_score_min) : (score <= -add_score_min)
        bool align_ok = dir_long ? (trend_state != "bear" and htf_state != "bear") : (trend_state != "bull" and htf_state != "bull")
        bool regime_ok = regime_state != "range"
        bool conflict_ok = dir_long ? not conflict_long : not conflict_short
        score_ok and align_ok and regime_ok and conflict_ok
f_action_for_dir(int posIn, int sigDir) =>
    sigDir == 0 ? "DROP" :
     (posIn == 0 ? "ENTRY" :
       (sigDir == posIn ? (f_add_allowed(sigDir) ? "ADD" : "DROP") :
         (f_reverse_allowed(posIn, sigDir) ? "ENTRY" : "DROP")))
f_reason_for_action(int posIn, string action, int sigDir) =>
    string reasonOut = "NO_SIGNAL"
    if action == "ENTRY" and posIn == 0
        reasonOut := "FLAT_ENTRY"
    else if action == "ADD"
        reasonOut := "IN_POSITION_SAME_DIR"
    else if action == "ENTRY" and posIn != 0
        reasonOut := "IN_POSITION_OPPOSITE_DIR"
    else if action == "DROP" and posIn != 0 and sigDir != 0 and sigDir == posIn
        reasonOut := "ADD_BLOCKED"
    else if action == "DROP" and posIn != 0 and sigDir != 0 and sigDir != posIn
        reasonOut := "REVERSE_BLOCKED"
    reasonOut
f_apply_diag_c_alert_policy(string ev, string actionIn, string reasonIn, int sigDir) =>
    [actionIn, reasonIn]
f_safe_qty(float q) =>
    na(q) ? 0.0 : math.max(math.min(q, 1.0), 0.0)
f_json_escape(string src) =>
    // 웹훅 JSON payload 문자열 안전화(따옴표/줄바꿈/백슬래시)
    string txt = str.tostring(src)
    txt := str.replace_all(txt, "\\", "\\\\")
    txt := str.replace_all(txt, "\"", "\\\"")
    txt := str.replace_all(txt, "\n", "\\n")
    txt := str.replace_all(txt, "\r", "\\r")
    txt := str.replace_all(txt, "\t", "\\t")
    txt
f_json_pair_s(string key, string val) =>
    ",\"" + key + "\":\"" + f_json_escape(val) + "\""
f_json_pair_n(string key, float val) =>
    ",\"" + key + "\":" + str.tostring(val)
f_json_pair_i(string key, int val) =>
    ",\"" + key + "\":" + str.tostring(val)
f_json_pair_b(string key, bool val) =>
    ",\"" + key + "\":" + str.tostring(val)
f_apply_cost_shield(string actionIn, string reasonIn) =>
    string actionOut = actionIn
    string reasonOut = reasonIn
    if is_binance_fut and cost_shield_enable and cost_shield_block_add and actionOut == "ADD"
        actionOut := "DROP"
        reasonOut := "COST_SHIELD_ADD_BLOCKED"
    [actionOut, reasonOut]
f_apply_ssot_block(string actionIn, string reasonIn) =>
    string actionOut = actionIn
    string reasonOut = reasonIn
    if ssot_block_enable and (actionOut == "ENTRY" or actionOut == "ADD")
        actionOut := "DROP"
        reasonOut := "SSOT_BLOCK"
    [actionOut, reasonOut]
f_apply_entry_cooldown(string actionIn, string reasonIn, int sigDir, int lastEntryBarIn, int lastEntryDirIn) =>
    string actionOut = actionIn
    string reasonOut = reasonIn
    int barsSinceEntry = na(lastEntryBarIn) ? 1000000 : (bar_index - lastEntryBarIn)
    bool isReverse = lastEntryDirIn != 0 and sigDir != 0 and sigDir != lastEntryDirIn
    if actionOut == "ENTRY"
        if barsSinceEntry < entry_cooldown_bars
            actionOut := "DROP"
            reasonOut := "ENTRY_COOLDOWN"
        else if isReverse and barsSinceEntry < reverse_cooldown_bars
            actionOut := "DROP"
            reasonOut := "REVERSE_COOLDOWN"
    [actionOut, reasonOut]
// 42-3C) 거래소/신뢰도/페이로드 유틸
f_alert_exchange() =>
    "BINANCEFUT"
// 알림 신뢰도(0~1) - score/posterior/EV 혼합
f_signal_conf(int sigDir) =>
    float score_conf = f_zz_clamp01(math.abs(score_panel) / 100.0)
    float post_prob = sigDir == 1 ? zz_post_prob_long : sigDir == -1 ? zz_post_prob_short : 0.5
    float posterior_conf = f_zz_clamp01(math.abs(post_prob - 0.5) * 2.0)
    float ev_val = sigDir == 1 ? zz_ev_diag_c_long : sigDir == -1 ? zz_ev_diag_c_short : 0.0
    float ev_thr = sigDir == 1 ? ev_thr_diag_c_L : sigDir == -1 ? ev_thr_diag_c_S : ev_thr_real
    float ev_conf = f_zz_clamp01((ev_val - ev_thr) / ev_conf_span)
    float entropy_conf = f_zz_clamp01(1.0 - sp_entropy_score)
    float coherence_conf = f_zz_clamp01(sp_coherence_score)
    float transition_conf = f_zz_clamp01(1.0 - sp_transition_risk)
    float conf_local = f_zz_clamp01(
         0.30 * score_conf +
         0.30 * posterior_conf +
         0.15 * ev_conf +
         0.10 * entropy_conf +
         0.10 * coherence_conf +
         0.05 * transition_conf)
    conf_local
f_entry_grade_physics_state(string grade) =>
    string gradeU = str.upper(grade)
    gradeU == "CORE" ? "ORDERED_CORE" : gradeU == "EARLY" ? "MIXED_EARLY" : "BLOCKED"
f_alert_payload(string ev, string sd, float qty, string action, string reason, int sigDir, string emitMode, string entryGrade, string entryQtyProfile) =>
    string ex = f_alert_exchange()
    string sym = syminfo.ticker
    string tf = timeframe.period
    string strategyId = f_strategy_id_effective()
    string strategySource = f_strategy_id_source()
    string posState = f_pos_state(tv_pos_dir)
    float sigConf = f_signal_conf(sigDir)
    bool pineStage1Owned = pine_stage1_bundle_runtime
    string bundleEvent = f_live_entry_bundle_event(ev, sd, entryGrade)
    bool pineStage1StagePass = pineStage1Owned and f_stage1_bundle_pass_for_event(bundleEvent, sd, pine_stage1_bundle_mask)
    string febtPhaseSelected = f_febt_pick_string(sigDir, febt_phase_long, febt_phase_short)
    float febtLockSelected = f_febt_pick_float(sigDir, febt_lock_score_long, febt_lock_score_short)
    float febtDelaySelected = f_febt_pick_float(sigDir, febt_delay_cost_long, febt_delay_cost_short)
    float febtLateSelected = f_febt_pick_float(sigDir, febt_late_risk_long, febt_late_risk_short)
    float febtFailureSelected = f_febt_pick_float(sigDir, febt_failure_risk_long, febt_failure_risk_short)
    float febtEdgeSelected = f_febt_pick_float(sigDir, febt_edge_long, febt_edge_short)
    int febtSameDirStreakSelected = f_febt_pick_int(sigDir, febt_same_dir_streak_long, febt_same_dir_streak_short)
    float febtBreakRetentionSelected = f_febt_pick_float(sigDir, febt_break_retention_long, febt_break_retention_short)
    float febtCloseControlSelected = f_febt_pick_float(sigDir, febt_close_control_long, febt_close_control_short)
    float febtImpulseDecaySelected = f_febt_pick_float(sigDir, febt_impulse_decay_long, febt_impulse_decay_short)
    float febtCounterRejectionSelected = f_febt_pick_float(sigDir, febt_counter_rejection_long, febt_counter_rejection_short)
    float febtMicroAbsorptionSelected = f_febt_pick_float(sigDir, febt_micro_absorption_long, febt_micro_absorption_short)
    bool febtCalcOkSelected = sigDir != 0 and febt_calc_ok
    bool febtStateValidSelected = sigDir != 0 and febt_state_valid
    string febtCalcReasonSelected = sigDir == 0 ? "SIDE_UNKNOWN" : febt_calc_reason
    string febtTimingActionSelected = f_febt_timing_action(febt_mode_runtime, febtPhaseSelected, febtCalcOkSelected)
    int barCloseMs = int(time_close)
    string signalKey = sym + "|" + tf + "|" + ev + "|" + str.tostring(barCloseMs) + "|" + str.tostring(sigDir)
    string signalId = signalKey + "|" + action + "|" + emitMode
    string trace_chain_key = sym + "|" + tf + "|" + str.tostring(barCloseMs) + "|" + emitMode
    string payload = "{\"schema_version\":\"DBJ_WEBHOOK_V2\",\"schema_family\":\"DONBEOLJA_BINANCE\""
    payload += f_json_pair_s("exchange", ex) + f_json_pair_s("symbol", sym) + f_json_pair_s("tf", tf) + f_json_pair_s("strategy_id", strategyId)
    payload += f_json_pair_n("price", close) + f_json_pair_n("confidence", sigConf) + ",\"account_balance\":null"
    payload += f_json_pair_i("bar_close_time_utc_ms", barCloseMs) + ",\"bar_index\":" + str.tostring(bar_index)
    payload += ",\"is_bar_close_confirmed\":" + str.tostring(barstate.isconfirmed) + f_json_pair_s("event", ev) + f_json_pair_s("side", sd)
    payload += f_json_pair_n("qty_pct", qty) + ",\"signal_key\":\"" + f_json_escape(signalKey) + "\"" + f_json_pair_s("signal_id", signalId)
    payload += ",\"trade_id\":\"\"" + ",\"tg_msg_id\":\"\""
    payload += f_json_pair_s("action", action) + f_json_pair_s("reason", reason)
    payload += f_json_pair_i("ssot_ref_utc_ms", barCloseMs) + f_json_pair_b("ssot_confirmed", barstate.isconfirmed) + f_json_pair_b("ssot_block", ssot_block_enable)
    payload += ",\"trace_payload_version\":\"TPTR_V2\""
    payload += f_json_pair_s("trace_chain_key", trace_chain_key) + f_json_pair_s("trace_emit_mode", emitMode)
    payload += f_json_pair_s("strategy_id_source", strategySource)
    payload += f_json_pair_b("cost_shield_enable", cost_shield_enable)
    payload += f_json_pair_n("cost_shield_entry_mult", cost_shield_entry_mult)
    payload += f_json_pair_b("qty_sanitized", true)
    if febt_payload_enable
        payload += f_json_pair_s("febt_mode", febt_mode_runtime)
        payload += f_json_pair_s("febt_phase", febtPhaseSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_lock_score", febtLockSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_delay_cost", febtDelaySelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_late_risk", febtLateSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_failure_risk", febtFailureSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_edge", febtEdgeSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_b("febt_state_valid", febtStateValidSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_b("febt_calc_ok", febtCalcOkSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_s("febt_calc_reason", febtCalcReasonSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_s("febt_timing_action", febtTimingActionSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_s("febt_authority", febt_authority_runtime)
    // [PATCH-41] 동적 Exit Policy
    float _atr_pct = atr_val / math.max(close, 1.0) * 100.0
    float _exit_tp1 = 3.0
    float _exit_trail = 1.0
    string _exit_source = "BINANCE_DEFAULT"
    if exit_dynamic_enable
        if regime_state == "trend"
            _exit_tp1 := math.min(math.max(_atr_pct * exit_tp1_atr_mult_trend, exit_tp1_floor), 8.0)
            _exit_trail := math.min(math.max(_atr_pct * exit_trail_atr_mult_trend, exit_trail_floor), 3.0)
        else if regime_state == "range"
            _exit_tp1 := math.min(math.max(_atr_pct * 2.0, exit_tp1_floor), 5.0)
            _exit_trail := math.min(math.max(_atr_pct * 1.2, exit_trail_floor), 3.0)
        else
            _exit_tp1 := math.min(math.max(_atr_pct * 2.8, exit_tp1_floor), 6.0)
            _exit_trail := math.min(math.max(_atr_pct * 1.0, exit_trail_floor), 3.0)
        _exit_source := "ATR_DYNAMIC"
    payload += ",\"exit_policy_tp1_pct\":" + str.tostring(_exit_tp1)
    payload += ",\"exit_policy_trail_pct\":" + str.tostring(_exit_trail)
    payload += ",\"exit_policy_source\":\"" + _exit_source + "\""
    payload += ",\"features\":{"
    payload += "\"signal_key\":\"" + f_json_escape(signalKey) + "\""
    payload += f_json_pair_s("signal_id", signalId) + ",\"trade_id\":\"\"" + ",\"tg_msg_id\":\"\""
    payload += f_json_pair_i("ssot_ref_utc_ms", barCloseMs) + f_json_pair_b("ssot_confirmed", barstate.isconfirmed) + f_json_pair_b("ssot_block", ssot_block_enable)
    payload += f_json_pair_s("strategy_id", strategyId) + f_json_pair_s("strategy_id_source", strategySource)
    payload += f_json_pair_s("event", ev) + f_json_pair_s("side", sd) + f_json_pair_n("qty_pct", qty)
    payload += f_json_pair_s("entry_grade", (ev == "LONG" or ev == "SHORT") ? entryGrade : "")
    payload += f_json_pair_s("entry_qty_profile", (ev == "LONG" or ev == "SHORT") ? "FIXED" : "")
    payload += f_json_pair_s("entry_grade_physics", (ev == "LONG" or ev == "SHORT") ? f_entry_grade_physics_state(entryGrade) : "")
    payload += f_json_pair_i("pos_dir", tv_pos_dir) + f_json_pair_s("pos_state", posState) + f_json_pair_s("action", action)
    payload += ",\"trace_payload_version\":\"TPTR_V2\"" + f_json_pair_s("trace_emit_mode", emitMode) + f_json_pair_s("trace_chain_key", trace_chain_key)
    payload += f_json_pair_b("cost_shield_enable", cost_shield_enable) + f_json_pair_n("cost_shield_entry_mult", cost_shield_entry_mult)
    payload += f_json_pair_b("cost_shield_block_add", cost_shield_block_add) + f_json_pair_n("cost_shield_gap_mult", cost_shield_gap_mult)
    payload += f_json_pair_b("qty_sanitized", true)
    payload += f_json_pair_s("pine_stage1_bundle_owner", pineStage1Owned ? "PINE" : "SERVER")
    payload += f_json_pair_s("pine_stage1_bundle_version", "REGIME_SCORE_CONF_POSTERIOR_WAVE_EV_V2")
    payload += f_json_pair_b("pine_stage1_bundle_enabled", pine_stage1_bundle_enable)
    payload += f_json_pair_b("pine_stage1_bundle_owned", pineStage1Owned)
    payload += f_json_pair_b("pine_stage1_bundle_stage_pass", pineStage1StagePass)
    payload += f_json_pair_b("pine_stage1_bundle_apply_early", pine_stage1_bundle_apply_early)
    payload += f_json_pair_b("pine_stage1_bundle_quality_filter_runtime", quality_filter_runtime)
    payload += f_json_pair_n("pine_stage1_bundle_mask", pine_stage1_bundle_mask)
    payload += f_json_pair_n("pine_stage1_bundle_confidence", sigConf)
    payload += f_json_pair_s("pro_score_line", score_line) + f_json_pair_s("pro_env_txt", env_txt)
    payload += f_json_pair_s("pro_vol_td_txt", vol_td_txt) + f_json_pair_s("pro_vol_td_txt_full", vol_td_txt_alert)
    payload += f_json_pair_s("pro_regime_state", regime_state) + f_json_pair_s("pro_trend_state", trend_state) + f_json_pair_s("pro_htf_state", htf_state)
    payload += f_json_pair_s("pro_asset_txt", asset_type_txt) + f_json_pair_s("pro_market_txt", market_txt_alert)
    payload += f_json_pair_b("pro_conflict", conflict) + f_json_pair_b("pro_conflict_long", conflict_long) + f_json_pair_b("pro_conflict_short", conflict_short)
    payload += f_json_pair_n("sp_entropy_score", sp_entropy_score) + f_json_pair_n("sp_coherence_score", sp_coherence_score) + f_json_pair_n("sp_transition_risk", sp_transition_risk) + f_json_pair_n("sp_field_alignment", sp_field_alignment) + f_json_pair_n("sp_domain_wall_density", sp_domain_wall_density) + f_json_pair_n("sp_susceptibility", sp_susceptibility) + f_json_pair_n("sp_free_energy", sp_free_energy) + f_json_pair_s("sp_state", sp_state)
    payload += f_json_pair_b("sp_core_promote_ok", sp_core_promote_ok) + f_json_pair_b("sp_live_allow_ok", sp_live_allow_ok) + f_json_pair_b("sp_hard_block", sp_long_hard_block)
    payload += f_json_pair_n("score", nz(score, 0.0)) + f_json_pair_n("score_panel", score_panel)
    payload += f_json_pair_n("zz_post_prob_long", zz_post_prob_long) + f_json_pair_n("zz_post_prob_short", zz_post_prob_short)
    payload += f_json_pair_n("zz_wave_conf", nz(zz_wave_conf, 0.5))
    payload += f_json_pair_n("zz_ev_core_long", zz_ev_core_long) + f_json_pair_n("zz_ev_core_short", zz_ev_core_short)
    if febt_payload_enable
        payload += f_json_pair_s("febt_mode", febt_mode_runtime)
        payload += f_json_pair_s("febt_phase", febtPhaseSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_lock_score", febtLockSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_delay_cost", febtDelaySelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_late_risk", febtLateSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_failure_risk", febtFailureSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_edge", febtEdgeSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_b("febt_state_valid", febtStateValidSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_b("febt_calc_ok", febtCalcOkSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_s("febt_calc_reason", febtCalcReasonSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_s("febt_timing_action", febtTimingActionSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_s("febt_authority", febt_authority_runtime)
        payload += f_json_pair_i("febt_same_dir_streak", febtSameDirStreakSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_recent_move_1_pct", febt_recent_move_1_pct)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_recent_move_2_pct", febt_recent_move_2_pct)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_break_retention", febtBreakRetentionSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_close_control", febtCloseControlSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_impulse_decay", febtImpulseDecaySelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_counter_rejection", febtCounterRejectionSelected)
        payload += f_json_pair_n("febt_micro_absorption", febtMicroAbsorptionSelected)
    payload += f_json_pair_n("zz_ev_diag_c_long", zz_ev_diag_c_long) + f_json_pair_n("zz_ev_diag_c_short", zz_ev_diag_c_short) + "}}"
    payload
// 42-3D) 실시간(선발) / 확정(잔여) 전송 유틸
f_emit_realtime_pre(int posIn, int lastEntryBarIn, int lastEntryDirIn, bool pulse, string ev, string sd, float qtyEff, string preReason, int sigDir, bool sentPrev, string entryGrade, string entryQtyProfile) =>
    bool sentOut = sentPrev
    int posOut = posIn
    int lastEntryBarOut = lastEntryBarIn
    int lastEntryDirOut = lastEntryDirIn
    if pulse
        string action = f_action_for_dir(posOut, sigDir)
        string reasonRt = preReason
        [actionPolRt, reasonPolRt] = f_apply_diag_c_alert_policy(ev, action, reasonRt, sigDir)
        action := actionPolRt
        reasonRt := reasonPolRt
        [actionCsRt, reasonCsRt] = f_apply_cost_shield(action, reasonRt)
        action := actionCsRt
        reasonRt := reasonCsRt
        [actionCdRt, reasonCdRt] = f_apply_entry_cooldown(action, reasonRt, sigDir, lastEntryBarOut, lastEntryDirOut)
        action := actionCdRt
        reasonRt := reasonCdRt
        [actionSbRt, reasonSbRt] = f_apply_ssot_block(action, reasonRt)
        action := actionSbRt
        reasonRt := reasonSbRt
        if action != "DROP"
            float qtyScaled = qtyEff * early_ratio
            if (action == "ENTRY" or action == "ADD") and is_binance_fut and cost_shield_enable
                qtyScaled := qtyScaled * cost_shield_entry_mult
            float qty = f_safe_qty(qtyScaled)
            if qty > 0
                alert(f_alert_payload(ev, sd, qty, action, reasonRt, sigDir, "REALTIME_PRE", entryGrade, entryQtyProfile), alert.freq_once_per_bar)
                if action == "ENTRY" or action == "ADD"
                    posOut := sigDir
                if action == "ENTRY"
                    lastEntryBarOut := bar_index
                    lastEntryDirOut := sigDir
                sentOut := true
    [sentOut, posOut, lastEntryBarOut, lastEntryDirOut]
f_emit_confirm(int posIn, int lastEntryBarIn, int lastEntryDirIn, bool pulse, string ev, string sd, float qtyEff, bool preSent, string confirmReason, int sigDir, string entryGrade, string entryQtyProfile) =>
    int posOut = posIn
    int lastEntryBarOut = lastEntryBarIn
    int lastEntryDirOut = lastEntryDirIn
    if pulse
        string action = f_action_for_dir(posOut, sigDir)
        string reason = f_reason_for_action(posOut, action, sigDir)
        [actionPolCf, reasonPolCf] = f_apply_diag_c_alert_policy(ev, action, reason, sigDir)
        action := actionPolCf
        reason := reasonPolCf
        [actionCsCf, reasonCsCf] = f_apply_cost_shield(action, reason)
        action := actionCsCf
        reason := reasonCsCf
        [actionCdCf, reasonCdCf] = f_apply_entry_cooldown(action, reason, sigDir, lastEntryBarOut, lastEntryDirOut)
        action := actionCdCf
        reason := reasonCdCf
        [actionSbCf, reasonSbCf] = f_apply_ssot_block(action, reason)
        action := actionSbCf
        reason := reasonSbCf
        float qtyBase = qtyEff
        if (action == "ENTRY" or action == "ADD") and is_binance_fut and cost_shield_enable
            qtyBase := qtyBase * cost_shield_entry_mult
        float qty = f_safe_qty(qtyBase)
        if qty > 0
            if preSent and action == "ADD" and confirmReason != ""
                reason := confirmReason
            alert(f_alert_payload(ev, sd, qty, action, reason, sigDir, "BAR_CLOSE", entryGrade, entryQtyProfile), alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
        if action == "ENTRY"
            posOut := sigDir
            lastEntryBarOut := bar_index
            lastEntryDirOut := sigDir
    [posOut, lastEntryBarOut, lastEntryDirOut]
// 42-3E) 단일 스트림 실행(실시간+확정 통합)
f_process_alert_stream(
    int posIn,
    int lastEntryBarIn,
    int lastEntryDirIn,
    bool erLongIn, bool erShortIn,
    bool ecLongIn, bool ecShortIn) =>
    int pos = posIn
    int lastEntryBar = lastEntryBarIn
    int lastEntryDir = lastEntryDirIn
    bool erLong = erLongIn
    bool erShort = erShortIn
    bool ecLong = ecLongIn
    bool ecShort = ecShortIn
    if barstate.isconfirmed
        [posCf1, lastBarCf1, lastDirCf1] = f_emit_confirm(pos, lastEntryBar, lastEntryDir, long_alert_pulse, "LONG", "BUY", f_alert_qty_long_short_eff(), false, "", 1, long_alert_entry_grade, "FIXED")
        pos := posCf1
        lastEntryBar := lastBarCf1
        lastEntryDir := lastDirCf1
        [posCf2, lastBarCf2, lastDirCf2] = f_emit_confirm(pos, lastEntryBar, lastEntryDir, short_alert_pulse, "SHORT", "SELL", f_alert_qty_long_short_eff(), false, "", -1, short_alert_entry_grade, "FIXED")
        pos := posCf2
        lastEntryBar := lastBarCf2
        lastEntryDir := lastDirCf2
        [posCf9, lastBarCf9, lastDirCf9] = f_emit_confirm(pos, lastEntryBar, lastEntryDir, ss_long_alert_pulse, "SS_LONG", "BUY", alert_qty_ss, false, "", 1, "", "")
        pos := posCf9
        lastEntryBar := lastBarCf9
        lastEntryDir := lastDirCf9
        [posCf10, lastBarCf10, lastDirCf10] = f_emit_confirm(pos, lastEntryBar, lastEntryDir, ss_short_alert_pulse, "SS_SHORT", "SELL", alert_qty_ss, false, "", -1, "", "")
        pos := posCf10
        lastEntryBar := lastBarCf10
        lastEntryDir := lastDirCf10
        [posCf11, lastBarCf11, lastDirCf11] = f_emit_confirm(pos, lastEntryBar, lastEntryDir, td9p_buy_alert_pulse, "TD9P_BUY", "BUY", alert_qty_td9p, false, "", 1, "", "")
        pos := posCf11
        lastEntryBar := lastBarCf11
        lastEntryDir := lastDirCf11
        [posCf12, lastBarCf12, lastDirCf12] = f_emit_confirm(pos, lastEntryBar, lastEntryDir, td9p_sell_alert_pulse, "TD9P_SELL", "SELL", alert_qty_td9p, false, "", -1, "", "")
        pos := posCf12
        lastEntryBar := lastBarCf12
        lastEntryDir := lastDirCf12
        [posCf13, lastBarCf13, lastDirCf13] = f_emit_confirm(pos, lastEntryBar, lastEntryDir, emo_long_alert_pulse, "EMO_LONG", "BUY", alert_qty_emo, false, "", 1, "", "")
        pos := posCf13
        lastEntryBar := lastBarCf13
        lastEntryDir := lastDirCf13
        [posCf14, lastBarCf14, lastDirCf14] = f_emit_confirm(pos, lastEntryBar, lastEntryDir, emo_short_alert_pulse, "EMO_SHORT", "SELL", alert_qty_emo, false, "", -1, "", "")
        pos := posCf14
        lastEntryBar := lastBarCf14
        lastEntryDir := lastDirCf14
        [posCf15, lastBarCf15, lastDirCf15] = f_emit_confirm(pos, lastEntryBar, lastEntryDir, false, "ICHI_BELL_LONG", "BUY", alert_qty_ichi, false, "", 1, "", "")
        pos := posCf15
        lastEntryBar := lastBarCf15
        lastEntryDir := lastDirCf15
        [posCf16, lastBarCf16, lastDirCf16] = f_emit_confirm(pos, lastEntryBar, lastEntryDir, false, "ICHI_BELL_SHORT", "SELL", alert_qty_ichi, false, "", -1, "", "")
        pos := posCf16
        lastEntryBar := lastBarCf16
        lastEntryDir := lastDirCf16
        if enter_all_exit
            alert(f_alert_payload("EXIT_ALL", "SELL", 1.0, "EXIT", "EXIT_ALL", f_dir_from_side("SELL"), "BAR_CLOSE", "", ""), alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
            pos := 0
    [pos, lastEntryBar, lastEntryDir, erLong, erShort, ecLong, ecShort]
[posOut, lastEntryBarOut, lastEntryDirOut, erLongOut, erShortOut, ecLongOut, ecShortOut] = f_process_alert_stream(
    tv_pos_dir,
    last_entry_bar,
    last_entry_dir,
    early_diag_c_long_sent, early_diag_c_short_sent,
    early_core_long_sent, early_core_short_sent
)
rt_diag_c_long_sent_bar := erLongOut and not early_diag_c_long_sent
rt_diag_c_short_sent_bar := erShortOut and not early_diag_c_short_sent
rt_core_long_sent_bar := ecLongOut and not early_core_long_sent
rt_core_short_sent_bar := ecShortOut and not early_core_short_sent
tv_pos_dir := posOut
last_entry_bar := lastEntryBarOut
last_entry_dir := lastEntryDirOut
early_diag_c_long_sent := erLongOut
early_diag_c_short_sent := erShortOut
early_core_long_sent := ecLongOut
early_core_short_sent := ecShortOut

//────────────────────────────────────
// SL/TP 라인 플롯 (표시 전용)
//────────────────────────────────────
plot(sltp_enable and sltp_dir != 0 ? sltp_entry : na, "SLTP Entry", color = color.white, linewidth = 1, style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(sltp_enable and sltp_dir != 0 ? sltp_sl    : na, "SLTP SL",    color = color.red,   linewidth = 2, style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(sltp_enable and sltp_dir != 0 ? sltp_tp    : na, "SLTP TP",    color = color.lime,  linewidth = 2, style = plot.style_linebr)
